Conclusion: 采集暴露（target exposure）在两种骨干网络下都能提升被测性能，但其对模态排序的影响取决于网络架构。研究支持将"采集感知的同测试集评估"作为多模态UAV基准中传统图像级划分的必要补充，以更准确地反映模型在全新采集场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: UAV image collections contain spatially and temporally related frames, yet semantic-segmentation benchmarks commonly split them at image level. Such splitting can place samples from one acquisition in both model development and testing, obscuring transfer to a genuinely new survey. Using the 734-sample WeedyRice-RGBMS-DB, we fix a 124-image target-acquisition test set and compare two protocols with identical train, validation, and test counts: target-held-out, which excludes the target acquisition from development, and target-exposed, which admits its remaining images. SegFormer-B0 is evaluated with RGB, four-band multispectral (MS), and seven-channel RGB+MS input over two fixed-split seeds. RGB is strongest under complete acquisition holdout ($0.7317\pm0.0201$ IoU), whereas RGB+MS becomes strongest after target exposure ($0.7822\pm0.0269$). A fixed-split U-Net/ResNet18 replication confirms positive exposure gains for all three inputs, but retains RGB as the best modality under both protocols. Acquisition exposure therefore increases measured performance across both evaluated backbones, while its effect on modality ranking is architecture-dependent. A supplied-split audit reveals strong near-sequential dependence, and corruption tests show that early fusion is substantially more sensitive to RGB--MS displacement than to moderate radiometric scaling. These results support acquisition-aware same-test evaluation as a necessary complement to ordinary image-level splitting in multimodal UAV benchmarks. The code and supporting the findings of this study will be publicly released upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [35] [MolParser-Mobile: Ultrafast OCSR System for Large-Scale Chemical Literature Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05807)
*Xi Fang,Haocheng Lu,Han Lyu,Chengxiang Luo,Linfeng Zhang,Guolin Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MolParser-Mobile，一个通过AutoML优化的轻量级端到端光学化学结构识别（OCSR）框架。该模型仅含9.98M参数，在单个NVIDIA RTX 4090D GPU上可达到每秒1,520个分子的吞吐量，同时保持有竞争力的识别精度，在多个基准上甚至表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCSR是化学文献挖掘的基础组件，支持分子数据库构建、反应提取和AI驱动的科学发现。尽管基于深度学习的方法在识别精度上取得显著进展，但推理吞吐量成为网络级部署的关键瓶颈，现有方法难以满足大规模实际应用需求。

Method: 提出MolParser-Mobile框架，采用AutoML优化策略设计轻量级端到端OCSR架构，通过自动化搜索手段获得紧凑且高效的模型结构，兼顾识别精度与推理效率。

Result: MolParser-Mobile仅包含9.98M参数，在单个NVIDIA RTX 4090D GPU上达到每秒1,520个分子的高吞吐量。在多个基准上保持有竞争力的识别精度，其中若干基准上表现优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 证明通过AutoML可以在不牺牲（甚至提升）识别精度的前提下大幅压缩OCSR模型规模并提升推理吞吐量，为OCSR技术的网络级大规模部署提供了可行的轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Optical Chemical Structure Recognition (OCSR) is a fundamental component of chemical literature mining, enabling molecular database construction, reaction extraction, and AI-driven scientific discovery. Despite substantial progress in recognition accuracy with recent deep learning-based methods, inference throughput remains a critical bottleneck that limits web-scale deployment. To address this challenge, we propose MolParser-Mobile, an AutoML-optimized lightweight end-to-end OCSR framework. MolParser-Mobile contains only 9.98M parameters, while reaching a throughput of 1,520 molecules per second on a single NVIDIA RTX 4090D GPU. Despite its compact design, it maintains competitive and, on several benchmarks, superior recognition accuracy.

</details>


### [36] [CoRe-SAM3: Conditional Semantic--Visual Reconciliation for SAM3 Crack Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05816)
*Shipeng Liu,Liang Zhao,Dengfeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对SAM3在裂缝分割中的不足，通过诊断其内部语义表征与视觉表征的功能差异，提出条件语义-视觉调和（CoRe）方法。以语义预测为主决策路径，用轻量语义校准调整域决策映射，并利用空间对齐的原生视觉证据生成有界、零初始化的条件残差来选择性修正预测。在五个裂缝数据集上，Crack IoU从62.34%提升至70.47%，clDice从81.98%提升至89.24%，仅需18.914K可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM3虽具备强大的开放概念分割能力，但直接应用于裂缝域会出现弱裂缝漏检、裂缝样背景误激活和局部边界错误。作者希望基于对模型内部表征功能差异的深入理解，提出一种参数高效的域适配策略。

Method: 首先在五个裂缝数据集上诊断SAM3内部提示条件化语义表征与原生视觉表征的功能差异，发现语义表征已包含大部分任务信息而视觉表征的效用依赖于当前语义状态。基于此提出CoRe：保留语义预测作为主决策路径，用轻量语义校准调整目标域决策映射，并以空间对齐的原生视觉证据生成零初始化、有界且正则化的条件残差，选择性修正既有预测。

Result: 在五个裂缝域上，CoRe-SAM3将平均Crack IoU从62.34%提升至70.47%，clDice从81.98%提升至89.24%，仅引入18.914K可训练参数。预测迁移分析显示CoRe平均纠正34.38%的原生错误，对SAM3正确分类像素的损伤率仅为0.23%。

Conclusion: 基于内部表征间功能差异的受约束预测修正是针对具有强任务特定语义先验的视觉基础模型的一种高效、参数节约的目标域适配策略。

Abstract: Crack segmentation requires a model to recognize target semantics while accurately recovering thin, low-contrast, and topologically continuous local structures. Although SAM3 provides strong open-concept segmentation, its direct application to the crack domain still misses weak cracks, activates crack-like background regions, and produces local boundary errors. We first diagnose the functional differences between the internal prompt-conditioned semantic representation and native visual representation of SAM3 on five crack datasets. The results show that the semantic representation already carries most task information for crack prediction, whereas the utility of the visual representation depends on the current semantic state. Directly combining the two representations does not yield consistent gains. Based on this finding, we propose Conditional Semantic--Visual Reconciliation, termed CoRe. CoRe retains semantic prediction as the primary decision path, applies lightweight semantic calibration to adjust the target-domain decision mapping, and uses spatially aligned native visual evidence to generate a zero-initialized, bounded, and regularized conditional residual that selectively corrects existing predictions. Across five domains, CoRe-SAM3 improves the average Crack IoU from 62.34% to 70.47% and clDice from 81.98% to 89.24%, while introducing only 18.914 K trainable parameters. Prediction-transition analysis further shows that CoRe corrects an average of 34.38% of native errors, with a damage rate of only 0.23% on pixels correctly classified by native SAM3. These results demonstrate that constrained prediction correction based on the functional differences between internal representations provides an effective and parameter-efficient target-domain adaptation strategy for vision foundation models with strong task-specific semantic priors.

</details>


### [37] [Map the Possibilities: Spatial Belief Fields for Language-Goal Aerial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05841)
*Haotian Xu,Yue Hu,Zhengqiu Zhu,Yong Zhao,Wenguan Wang,Quanjun Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SBFNav提出了一种基于语言条件空间信念场（SBF）的闭环导航框架，通过维护目标位置的概率分布而非单一预测点，在部分观测条件下保留多种空间假设，并在CityNav基准上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言目标导航方法往往将语言理解过早地压缩为单一航点或动作，无法充分应对不完全证据和模糊关系所带来的空间不确定性。

Method: 提出SBFNav框架，核心是语言条件化空间信念场（SBF），它表示任务条件下合理目标位置的概率分布，随着新证据的积累逐帧更新。每次决策时，框架从该分布中选择与指令和观测最匹配的目标作为度量航点用于控制。

Result: 在CityNav原始及修订版基准上取得最佳总体性能。在Test Unseen分割上，SR从25.91%提升至32.29%，SPL从19.63%提升至30.43%。消融实验验证了空间信念建模优于单点预测。

Conclusion: 空间信念场表示比单一航点/动作预测更能有效处理语言引导导航中的不确定性，显著提升了大规模连续环境中语言导航的定位精度和路径效率。

Abstract: Language-goal aerial navigation requires an agent to local- ize a potentially unobserved target from relational instruc- tions and partial observations, and translate this inference into metric actions in large-scale continuous environments. Existing methods often reduce language grounding to one single waypoint or action, prematurely collapsing the spatial uncertainty inherent in incomplete evidence and ambiguous relations. To address this limitation, we introduce SBFNav, a closed-loop navigation framework centered on a language- conditioned Spatial Belief Field (SBF). Unlike ego-centric maps that primarily record what has been observed, SBF rep- resents a task-conditioned distribution over plausible target locations, preserving multiple spatial hypotheses under par- tial evidence. At each step, this distribution is updated from accumulated observations as new evidence becomes avail- able. Built on this representation, SBFNav selects the goal that best aligns with the instruction and observations as a met- ric waypoint for control. Experiments on both the original and revised CityNav benchmarks achieve the best reported overall performance. On the Test Unseen split, our method improves SR from 25.91% to 32.29% and SPL from 19.63% to 30.43%. Ablation studies further confirm the advantages of spatial-belief modeling over single-point prediction.

</details>


### [38] [Hierarchical Prompt Injector for Domain Generalization Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05864)
*Xin Kun Lin,Ruoyu Guo,Jiaqi Guo,Maurice Pagnucco,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出空间层次提示（SHP）和层次提示注入器（HPI）用于域泛化语义分割任务。SHP为每个类别提供多视角的区域级几何锚点，HPI实现空间自适应的提示注入，在合成到真实和真实到真实基准上分别达到70.62%和72.74%的mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域泛化语义分割中，视觉模型常依赖跨域不稳定的低级外观线索，而结构属性具有跨域稳定性。现有基于提示学习的方法将每个类别编码为单一整体提示，且对所有像素统一应用，无法适应部分遮挡、视角变化等导致仅部分目标区域可见的情况。

Method: 提出SHP（空间层次提示），为每个类别补充从不同视角提取的区域级几何锚点以覆盖任意视角下的结构外观；提出HPI（层次提示注入器），通过建模提示与视觉特征的语义相关性和空间影响来实现空间自适应的提示注入，并引入辅助监督来对齐层次提示与目标区域。

Result: 在合成到真实基准上达到70.62% mIoU，在真实到真实基准上达到72.74% mIoU。

Conclusion: 空间层次提示与自适应注入机制有效提升了跨域语义分割的性能，验证了利用区域级结构先验进行域泛化的有效性。

Abstract: Domain Generalized Semantic Segmentation (DGSS) is a challenging task, as vision models often rely on low-level appearance cues that change across domains. In contrast, structural attributes exhibit cross-domain stability, motivating the use of structural priors for DGSS. Existing methods use prompt learning to transfer such priors into DGSS models, but typically encode each class as a single holistic prompt. Moreover, these methods apply prompts uniformly to all pixels, offering no mechanism to adapt when only a subset of object regions is visible due to viewpoint changes, occlusion, and environmental variation. We address this with \textbf{Spatial Hierarchical Prompts (SHP)} that enrich each class with region-level geometric anchors capturing structural appearance from distinct viewing angles, ensuring complementary coverage under arbitrary viewpoints. Additionally, we propose the \textbf{Hierarchical Prompt Injector (HPI)}, which enables spatially adaptive prompt injection in foundation models. HPI spatially grounds prompts by modeling their semantic relevance and spatial influence with visual features. Considering the difficulty of learning spatially and semantically aware prompt injection, we further introduce auxiliary supervision to align hierarchical prompts with their corresponding object regions. We achieve 70.62\% and 72.74\% mIoU on synthetic-to-real and real-to-real benchmarks, respectively. Code and checkpoints are released at https://github.com/MosukFate/HPI

</details>


### [39] [FACT: A Forensic Agent with Compiled Tool-Use Trajectories for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05876)
*Jiaoyang Chen,Bin Hu,Jingyu Hu,Kun Zhou,Qin Zhang,Zhengzhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FACT（法证智能体与编译工具使用轨迹）框架，用于开放世界AI生成图像检测。与依赖固定取证线索的传统检测器不同，FACT通过学习图像条件下的工具调用策略，自适应决定调用哪些取证工具、解读返回证据并在充足时停止。通过Evolve-Distill-Refine流水线（进化验证取证技能→编译为工具轨迹→蒸馏为紧凑智能体→用成本感知GRPO优化），在两个内部基准和四个公开基准上均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成图像检测正面临开放世界挑战：新型图像生成器不断涌现，生成图像极其逼真使视觉伪影更难识别。传统检测器依赖固定取证线索，导致对一个生成器家族有效的检测器在另一个生成器上失效。

Method: 提出FACT框架，核心是学习图像条件化的工具使用策略，不应用固定检测器，而是自主决定调用哪些取证工具并解读证据。采用Evolve-Distill-Refine流水线：（1）进化出执行验证的取证技能；（2）将技能编译为动作-观察工具使用轨迹；（3）将轨迹蒸馏为紧凑智能体；（4）用成本感知的GRPO算法细化策略。

Result: 在两个内部基准和四个公开基准上，FACT在所有对比方法中取得最佳性能，包括对最近未见过的生成器、深度伪造图像和篡改图像也表现优异。

Conclusion: FACT通过动态调用取证工具而非依赖固定检测器的方式，有效解决了AI生成图像检测的开放世界泛化问题，在包括新型生成器在内的多种场景下均超越现有方法，为取证检测提供了更具适应性的新范式。

Abstract: AI-generated image detection is increasingly open-world: new image generators produce highly realistic images that make visual artifacts harder to identify. Existing detectors usually rely on a fixed set of forensic cues, so a detector that works well for one generator family may fail on another. We introduce FACT (Forensic Agent with Compiled Tool-use Trajectories), which learns an image-conditioned tool-use policy for forensic analysis. Instead of applying a fixed detector, FACT decides which forensic tools to call, interprets the returned evidence, and stops when sufficient evidence has been collected. FACT follows an Evolve--Distill--Refine pipeline: it evolves an execution-verified forensic skill, compiles the skill into action--observation tool-use trajectories, distills them into a compact agent, and refines the policy with cost-aware GRPO. Across two internal and four public benchmarks, FACT achieves the best performance among all compared methods, including on recent unseen generators, deepfakes, and manipulated images.

</details>


### [40] [UniFusion: Sparse-View 4D Reconstruction via Unified Spatio-temporal Depth Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05888)
*Yongzhe Lyu,Shaofei Wang,Yixin Chen,Siyuan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的时空深度对齐框架，用于解决稀疏视角视频中的4D重建问题。该方法将跨视角和跨时间的深度图表示为时空神经场，联合解决不一致性问题，无需前景分割或外部追踪模型，并通过深度顺序损失和尺度平移不变损失提升深度质量，最终引导动态高斯溅射模型进行4D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角视频的4D重建通常依赖单目深度估计提供先验，但有限的跨视角重叠和时序变化导致深度预测在视角和时间维度上不一致。现有方法分阶段对齐空间与时间维度，需要前景分割掩膜，且未能利用时间线索进行跨视角对齐。

Method: 提出一个统一的空间-时间深度对齐框架：(1) 将跨视角和跨时间的深度图表示为时空神经场，隐式捕捉时空相关性，无需外部分割/追踪模型；(2) 提出多视角深度顺序损失；(3) 结合经典的尺度和平移不变损失以进一步提升深度质量；(4) 对齐后的深度用于初始化和监督高斯溅射模型进行4D重建。

Result: 在Ego-Exo4D和EgoHuman数据集上的实验表明，所提的深度对齐方法能显著提升动态高斯溅射重建方法在新颖时间/视角合成以及几何精度和一致性方面的性能。

Conclusion: 统一的时空深度对齐框架可以有效解决稀疏视角视频中的跨视角与跨时间深度不一致问题，无需前景/背景区分，并为动态高斯溅射4D重建提供显著增益。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the challenging problem of 4D reconstruction from sparse-view videos. This setup usually relies on monocular depth estimation to provide priors for the reconstruction model. A key challenge arises from limited cross-view overlap and temporal variation, making monocular depth predictions inconsistent across views and time. Existing methods align spatial and temporal dimensions in separate stages, requiring foreground segmentation masks while failing to leverage temporal cues for cross-view alignment. Contrary to these methods, we propose a unified spatial-temporal depth alignment framework that jointly resolves cross-view and cross-time inconsistencies without distinguishing foreground/background. Our method represents depth maps across views and time as a set of spatio-temporal neural fields. This representation not only yields fast convergence, but also captures spatio-temporal correlation among depth maps implicitly, without dependence on external segmentation/tracking models. We also propose a multi-view depth-order loss while leveraging the classic scale-and-shift-invariant loss to further improve the final depth quality. The aligned depths initialize and supervise Gaussian splatting models for 4D reconstruction. Experiments on Ego-Exo4D and EgoHuman demonstrate that our improved depth alignment substantially benefits dynamic Gaussian-splatting-based reconstruction methods for novel-time/view synthesis and geometry accuracy/consistency.

</details>


### [41] [GenPuzzle: Benchmarking Visual Reasoning in Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05902)
*Changpeng Zhao,Yiren Song,Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GenPuzzle基准，用于评测以推理为中心的图像生成能力。该基准包含12个轨道的2,005个视觉谜题（如迷宫、数独、七巧板、数学视觉证明等），要求模型在保留输入状态的同时输出符合逻辑的视觉解答。最强的前沿生成模型仅达到40.57 Macro Overall，表明逻辑推理、几何理解和状态保持方面仍存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成系统日益融合多模态理解、推理与合成能力，但已有评测多聚焦于美学质量、提示对齐、组合性等表层能力，缺乏对系统能否真正解决视觉问题并以像素形式忠实表达解决方案的系统性评估。

Method: 构建GenPuzzle基准：包含12个轨道共2,005个视觉谜题，覆盖模式补全、空间构造、迷宫、数独、数织、七巧板、棋类、火柴棍谜题、正投影和数学视觉证明等类型。采用任务特定的评测协议——离散网格输出通过转录后程序化验证，视觉复杂输出通过多层次MLLM评分规则评估，并通过对齐人类参考分数来筛选自动评判器。

Result: 三个前沿生成模型中，最强模型仅达到40.57 Macro Overall，暴露出在逻辑、几何、状态保持和指令执行等方面的频繁失败。

Conclusion: GenPuzzle为衡量图像生成从"渲染"迈向"视觉问题求解"提供了测试平台，表明当前的图像生成系统在真正解决视觉推理问题方面仍有很大提升空间。

Abstract: Recent image generation systems increasingly combine multimodal understanding, reasoning, and synthesis, suggesting that they may do more than render plausible scenes. Yet existing evaluations emphasize aesthetics, prompt alignment, compositionality, or text-based answers, leaving unclear whether these systems can solve visual problems and faithfully express solutions in pixels. We introduce GenPuzzle, a benchmark for reasoning-centric image generation. GenPuzzle contains 2,005 problems across 12 tracks, spanning pattern completion, spatial construction, mazes, Sudoku, nonograms, tangrams, board games, matchstick puzzles, orthographic projection, and mathematical visual proof. Each task provides a visual puzzle and requires an image output that preserves the input state while executing a logically valid solution. GenPuzzle uses task-specific evaluation protocols: discrete grid outputs are transcribed and verified programmatically, while visually complex outputs are assessed with tiered, multidimensional, or binary multimodal large language model (MLLM) rubrics. We further select the automatic judge by measuring agreement with human reference scores. Across three frontier generators, the strongest model reaches only 40.57 Macro Overall, revealing frequent failures in logic, geometry, state preservation, and instruction execution. GenPuzzle provides a testbed for measuring progress from image rendering toward visual problem solving.

</details>


### [42] [FreeTransformSR: Efficient Lightweight Image Super-Resolution via Free Low-Rank Learnable Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05912)
*Hongji Li,Yunhui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeTransformSR提出了一种基于通道级自由低秩可学习变换的轻量级超分辨率网络，通过数据驱动的方式学习任务自适应基函数实现特征调制，并整合局部特征调制分支、软复杂度自适应模块和自适应强度调制策略，在五个基准数据集上以仅595K参数达到具有竞争力的PSNR/SSIM性能，适用于资源受限场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像超分辨率方法虽然在重建质量上不断进步，但往往伴随着大量参数和计算开销，难以在资源受限的实际场景中部署。因此需要一种既保持良好重建性能又具有高效参数量和推理速度的轻量级超分辨率方案。

Method: 提出基于通道级自由低秩可学习变换的轻量级网络架构。主要包含三个核心组件：(1)自由低秩可学习变换，以数据驱动方式学习任务自适应基函数，实现低参数开销的特征调制；(2)局部特征调制分支，通过深度可分离卷积补充变换域处理以增强高频细节恢复；(3)软复杂度自适应模块，利用轻量级门控网络动态融合局部卷积与窗口自注意力分支的输出，根据区域纹理特征自适应调整融合比例。此外还引入自适应强度调制策略，在样本级别动态调整变换域响应强度。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，FreeTransformSR以显著更少的参数和FLOPs实现了具有竞争力的PSNR/SSIM性能。具体而言，在BSD100 x2上达到32.41 dB，在Urban100 x4上达到27.00 dB，仅需595K参数，且推理速度优于对比方法。

Conclusion: FreeTransformSR通过创新的自由低秩可学习变换框架及多组件的协同设计，在参数效率与重建质量之间取得了良好的平衡，验证了其在资源受限场景中作为高效超分辨率解决方案的可行性与实用性。

Abstract: Single image super-resolution aims to reconstruct high-resolution images from low-resolution inputs. This paper proposes FreeTransformSR, a novel lightweight super-resolution network based on a channel-wise free low-rank learnable transform. The transform learns task-adaptive basis functions in a data-driven manner, enabling adaptive feature modulation with minimal parameter overhead. To further enhance high-frequency detail recovery, we introduce a local feature modulation branch that complements transform-domain processing with depthwise convolution. In addition, a soft complexity adaptive module dynamically fuses the outputs of local convolution and window self-attention branches through a lightweight gating network, adaptively adjusting the fusion ratio based on regional texture characteristics. An adaptive intensity modulation strategy is also incorporated to adjust transform-domain response strength at the sample level, enabling the network to dynamically adjust processing intensity according to input features. Extensive experiments on five benchmark datasets demonstrate that FreeTransformSR achieves competitive PSNR/SSIM performance with significantly fewer parameters and FLOPs. Specifically, FreeTransformSR achieves 32.41 dB on BSD100 x2 and 27.00 dB on Urban100 x4 with only 595K parameters, while delivering faster inference speed than competing methods, making it well-suited for deployment in resource-constrained scenarios. Source code is available at: https://github.com/HJiLi/FreeTransformSR.

</details>


### [43] [STAR-Pro: Stage-Wise Token Adaptive Reduction with Progressive Refinement for Efficient Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05916)
*Yichen Guo,Tinghao Wang,Qizhe Zhang,Lingbei Meng,Yuan Zhang,Jiajun Cao,Hao Jiang,Chenwei Wu,Jixian Wu,Sixiang Chen,Tao Luo,Hongyang Cheng,Kai Tang,Chenxi Li,Renyuan Li,Xiande Huang,Wenya Wang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR-Pro，一种无需训练的视觉token剪枝框架。通过两个互补分析发现：激进的剪枝会丢弃大量视觉信息，且文本到视觉的注意力随解码器深度而变化，导致一次性剪枝决策不可靠。STAR-Pro采用两阶段策略：自适应阶段通过枢轴QR构造过预算的覆盖候选池，渐进阶段利用逐步演进的注意力裁剪生成嵌套幸存集。在7个LVLM和18个基准上验证了其有效性，在LLaVA-Video-7B上减少90.5%视觉token仍保留92.7%性能，实现2.24倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）处理数百到数千个视觉token带来巨大计算开销，促使研究者探索无需训练的视觉token剪枝方法。然而，作者发现现有研究中存在两个关键问题未被充分认识：一是激进的token剪枝在跨模态融合前就丢弃了大量实质性视觉信息；二是文本到视觉注意力在不同层间显著变化，使得一次性的静态剪枝决策不可靠。这两个发现共同揭示了有效剪枝需要同时保留融合前的广泛视觉覆盖，并在融合过程中逐步精炼保留的token。

Method: STAR-Pro是一个无需训练的两阶段框架。自适应阶段（Adaptive Stage）采用枢轴QR分解（pivoted QR）来构造一个超出预算的视觉token特征覆盖候选池，确保保留广泛的视觉信息。渐进阶段（Progressive Stage）利用选定的解码器层上逐步演进的文本到视觉注意力，在目标层平均token预算约束下，从候选池中逐步剪枝以构造嵌套的幸存者集合（nested survivor set），实现跨层的渐进精炼。

Result: 在7种不同架构的LVLM和18个图像及视频基准上进行了广泛实验。在LLaVA-Video-7B上，STAR-Pro在激进的剪枝条件下将视觉token减少90.5%，保留基线92.7%的性能，并实现了2.24倍的端到端推理加速。实验结果证明了该方法在多种LVLM架构上的通用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 作者提出一个关键洞察：有效的token剪枝不能只依赖一次性决策，而应同时兼顾融合前的广泛视觉覆盖与融合过程中的渐进精炼。STAR-Pro通过两阶段框架将这一原则落地，无需任何训练即可在各种LVLM上实现大幅推理加速且保持高保真性能，为视觉token高效推理提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) achieve strong multimodal understanding, but the hundreds to thousands of visual tokens they process impose substantial computational overhead, motivating training-free visual token pruning. In this work, we conduct two complementary analyses of visual token pruning. First, we measure the feature-space coverage of tokens retained before cross-modal fusion and find that aggressive pruning discards substantial visual information. Second, we track text-to-visual attention across decoder layers and find that the visual tokens considered important change substantially with depth, making one-shot pruning decisions unreliable. Together, these findings show that effective pruning should preserve broad visual coverage before fusion and progressively refine the retained tokens as cross-modal evidence evolves during fusion. We therefore propose STAR-Pro (STage-Wise Adaptive Token Reduction with Progressive Refinement), a training-free two-stage framework. Its Adaptive Stage applies pivoted QR to construct an over-budget feature-coverage candidate pool, while its Progressive Stage uses evolving text-to-visual attention at selected decoder layers to prune a nested survivor set under a target layer-average token budget. Extensive experiments across seven LVLMs spanning multiple architectures and 18 image and video benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of STAR-Pro under aggressive pruning. On LLaVA-Video-7B, STAR-Pro reduces visual tokens by 90.5%, retains 92.7% of baseline performance, and achieves a $2.24\times$ measured inference speedup. Code is available at https://github.com/EasonAI-5589/starpro.

</details>


### [44] [PAI-Actor: Cinematic Multi-Character Replacement in Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05918)
*Bangxun Tang,Heyuan Gao,Yiren Song,Guian Fang,Zijian He,Jie Yang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PAI-Actor，一个电影级多角色动画框架，用于在真实视频片段中替换和动画化多个角色，同时保留原始场景动态、相机运动和背景内容。该方法将多角色动画形式化为结构引导的人体恢复问题，构建了基于高质量电影数据的训练流水线，并通过双向到自回归的蒸馏框架实现高质量短片段生成（1080P）和高效长视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动画系统主要驱动单一静态图像或单一主体，无法满足在真实动态电影场景中替换并动画化多个角色并保持场景一致性、光照、阴影和电影感外观的需求，因此需要一个能够处理复杂多角色场景的框架。

Method: 1) 将多角色动画形式化为结构引导的人体恢复问题；2) 构建基于高质量电影数据的电影驱动训练流水线；3) 提出双向到自回归（bidirectional-to-autoregressive）蒸馏框架：先训练双向扩散Transformer用于1080P高质量短片段生成，再蒸馏为自回归视频到视频模型以支持高效推理和更长视频生成。

Result: 实验表明PAI-Actor实现了高保真多角色动画，具备强大的场景一致性、电影级视觉质量和高效的长序列视频生成能力。

Conclusion: PAI-Actor通过结构引导的人体恢复和电影数据驱动的训练，结合双向到自回归蒸馏策略，成功实现了动态电影场景中多角色替换与动画化的实际应用，兼顾了生成质量与推理效率。

Abstract: We present PAI-Actor, a cinematic multi-character animation framework for character replacement in dynamic movie scenes. Unlike conventional animation systems that mainly drive a single static image or a single subject, our goal is to replace and animate multiple characters within real video clips while preserving the original scene dynamics, camera motion, and background content. This setting is particularly challenging because the generated characters must remain consistent with the source performance in motion and interaction, while also matching the surrounding background in lighting, shadow, composition, and overall cinematic appearance. To address this, we formulate multi-character animation as a structure-guided human recovery problem and build a movie-driven training pipeline from high-quality film data. Furthermore, to support practical cinematic production, we introduce a bidirectional-to-autoregressive distillation framework: we first train a bidirectional diffusion transformer for high-quality short-clip generation at 1080P resolution, and then distill it into an autoregressive video-to-video model for efficient inference and longer video generation. Experiments show that PAI-Actor enables high-fidelity multi-character animation with strong scene consistency, cinematic visual quality, and efficient long-form generation.

</details>


### [45] [AVSplat: Dense-View Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting with Assist-View Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05925)
*Muyu Xu,Fangneng Zhan,Yu Wei,Hanspeter Pfister,Shijian Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AVSplat提出了一种无位姿前馈3D高斯泼溅框架，通过Assist View预处理（每视图与少量相关且多样的辅助视图进行轻量交互）和自适应温度感知体素融合（基于占用率和点置信度锐化归属），解决了密集视图输入导致性能退化的问题，实现了稳定的正视图缩放效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无位姿前馈3D高斯泼溅方法在密集多视图输入下性能反而退化，原因是全局聚合将注意力分散到过多token上，且朴素体素融合将大量高斯平均成过度平滑的表示，无法从增加的视图数量中获得可靠收益。

Method: AVSplat包含两个关键组件：1）Assist View Preconditioning——在全局注意力之前，每个视图仅与少量按相关性和多样性挑选的辅助视图进行单次轻量交互，缓存的特征提供聚焦的场景上下文以稳定对应关系；2）Occupancy-guided Voxel Fusion——自适应的温度感知体素融合，在高占用率下根据占用率和点置信度锐化归属权重，避免过平滑。

Result: AVSplat恢复了正视图缩放效应，即在增加输入视图数量时性能保持稳定或持续提升而非退化。消融实验表明，Assist View Preconditioning主要负责防止密集视图退化，而Occupancy-guided Voxel Fusion贡献了大部分单视角图像质量提升。

Conclusion: 通过将额外视图转化为聚合和表征上的可靠信号，AVSplat有效解决了密集视图输入下的性能退化问题，验证了稀疏交互辅助视图和占用率引导融合在无位姿3D高斯泼溅中的有效性。

Abstract: Pose-free feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting enables novel view synthesis from uncalibrated multi-view images. Although more views should improve performance, existing methods often degrade with dense-view inputs because global aggregation spreads attention over many tokens, and naive voxel fusion averages many Gaussians into overly smooth representations. We present AVSplat, a framework that turns additional views into reliable signals for both aggregation and representation. Before global attention, each view performs a single lightweight interaction with a small set of Assist Views chosen for relevance and diversity, and the cached features provide a focused scene context that stabilizes correspondence. For representation, we use adaptive temperature-aware voxel fusion that sharpens attribution under high occupancy, guided by occupancy and point confidence. Crucially, AVSplat restores positive view scaling where performance remains stable or improves as more input views are added, instead of degrading in the dense-view regime. Ablations show that Assist View Preconditioning is primarily responsible for preventing dense-view degradation, while Occupancy-guided Voxel Fusion contributes most of the single-point image-quality gains.

</details>


### [46] [Beyond Classification: Structured Supervision Aligns Visual Evidence with Medical Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05937)
*Hexiang Bai,Hanyang Xu,Xiaoxue Li,Xiaoliang Wu,Shangde Gao,Hongxia Xu,Ke Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了三种结构化监督预训练范式（图自监督拓扑先验、分割密集像素约束、图像-文本跨模态语义对齐）以解决ViT在医学图像分析中因全局分类预训练导致的空间塌缩问题，实验发现跨模态图像-文本对齐效果最优，能够引导模型注意力聚焦于真实病灶区域，在下游临床分类任务中取得更优准确率与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ViT预训练采用全局图像分类，存在空间塌缩问题，即模型过度依赖背景捷径而非定位关键的前景病灶区域，限制了医学图像分析中视觉证据与精确医学语义的对齐。

Method: 系统比较三种结构化监督预训练范式：(1)基于图自监督的拓扑先验；(2)基于分割的密集像素级约束；(3)基于图像-文本对的跨模态语义对齐。通过嵌入高维诊断逻辑，引导注意力聚焦于精确视觉证据。

Result: 三种结构化监督均能缓解全局池化瓶颈并将视觉注意力引导至前景区域，但图像-文本对齐表现最优，显著提升下游临床分类任务的准确率，并产生聚焦真实病理特征的高可解释性注意力图，大幅超越普通分类基线。

Conclusion: 语义丰富的跨模态预训练能从根本上增强模型的特征表示能力，是实现精准且可解释医学图像分析的有效途径。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) have shown immense potential in medical image analysis. However, standard pre-training via global image classification suffers from spatial collapse, where models rely heavily on background shortcuts rather than localising critical foreground lesions. To overcome this limitation and align visual evidence with precise medical semantics, we systematically investigate alternative pre-training paradigms.Specifically, we evaluate three independent forms of structured supervision: topological priors via graph self-supervision, dense pixel-level constraints via segmentation, and cross-modal semantic grounding via image-text pairs. Notably, our empirical analysis reveals that while all three forms of structured supervision successfully alleviate the global pooling bottleneck and steer visual attention towards foreground regions, image-text alignment achieves the most superior performance. By embedding high-dimensional diagnostic logic, the cross-modal approach not only anchors attention on precise visual evidence but also enables profound abstract reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that this semantically enriched pre-training fundamentally enhances the model's feature representation. Consequently, when fine-tuned for downstream clinical classification tasks, our models achieve superior accuracy and yield highly interpretable attention maps focused on true pathological features, vastly outperforming vanilla classification baselines.

</details>


### [47] [ProtoRAG: Prototype-Based Retrieval Augmentation for Few-Shot Fine-Grained Remote Sensing Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05953)
*Jian Wang,Yuxiang Hong,Chufeng Zhou,Chao Pang,Xiaokang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了ProtoRAG，一种基于原型检索增强的少样本细粒度遥感目标检测框架，通过解耦粗定位与细粒度识别并结合外部目标级视觉记忆，在三个数据集上超越最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本细粒度遥感目标检测面临双重挑战：有限的标注既要支持目标定位，又要支持视觉相似子类间的判别。多模态大语言模型虽能提供粗定位，但缺乏可靠的细粒度识别的视觉证据。

Method: ProtoRAG包含三个核心组件：(1) 判别性原型空间学习（DPSL），通过监督对比学习和原型一致性正则化构建稳定的判别性原型表示；(2) 不确定性引导的候选约束推理策略，仅对模糊实例调用多模态推理并注入检索到的候选特定视觉参考；(3) 将粗定位与细粒度识别解耦，通过外部目标级视觉记忆增强MLLM。

Result: 在MAR20、HRSC2016和FAIR1M-2.0三个数据集共九种少样本设置上均超越代表性基线，mAP50分别比最强基线高出14.80、2.27和4.04。

Conclusion: 实验证明ProtoRAG通过检索增强机制有效解决了MLLM在少样本细粒度遥感目标检测中缺乏视觉证据的问题，验证了解耦定位与识别策略的有效性。

Abstract: Few-shot fine-grained object detection (FGOD) in remote sensing imagery is challenging because limited annotations must support both object localization and discrimination among visually similar subcategories. Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide strong coarse object localization, they lack explicit visual evidence for reliable fine-grained recognition. To address this limitation, we propose ProtoRAG, a prototype-based retrieval-augmented framework that decouples coarse localization from fine-grained recognition by equipping MLLMs with an external object-level visual memory. To construct a reliable visual memory from limited support samples, we introduce Discriminative Prototype Space Learning (DPSL), which encourages discriminative and prototype-stable representations through supervised contrastive learning and prototype-consistency regularization. We further develop an uncertainty-guided candidate-constrained reasoning strategy that augments MLLMs with retrieved candidate-specific visual references and invokes multimodal reasoning only for ambiguous instances. Extensive experiments show that ProtoRAG consistently surpasses representative baselines in nine few-shot settings, outperforming the strongest baselines by 14.80, 2.27, and 4.04 mAP$_{50}$ on MAR20, HRSC2016, and FAIR1M-2.0, respectively.

</details>


### [48] [STP-BENCH: A Unified Systematic Benchmark for Virtual Spatial Transcriptomics from Histopathology Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05956)
*Youngmin Chung,Ji Hun Ha,Andrew H. Song,Cristina Almagro-Pérez,Chaeyoung Seo,Won Jun Suh,Jeong Won Beom,Kyoung Bin Oh,Eytan Ruppin,Faisal Mahmood,Joo Sang Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了STP-BENCH，一个标准化的虚拟空间转录组（虚拟ST）模型基准测试框架，涵盖6种癌症类型、两个ST平台（Visium和Xenium），包含超过30,000个点及至少15张幻灯片的训练数据。作者系统评估了21种预测方法，统一使用病理学基础模型作为形态学编码器，并深入考察了基因可恢复性、下游生物学效用（细胞类型去卷积和空间域识别）以及模型在不同域偏移和数据规模下的鲁棒性。关键发现是统一形态学编码会显著改变先前研究中的模型排名，表明此前评估中架构创新与图像编码存在混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学虽能提供肿瘤异质性的空间解析基因表达，但高昂的实验成本限制了其大规模应用，因此出现了直接从H&E组织切片预测空间基因表达的虚拟ST计算方法。然而，该领域缺乏标准化基准评估——已有研究依赖小型异构数据集、训练和推理流程不一致，且对生物学可解释性和模型鲁棒性的评估不足，这使得衡量领域进展变得困难。

Method: 作者构建了STP-BENCH基准框架：(1) 涵盖6种癌症类型和2个ST平台（Visium和Xenium），每个训练数据集含超过30,000个点和至少15张幻灯片以确保统计可靠性；(2) 在架构适用的情况下，用统一的病理学基础模型作为形态学编码器重新实现并评估21种预测方法；(3) 系统性分析从组织形态学可恢复的基因和基因集；(4) 通过细胞类型去卷积和空间域识别评估预测图谱的下游生物学效用；(5) 评估模型在域偏移和数据规模变化下的可靠性。

Result: 统一形态学编码大大重新排序了先前研究中建立的模型排名，表明此前评估中架构创新与图像编码被混淆。作者将模型排名变化归因于形态学编码器的统一，这揭示了架构层面创新与底层图像编码方式的贡献被混杂的问题。

Conclusion: STP-BENCH为虚拟ST领域提供了标准化、可复现的社区基准，有助于厘清不同模型方法的真实性能差异。作者公开了该基准（https://github.com/NEXGEM/STP-Bench）以支持可复现性，并推动该领域的规范评估。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) provides unprecedented insights into tumor heterogeneity by capturing spatially resolved gene expression, yet its high experimental cost hinders large-scale adoption. Consequently, computational approaches that predict spatial gene expression directly from hematoxylin and eosin slides, termed virtual ST, have rapidly emerged. Despite this progress, assessing advances in the field remains difficult due to insufficient benchmarking: prior studies rely on small, heterogeneous datasets, inconsistent training and inference pipelines, and limited evaluation of biological interpretability and model robustness. To address these gaps, we present STP-BENCH, a standardized benchmark for virtual ST models. STP-BENCH comprises six cancer types spanning two ST platforms (Visium and Xenium), with each training dataset containing more than 30,000 spots and at least 15 slides to ensure statistical reliability. We evaluate 21 predictive approaches, re-implemented with a unified pathology foundation model as the morphological encoder when architecturally applicable. Beyond conventional benchmarks that report average predictive accuracy on highly variable genes, we systematically examine which genes and gene sets are recoverable from histomorphology. We further evaluate the downstream biological utility of predicted profiles through cell-type deconvolution and spatial domain identification, and assess model reliability under domain shifts and data scaling. Notably, unified morphological encoding substantially re-orders model rankings established in prior studies, indicating that architectural innovations and image encoding have been conflated in previous evaluations. We publicly release STP-BENCH to support reproducibility and serve as a community benchmark at https://github.com/NEXGEM/STP-Bench.

</details>


### [49] [FineHOI: Part-Aware Dense Representations for Zero-Shot Human-Object Interaction Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05959)
*Francesco Tonini,Lorenzo Vaquero,Mohammad Mahdi Derakhshani,Cees Snoek,Elisa Ricci,Cigdem Beyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FineHOI，一个零样本人-物交互（HOI）检测框架，通过密集的补丁级特征显式建模交互关系。该方法利用自适应部件级注意力模块，通过无监督聚类将人类和物体分解为语义一致的部件并依据交互相关性重新加权，再通过区域感知交互Transformer整合部件级与全局特征。实验表明FineHOI在未见交互上持续优于现有零样本HOI方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本HOI方法依赖视觉-语言模型，但通常使用全局或检测器中心的特征，这会压缩交互线索并阻碍细粒度空间推理。由于人-物交互由局部空间关系定义，全局和检测器中心表示无法保留这些关键信息，因此需要从密集补丁级特征出发显式建模交互。

Method: FineHOI包含两个核心模块：1) 自适应部件级注意力模块，通过无监督聚类将人类和物体分解为语义一致的部件，并根据交互相关性对部件进行重新加权；2) 区域感知交互Transformer，用于整合部件级特征和全局特征，生成最终的HOI嵌入表示。

Result: 大量实验表明FineHOI在零样本HOI检测任务上持续优于现有方法，尤其在未见过（unseen）交互类别上取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: FineHOI验证了显式建模密集补丁级局部空间关系对于零样本HOI检测的有效性，为克服全局特征不适配细粒度交互推理问题提供了可行的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Human-Object Interaction (HOI) detection aims to localize humans and objects in images and classify their interactions. Zero-shot HOI focuses on recognizing interactions that are not observed during training, requiring models to generalize beyond seen verb-object compositions. Recent approaches leverage Vision-Language Models (VLMs), benefiting from rich semantic representations. However, they often rely on global or detector-centric features that compress interaction cues and hinder fine-grained spatial reasoning. To overcome this limitation, we propose FineHOI, a zero-shot HOI framework that explicitly models interactions from dense patch-level features. Our approach is motivated by the observation that human-object interactions are defined by localized spatial relationships, which are not preserved by global and detector-centric representations. To this end, we introduce an Adaptive Part-Level Attention module that decomposes humans and objects into semantically coherent parts via unsupervised clustering, and re-weights them based on their interaction relevance. These representations are then integrated through a Region-Aware Interaction Transformer that integrates part-aware and global features and produces the final HOI embedding. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FineHOI consistently outperforms existing zero-shot HOI methods, achieving particularly strong gains on unseen interactions. Code is available at https://github.com/francescotonini/fine-hoi.

</details>


### [50] [Test-Time Weak-to-Strong Alignment: Transferring Implicit Rewards from Weak to Strong Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05968)
*Xin Xie,Fan Zhang,Dong Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AlignGraft方法，通过一对弱模型（源对齐模型及其基座）的"速度差"作为监督信号，在采样过程中对冻结的大规模文本到图像流模型进行测试时对齐，无需奖励函数或其梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐微调方法需要对每个检查点重复训练且无法在采样时灵活调整对齐目标；而测试时对齐方法依赖从奖励函数本身获取每步信号（通过梯度或单独训练的价值函数），这些方法受限于奖励函数的可用性和计算成本。

Method: 引入"源对齐对"概念（一个源对齐模型及其对应的基座模型），其在训练期间存储的奖励信号被编码为隐含的、逐步的、KL锚定的速度差。AlignGraft在采样过程中将源对齐对的"速度差"添加到更大、冻结、从未微调的模型上，实现跨规模的模型形式监督传输。该方法在共享加噪核下是精确的传输，测试时仅需一个标量控制对齐强度，且可外推超过源对齐模型的强度。

Result: 在图像和视频流模型（Stable Diffusion 3.5、FLUX和Wan）上的实验表明：转移能在偏好、组合和文本渲染奖励上提升冻结的大模型表现，可以超越源对齐模型本身，同时以微小恒定采样开销保持大模型保真度。一次在弱模型上的对齐运行产生的监督可被整个模型家族在测试时复用。

Conclusion: AlignGraft验证了模型形式的监督可以跨规模传递对齐信息，使一次对齐运行惠及整个模型家族，为测试时对齐提供了无需奖励函数的替代方案。

Abstract: Aligning a text-to-image generation flow model with a reward makes it follow objectives that the training data alone does not provide. Alignment fine-tuning delivers this by reinforcement learning (RL) or preference optimization, but it must be repeated for every checkpoint and returns a model fixed at the reward and strength it was trained with. Test-time alignment instead steers a frozen model during sampling, allowing task-specific and sample-specific guidance. Existing methods obtain this only by drawing the per-step signal from the reward function itself, through its gradient, or through a separately trained value function. We propose changing the supervision source: let a pair of weak models, not a reward function, supply the supervision. A source aligned model, kept together with its base as a source alignment pair, stores its training reward as an implicit, step-wise, KL-anchored signal expressed in the sampler's own coordinates. We explore whether this model-form supervision can cross scale, and show that it does: our method, AlignGraft, aligns a larger, frozen, never-tuned model by adding the pair's velocity difference during sampling. The transport is exact under a shared noising kernel and needs neither the reward nor its gradient at test time. The method has no schedules, only a single scalar that controls the alignment strength and can extrapolate it beyond that of the source alignment pair. Across image and video flow models (Stable Diffusion 3.5, FLUX, and Wan), the transfer lifts the frozen large model on preference, compositional, and text-rendering rewards, can exceed the source aligned model itself, and preserves the large model's fidelity at a small constant sampling overhead. Extensive experiments show that one alignment run on a weak model produces supervision that the whole model family can reuse at test time.

</details>


### [51] [Efficient and Robust Camera-independent Multiview 3D Geometric Reconstruction from Noisy Monocular Depth Estimation and Multiple Point Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05972)
*Marius Leordeanu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅基于点间线性关系进行3D几何重建的高效鲁棒方法，通过学习一个与相机无关的线性自回归矩阵W来表示3D点集的固有几何结构，无需使用相机投影矩阵或恢复相机姿态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D重建方法通常依赖相机模型和位姿估计，而深度学习方法提供的单目深度图虽然快速但噪声较大。作者旨在探索一种绕过相机参数、仅利用点集内部几何关系的重建方案，以提高鲁棒性和简易性。

Method: 方法包含两个版本：(1) 基于多帧带噪声的单目深度图，通过鲁棒线性估计学习3D点之间的线性自回归矩阵W；(2) 在弱透视投影条件下，直接从2D图像投影中鲁棒估计3D点间的线性组合关系。W的特征值为1的主特征向量提供了3D点配置的齐次表示。整个推导过程从不使用相机投影矩阵。

Result: 该方法能够仅从多帧点对应关系中恢复3D几何结构，矩阵W是点集的内在属性，不随世界坐标系或相机位姿变化；两个版本分别适用于单目深度图和弱透视投影两种场景，均展现了鲁棒的3D结构重建能力。

Conclusion: 作者证明了3D结构可以从点间时不变的线性关系中直接恢复而无需相机位姿，这与现有依赖相机参数的3D重建文献形成了关键区别。该方法为3D几何重建提供了一种新的、与相机解耦的途径。

Abstract: We present an efficient and robust method for 3D geometric reconstruction that is based solely on the camera-independent linear relationships among a given set of points, which are stable over time and robustly estimated using multiple point matches. We essentially learn, from correspondences between points across several frames, a linear geometric auto-regression matrix $\mathbf{W}$, which establishes how a point in 3D can be expressed as a linear combination of all the others. This matrix is constant and does not depend on the world coordinate system or the camera pose---it is an intrinsic property of the point set. We also show that the principal eigenvectors of $\mathbf{W}$, which all have eigenvalue $1$, provide a homogeneous representation of the 3D point configuration.
  The first version of our method takes advantage of noisy monocular depth maps in order to obtain, from multiple frames, a robust geometric auto-regression matrix $\mathbf{W}$ of linear relationships between the 3D points. Thus, we build on recent advances in deep learning, which now provide monocular depth estimation models that are fast but very often noisy. Our approach handles noise through robust linear estimation over several frames.
  The second version of our method does not need monocular depth estimation maps. It applies in cases of weak-perspective projection, when the linear combinations between the 3D points can be robustly estimated from their 2D projections in the image.
  Note that the camera projection matrix is never used in our derivations. Consequently, our method does not recover camera pose, but only 3D structure. This is a key difference between our method and the related literature on 3D geometric reconstruction.

</details>


### [52] [Accelerating Diffusion Transformers with Gaussian Process Rectified Feature Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05981)
*Zhirong Shen,Rui Huang,Chang Zou,Shikang Zheng,Jiacheng Liu,Peiliang Cai,Zhengyi Shi,Yaosong Du,Liang Feng,Xiaobing Tu,Jinkui Ren,Xiantao Zhang,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GP-Refiner，一种基于高斯过程回归的即插即用校正框架，用于加速扩散Transformer的同时修正预测型特征缓存方法随步骤增加而产生的偏差，通过将全计算步骤特征视为参考特征的含噪声观测来解决标签数据不可用问题，并利用GPR的后验方差实现不确定性自适应计算策略，在多种模型上均取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer计算成本高，严重阻碍实时应用。预测型特征缓存虽被广泛用于加速，但随着推理步骤增加，其预测与参考全计算轨迹之间的偏差逐渐累积增大。虽然有直觉想法是用在线回归模型动态校正此偏差，但加速过程中无法获得标签数据（即参考全计算特征），这是需要解决的核心难题。

Method: 首先通过统计分析发现，缓存方法在全计算步骤的特征残差相对于参考全计算轨迹局部服从零均值高斯分布。基于此发现，将全计算步骤的特征视为参考特征的含噪声观测，从而解决标签数据不可用的问题。进一步提出即插即用的GP-Refiner框架，使用高斯过程回归进行偏差校正，并利用GPR的性质引入不确定性自适应计算策略，通过实时监控后验方差来触发必要的全计算校准。

Result: 实验表明，该框架在多种模型上结合各种最先进方法时均取得显著改进。将GP-Refiner与TaylorSeer结合使用时，计算负载降低19.3%，PSNR提升0.9 dB，LPIPS从0.46降至0.29。

Conclusion: GP-Refiner通过统计观测解决标签数据不可用问题，并借助高斯过程回归的不确定性驱动自适应计算策略，有效修正了扩散Transformer加速过程中的预测偏差，在降低计算开销的同时提升了生成质量，且作为即插即用框架具有良好的通用性。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers have become the dominant paradigm in generative AI, but their high computational costs severely hinder real-time applications. Prediction-based feature caching is widely used to accelerate diffusion transformers; however, as the number of steps increases, the deviation between its predictions and the reference full-compute trajectory gradually grows. An intuitive idea is to use an online regression model to dynamically correct this deviation, but it faces the issue of label data being unavailable during the acceleration process. This paper presents a statistical observation that the residuals between the features of full computation steps using caching methods and reference full-compute trajectory locally exhibit a zero-mean Gaussian distribution. By treating the features of full computation steps as noisy observations of reference features, the data acquisition problem is resolved. Based on this observation, a plug-and-play GP-Refiner correction framework is proposed. This method utilizes Gaussian Process Regression for correction and, leveraging the properties of GPR, introduces an uncertainty-adaptive computation strategy that triggers necessary full-computation calibration by monitoring the posterior variance in real time. Experiments demonstrate significant improvements across different models when combined with various state-of-the-art methods. Integrating the proposed framework with TaylorSeer reduces the computational load by 19.3% while improving PSNR by 0.9 dB and reducing LPIPS from 0.46 to 0.29. Code is available in https://github.com/Aredstone/GP-Refiner.

</details>


### [53] [MORPHA: Morphology-Constrained Training and the Limits of Cross-Acquisition Transfer in Low-Resource Malaria Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05990)
*Favour Okechukwu Igwezeke,Chikodili Helen Ugwuishiwu,Joseph Uzochukwu Emesiani,Samuel Ifebuche Agada,Ekenechukwu Lilian Anozie,Mary Ofuru Kama,Adaobi Chiazor Emegoakor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究低资源疟疾显微成像中跨制片制备方式的迁移问题，提出形态一致性约束MORPHA，利用BBBC041数据集的阶段标注统计约束训练过程。在乌干达Lacuna真实田野数据的薄涂片到厚涂片迁移中，MORPHA将二分类泛化下降减少30.8%（F1从0.578提升至0.699），域内校准误差降低49%（ECE从0.0162降至0.0082）。内容无关对照实验表明真实测量内容而非约束本身贡献了主要增益，同时标准置信度正则化在原始迁移上表现更优，揭示了两者的适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源疟疾显微成像场景中，模型在一种制片制备方式上训练后常面对另一种制备方式的图像，形态学约束能否跨此类采集差距迁移尚不明确。现有工作缺乏对真实非洲田野数据上这一问题的系统研究，也难以回答形态学先验相比通用正则化的增补价值在哪里。

Method: 提出MORPHA形态一致性约束：从带阶段标注的BBBC041数据集中推导阶段条件统计量，惩罚偏离这些统计的预测。该约束统一适用于二分类、目标级和阶段感知三个范式，不改变架构或推理，仅在二分类范式中塑造训练过程。检测范式被映射为边界条件。通过内容无关对照（对随机统计施加相同约束）和与两种标准置信度正则化比较来定位形态学约束的独特贡献。

Result: 在薄涂片细胞到厚涂片田野图像的迁移中，MORPHA将二分类泛化下降减少30.8%（F1: 0.578→0.699），域内代价可忽略，域内校准误差降低49%（ECE: 0.0162→0.0082）。内容无关对照仅恢复25.3%的差距（vs 30.8%），证明测量内容贡献增益。标准置信度正则化在原始迁移上超过该约束。两个部署相关边界被揭示：薄片统计不迁移到厚片检测（滋养体AP@0.50降至0.000），跨采集伪标签在过滤前即失效。

Conclusion: 形态学一致性约束在二分类跨采集迁移中提供实质增益且域内成本低，其价值来自测量得到的生物学内容而非约束机制本身。但形态学先验并非万灵药：对于检测任务和伪标签策略，存在明确失效边界，需结合通用正则化策略。研究为低资源疟疾数据与模型设计提供了基于证据的指导。

Abstract: In low-resource malaria microscopy, a model trained on one smear preparation routinely meets images from another, and how well morphology-based constraints transfer across this acquisition gap is unclear. We study this on real African field microscopy from Uganda (Lacuna), asking where encoding measured parasite morphology as a training constraint improves cross-acquisition transfer and where generic regularisation suffices. We present MORPHA, a morphological consistency constraint that derives stage-conditional statistics from the stage-annotated BBBC041 dataset and penalises predictions that deviate from them. Defined uniformly across binary, object-level, and stage-aware regimes without changing architecture or inference, it shapes training in the binary regime. The detection regime is a mapped boundary. On transfer from thin-smear cells to thick-smear field images, the constraint reduces the binary-classification generalisation drop by 30.8% (F1 0.578 to 0.699) at negligible within-domain cost and lowers in-distribution calibration error by 49% (ECE 0.0162 to 0.0082). A content-free control applying the identical constraint to random statistics recovers less of the drop (25.3% vs 30.8%), indicating the measured content, not constraining alone, contributes to the gain. Two standard confidence regularisers exceed the constraint on raw transfer, locating where morphology adds value and where generic regularisation suffices. We map two deployment-relevant boundaries: thin-smear statistics do not transfer to thick-smear detection (trophozoite AP@0.50 falls to 0.000), and cross-acquisition pseudo-labelling fails before filtering applies. Together these yield a morphology-grounded consistency signal and evidence-based guidance for malaria dataset and model design in low-resource settings.

</details>


### [54] [Geometry-Aware Test-Time Learning for Quantitative Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06004)
*Gege Zhang,Shuaicheng Niu,Gang Dai,Lei Sun,Shuangping Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TTL-SR，一种基于几何感知的测试时学习框架，用于提升视觉语言模型（VLM）在量化空间推理任务中的表现。该方法利用几何一致性约束和未标注测试数据，通过辅助查询增强、自适应几何触发过滤和几何感知多目标损失，在不使用额外3D标注的前提下实现模型自适应，在Q-Spatial-ScanNet数据集上分别提升了Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct和SpatialRGPT-VILA-1.5-8B约6.47%和9.41%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在量化空间推理任务中表现脆弱，面对分布偏移（如新物体配置或重述的空间查询）时容易产生不一致或矛盾的预测，这源于学习表示与底层几何之间的错配。而获取3D空间监督信息的成本高昂，难以通过大规模标注来缓解该问题，因此亟需一种无需额外3D标注的自适应方法。

Method: TTL-SR是一个几何感知的测试时学习框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）通过几何耦合的辅助查询增强输入，增加可用的几何信号；（2）利用自适应几何触发机制过滤不可靠预测，构建结构化的token级伪标签；（3）基于仅使用测试数据的几何感知多目标损失函数更新模型参数。

Result: 在Q-Spatial-ScanNet数据集上，TTL-SR为Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct带来6.47%的准确率提升，为SpatialRGPT-VILA-1.5-8B带来9.41%的准确率提升，显著改善了空间推理性能。

Conclusion: TTL-SR证明了通过测试时学习结合几何一致性约束，可以在不需要昂贵3D标注的情况下有效缓解VLM在空间推理领域的分布偏移问题，为提升VLM的量化空间推理稳健性提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Quantitative spatial reasoning in visual-language models (VLMs) aims to infer spatial distances and directional relationships among objects in 3D space from a 2D image and a natural language query. Despite recent progress, VLM spatial reasoning remains brittle under distribution shifts, largely due to the high cost of 3D supervision. As a result, models often produce inconsistent or contradictory predictions when faced with novel object configurations or rephrased spatial queries, revealing a misalignment between learned representations and underlying geometry. To address this, we propose TTL-SR, a geometry-aware Test-Time Learning framework for quantitative Spatial Reasoning that leverages geometric consistency constraints and unlabeled test data to adapt models to target domains. Specifically, TTL-SR augments the input query with geometrically coupled auxiliary queries, filters unreliable predictions via adaptive geometric triggering to construct structured token-level pseudo-labels, and updates model parameters under a geometry-aware multi-objective loss using only test data. Experimental results demonstrate that TTL-SR significantly boosts spatial reasoning performance, yielding 6.47% and 9.41% accuracy gains for Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct and SpatialRGPT-VILA-1.5-8B on Q-Spatial-ScanNet dataset, respectively.

</details>


### [55] [Depth-to-Image Synthesis-Driven Generative Unguided Depth Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06007)
*Jiayi Yuan,Na Zhao,De Wen Soh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GUDC（生成式无引导深度补全）方法，创新性地将2D生成模型（ControlNet）与无引导深度补全相结合，利用稀疏深度合成伪图像以提供语义线索，从而在不依赖真实RGB输入的情况下实现语义感知的深度补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有引导的深度补全方法高度依赖RGB图像质量和对齐精度，而无引导方法由于缺乏显式视觉线索往往精度受限。现有方法无法同时兼顾引导信息带来的语义丰富性和无引导设置的鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 利用ControlNet的深度条件生成能力，从稀疏深度合成伪图像，将无引导深度补全转换为语义引导任务；2) 提出多层级稠密到稀疏表示蒸馏策略，以稠密深度特征作为教师信号，为稀疏深度输入微调ControlNet，解决图像-深度错位问题；3) 提出伪图像语义注意力融合模块，在补全过程中自适应提取有效语义信息并抑制纹理幻觉等伪影。

Result: 在KITTI和NYUv2数据集上的大量实验表明，GUDC相比现有方法取得了更优的精度和更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GUDC成功验证了将2D生成模型能力引入无引导深度补全的可行性，实现了无需真实RGB输入的语义感知深度推断，为深度补全领域提供了新的研究范式。

Abstract: Guided depth completion methods heavily depend on RGB quality and alignment, while unguided ones often suffer from limited precision due to the absence of explicit visual cues. In this paper, we present Depth-to-Image Synthesis-Driven Generative Unguided Depth Completion (GUDC), a new completion paradigm that innovatively bridges advanced 2D generative models with unguided depth completion, enabling semantics-aware depth inference without real RGB inputs. Our key idea is to exploit ControlNet's powerful depth-conditioned generation capability to synthesize pseudo-images directly from sparse depth, effectively converting the original unguided setting into a semantics-guided one. To address the potential image-depth misalignment caused by depth sparsity, we propose a multi-level dense-to-sparse representation distillation strategy for ControlNet fine-tuning, where dense-depth features act as teacher signals to distill consistent structural representations for sparse-depth inputs. Furthermore, during pseudo-image-guided completion, we propose a pseudo-image semantic attention fusion module to adaptively extract informative semantic cues from pseudo-images while suppressing artifacts (e.g., texture hallucinations). Extensive experiments on KITTI and NYUv2 validate that our GUDC achieves superior accuracy and robustness over existing methods.

</details>


### [56] [FujinSplat: Seeing Through Smoke with RAW-Domain Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06017)
*Gengjia Chang,Ziteng Cui,Shuhong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FujinSplat提出了一种在RAW域中进行烟雾场景三维重建的新方法。通过分离参与介质的视点相关辐射影响与ISP的非线性色调/颜色变换，利用逐场景的Base ISP作为光度锚点，并训练一个场景无关的控制器来回归逐视图的校正动作，最终监督一个静态3D高斯表示，在真实烟雾基准RealX3D上超越了物理重建和恢复-重建管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 烟雾场景的外观由两个耦合过程共同决定：参与介质对视点相关的场景辐射产生改变，随后ISP施加非线性色调与颜色变换。传统的逐视图sRGB去雾在ISP已经将两者纠缠之后进行操作，而标准3D重建则忽略介质将其吸收进场景几何和辐射中，两种方式都无法有效解决该问题，因此需要在RAW域中解耦这两个过程。

Method: FujinSplat在RAW域中处理问题，使两个过程保持可分离性。首先，从场景的雾霾RAW捕获中拟合一个逐场景的Base ISP映射到其自身的相机渲染，然后冻结该ISP作为固定的光度锚点且不执行去雾。其次，通过分析专家修正发现了一个紧凑的低维修正空间，可从RAW中识别。FujinSplat在训练姿态下拟合逐视图的动作答案，并训练一个场景无关的控制器从RAW中回归这些动作。修正后的视图监督一个静态3D高斯表示，同时结合一个有界的逐视图残差来调和跨视角的光度不一致性。

Result: 在RealX3D真实烟雾基准上，FujinSplat明显优于最强的可比基线，领先于基于物理的重建方法和先恢复后3DGS的管线。

Conclusion: FujinSplat验证了在RAW域中解耦介质影响与ISP变换的有效性，通过Base ISP提供固定光度锚点并结合场景无关控制器的修正策略，能够从有雾场景中恢复干净的3D表示，为烟雾场景重建提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: The appearance of a smoky scene is shaped by two processes that a camera records together: the participating medium alters scene radiance in a view-dependent way, and the image signal processor (ISP) then remaps the result through a nonlinear tone and color transformation. Recovering a clean 3D scene requires separating both. Per-view sRGB dehazing acts only after the ISP has entangled them; standard 3D reconstruction ignores the medium and absorbs it into scene geometry and radiance. FujinSplat addresses the problem in the RAW domain, where the two processes remain separable. A per-scene Base ISP is fitted from the scene's hazy RAW captures to its own camera renderings and then frozen, providing a fixed photometric anchor that performs no dehazing. Analyzing expert corrections reveals a compact, low-dimensional correction space identifiable from RAW alone. FujinSplat therefore fits per-view action answers at the training poses and trains a single scene-agnostic controller to regress them from RAW; the corrected views supervise one static 3D Gaussian representation, jointly with a bounded per-view residual that reconciles cross-view photometric inconsistencies. On the RealX3D real-world smoke benchmark FujinSplat clearly outperforms the strongest comparable baseline, ahead of both physics-based reconstruction and restoration-then-3DGS pipelines.

</details>


### [57] [What Does Animal Re-Identification Learn? Linear Biological Concepts and Their Origins in Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06020)
*Robert Nolting,Alexandra Schild,Moritz Weckbecker,Maximilian Schall,Gerard de Melo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究使用可解释性方法分析基于ViT的大猩猩个体再识别（Re-ID）模型，发现尽管度量学习目标未提供生物学概念的显式监督，模型的表征仍沿性别和年龄等生物学有意义的方向组织，且这些方向具有因果驱动力，可揭示模型的生物学结构和失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外监控依赖相机陷阱产生大量影像，人工分析难以跟上，动物个体再识别（Re-ID）变得至关重要。然而基于ViT的Re-ID模型采用度量学习目标，缺乏对生物学概念的显式监督，因此理解模型决策依据的线索具有挑战性。作者希望探究这类模型是否仍能自发地在表征中组织出有生物学意义的结构。

Method: 使用DINOv3骨干网络，通过三元组边际损失微调用于西部低地大猩猩的个体Re-ID。采用线性探测（linear probing）在表征中寻找性别和年龄的线性方向，并在留出的个体上检验泛化能力；使用激活引导（activation steering）验证性别方向是否被模型因果性地使用；对比预训练与微调骨干网络分析特征的来源与迁移；通过数据归因（data attribution）分析表征的冗余编码和生物学梯度结构。

Result: 性别和年龄作为线性方向在表征中涌现，可泛化到未见个体，AUROC最高达0.91，且仅需每只个体一张图像即可恢复。激活引导表明性别方向被模型因果使用，能翻转大量预测至相反性别。Re-ID训练并未创造这些概念，而是将其在网络中重新定位。数据归因显示表征反映分级的生物学轴，在群体中冗余编码，并受视觉上模糊的个体塑造。

Conclusion: 这些发现表明可解释性方法能揭示Re-ID表征背后的生物学结构和失败模式，为野生动物监控中可审计的计算机视觉系统迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Conservation increasingly relies on camera traps that collect more wildlife imagery than experts can manually analyze, making animal re-identification (Re-ID) essential for monitoring individuals and populations. Yet understanding which cues drive model decisions is challenging for ViT-based Re-ID models, whose metric-learning objectives provide no explicit supervision for biological concepts. We ask whether such models nonetheless organize their representations along biologically meaningful axes. Using a DINOv3 backbone fine-tuned for Western lowland gorilla Re-ID with triplet-margin loss, we find that sex and age emerge as linear directions that generalize to held-out individuals, reaching up to 0.91 AUROC and being recoverable from a single image per individual. Activation steering further shows that the sex direction is causally used by the model, flipping a significant fraction of predictions to the opposite sex. Comparing off-the-shelf and fine-tuned backbones shows that Re-ID training does not create these concepts, but relocates them across the network. Finally, data attribution reveals that the representation we find reflects a graded biological axis, is redundantly encoded across the population and shaped by visually ambiguous individuals. Together, these findings show how interpretability can uncover both the biological structure and failure modes of Re-ID representations, providing a step toward auditable computer vision for wildlife monitoring.

</details>


### [58] [Image-Scale Robustness and Visual Recognition Performance: A Cross-Architecture Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06051)
*Anish Monsley Kirupakaran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了20个涵盖7种架构家族的预训练ImageNet-1K分类器对图像尺度变化的鲁棒性，发现尺度鲁棒性与基线识别准确率之间存在强负相关（Pearson r = -0.890，R² = 0.792，p < 10⁻⁶），而模型参数量、架构家族和表征稳定性对尺度鲁棒性的解释力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉识别模型对图像尺度变化敏感是已知现象，但不同异构架构之间支配这种敏感性的因素尚不清楚，缺乏跨架构统一的定量规律。

Method: 评估20个预训练ImageNet-1K分类器（涵盖卷积、移动、高效和Transformer等7种架构家族），系统降低输入图像尺度构建尺度-准确率响应曲线，定义特征尺度（characteristic scale）作为识别性能显著下降起点的紧凑度量，并分析其与基线准确率、参数量、架构家族及表征稳定性的关系。

Result: 基线准确率与特征尺度呈强负相关（r = -0.890），该关系在bootstrap重采样、留一架构和留一家族分析中保持稳定；参数量在控制基线准确率后无额外解释力（p = 0.80），架构家族也无显著增量解释力；特征尺度与表征稳定性几乎无关联（r = -0.003，p = 0.991）。

Conclusion: 在所研究的模型中，尺度鲁棒性主要由基线识别性能组织，而非模型大小、架构家族或表征稳定性。该研究提供了跨视觉架构表征尺度鲁棒性的经验框架，并识别出一种可复现的accuracy-scale规律，值得进一步的理论研究。

Abstract: The sensitivity of visual recognition models to changes in image scale is well established, yet the factors governing this sensitivity across heterogeneous architectures remain unclear. In this work, we investigate whether scale robustness exhibits a common quantitative structure across modern vision models. We evaluate 20 pretrained ImageNet-1K classifiers spanning seven architectural families, including convolutional, mobile, efficient, and Transformer-based architectures. By systematically reducing input image scale, we construct scale-accuracy response curves and define a characteristic scale as a compact measure of the onset of substantial recognition degradation. We then examine the relationship between characteristic scale and baseline recognition accuracy, model parameter count, architectural family, and representation stability. A strong inverse association is observed between baseline accuracy and characteristic scale (Pearson r = -0.890, R^2= 0.792, p < 10^-6). This relationship remains stable under bootstrap resampling, leave-one-architecture-out analysis, and leave-one-family-out analysis. In contrast, parameter count provides negligible additional explanatory power after controlling for baseline accuracy (p = 0.80), while architectural family does not provide significant incremental explanatory power. Furthermore, characteristic scale shows essentially no association with representation stability (r = -0.003, p = 0.991). These results indicate that, across the studied models, scale robustness is strongly organized by baseline recognition performance rather than simply by model size, architectural family, or representation stability. The study provides an empirical framework for characterizing scale robustness across vision architectures and identifies a reproducible accuracy-scale regularity that warrants further theoretical investigation.

</details>


### [59] [DriveZero: End-to-End Driving Beyond Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06055)
*Hao He,Chengcheng Hu,Zirun Su,Heng Zhang,Haisong Liu,Jinke Li,Haochen Tian,Zhenwei Shen,Hongyang Li,Zhichao Li,Yunchen Yang,Bochao Huang,Siyu Zhang,Kuangye Chen,Xiongjie Zhang,Wentao Dai,Hengchen Dai,Siyuan Liu,Zehao Huang,Naiyan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveZero是一个端到端自动驾驶系统，将驾驶任务分解为感知模型和动作模型，分别在最适合各自的数据条件下进行预训练。感知模型DriveVFM融合多个冻结视觉基础模型，动作模型DriveRL通过混合代理闭环强化学习框架训练。系统在nuPlan、NAVSIMv1/v2和HUGSIM基准上均取得领先性能，无需任何人类轨迹监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶系统过度依赖模仿人类驾驶日志，其学习行为受限于录制轨迹的质量和行为覆盖范围，无法超越人类示范的边界。

Method: 将驾驶分解为感知和动作两个子任务。(1) 感知模型DriveVFM：从原始图像中整合DINOv3、SigLIP2、SAM和Depth Anything V2等多个冻结视觉基础模型，无需任务特定标注。(2) 动作模型DriveRL：将真实驾驶日志转化为交互世界，使用PPO算法训练特权教师策略进行闭环回放。(3) 通过目标条件教师策略在增强驾驶意图下进行查询，生成多样化且目标一致的监督信号来蒸馏训练最终规划器。

Result: DriveRL在nuPlan的Val14、Test14-hard和Test14-random三个社区划分上平均得分93.57，在非反应和反应模式下均超过Log-Replay专家。DriveZero在NAVSIMv1、NAVSIMv2和闭环HUGSIM基准上达到最先进性能，无需任何人类轨迹监督。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了感知与动作模型分开预训练再统一集成的范式有效性，展示了通过闭环强化学习结合目标条件查询能够产生超越人类日志的多样化驾驶行为，为超越模仿学习的端到端自动驾驶提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Most end-to-end autonomous-driving systems learn by imitating human driving logs, leaving their learned behavior constrained by the quality and behavioral coverage of the recorded trajectories. This report presents DriveZero, an end-to-end system that learns driving behavior beyond human demonstrations. It decomposes driving into a perception model and an action model, pretrains each in the regime best suited to it, and combines them into one end-to-end planner. The two models call for different learning recipes: perception must understand the world, and benefits from massive and diverse visual data; action must interact with it, and requires closed-loop feedback. On the action side, we introduce DriveRL, a mixed-agent closed-loop reinforcement-learning framework. It converts real driving logs into interactive worlds, where a privileged teacher policy is trained with PPO through closed-loop rollouts. For the perception model, DriveVFM consolidates multiple frozen vision foundation models, including DINOv3, SigLIP2, SAM and Depth Anything V2, into a single backbone from raw images alone, requiring no task-specific annotations. DriveZero then unifies the two: a camera-only planner that distills the frozen DriveRL teacher through its rolled-out trajectories. The goal-conditioned teacher can moreover be queried under augmented driving intents, yielding diverse, goal-consistent supervision that logged data cannot provide. On nuPlan, DriveRL with value-guided test-time action search achieves a mean score of 93.57 across the Val14, Test14-hard, and Test14-random community splits in both non-reactive and reactive modes, exceeding the Log-Replay expert on all three splits. DriveZero achieves state-of-the-art performance on NAVSIMv1, NAVSIMv2 and the closed-loop HUGSIM benchmark without any human trajectory supervision.

</details>


### [60] [GradeTrap: Authority Cues in Images Shift VLM Judgments Despite Explicit Instructions to Ignore Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06058)
*Deep Dessai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GradeTrap评估框架，通过将学生答案（引发谄媚迎合）与同伴/教师/官方答案键（引发权威服从）相对立，测试视觉语言模型（VLM）在明确的"独立求解并忽略所有学生答案"指令下是否会不恰当地屈从于权威线索。结果显示官方答案键的来源会使模型改判增19.5个百分点，远高于学生答案或普通第二答案对照，说明模型在真实场景中仍难以独立评估证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着VLM在真实世界中日益被部署，模型需要独立评估证据而非不加批判地服从人类权威。现有模型往往表现出谄媚倾向和权威服从行为，但缺乏一个将两种社会线索（学生答案引发的谄媚与权威引发的服从）直接置于冲突之中的受控评估框架来量化这一问题。

Method: 引入GradeTrap受控评估：在60个合成现实场景中，将学生答案与冲突的同伴/教师/官方答案键相对立。模型被明确指示独立求解并忽略所有学生答案、反馈和评分标记。实验包含5个中性试验建立基线偏好，随后进行3轮共6种实验线索（含对照）的重复。在Gemini 3.5 Flash-Lite、GPT-5.6 Luna和Claude Haiku 4.5三个模型上测试。

Result: 在45项共同交集中，普通第二答案对照组产生5.4%的冲突答案选择率。相对于该对照，池化组内变化显示：同伴评审无可靠效应，教师评审效应为6.9个百分点，官方答案键效应高达19.5个百分点。仅展示冲突学生答案相对于仅展示学生参考答案，选择率仅从2.2%升至5.2%。三个模型间的效应幅度存在差异。

Conclusion: 尽管模型被明确指示忽略学生输入，官方答案键的来源身份仍能显著重定向模型判断（效应远超学生答案或普通对照），揭示VLM对权威来源存在深层且顽固的敏感。这一发现对模型在真实评估场景中的独立性与可靠性构成警示。

Abstract: As vision-language models (VLMs) become increasingly capable and are deployed in consequential real-world settings, they must evaluate evidence independently rather than defer uncritically to human authority. We introduce GradeTrap, a controlled evaluation that places two social cues in direct conflict: a student answer, which should attract sycophantic agreement, and a conflicting answer attributed to a peer, teacher, or official answer key, which should attract authority-based deference. Models produce free-form answers while being explicitly instructed to solve independently and ignore all student answers, feedback, and grading marks. We test the models on 60 synthetic real-world trade-off scenarios. Five neutral trials establish a stable model-relative preference, followed by three repetitions of six experimental cues including controls. On the 45-item common intersection across Gemini 3.5 Flash-Lite, GPT-5.6 Luna, and Claude Haiku 4.5, a generic second-answer control yields 5.4% conflicting-answer selection. Relative to that control, pooled within-item changes show no reliable peer-review effect, a 6.9-point teacher-review effect, and a 19.5-point official-key effect. In contrast, a displayed conflicting student answer alone compared to a displayed student reference answer alone only raises selection from 2.2% to 5.2%. Official-key provenance therefore redirects judgements more than a student answer or the generic second-answer control, despite an explicit ignore instruction and an opposing student answer given along with the official key. Effects vary in magnitude across the three models.

</details>


### [61] [NSFlow: End-to-End Differentiable Neuro-Symbolic Optical Flow for Visual Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06074)
*Yicheng Lin,Yuxiu Xu,WenDong Chen,Cong Li,Bin Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合神经符号框架，将CNN特征提取与可微Lucas-Kanade优化器结合，用于端到端训练的稀疏光流估计。该方法在保持实时运行能力的同时，较传统优化方法和纯回归方法均有精度提升，在VIO系统中平均误差降低42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于优化的光流方法（如LK）在小位移场景表现良好，但对大运动和光照变化敏感；而现代基于回归的深度学习方法虽鲁棒性强，但计算量大且缺乏显式几何一致性，不适合高效的VO/VIO前端。两者之间存在鸿沟，需要一种兼顾鲁棒性、几何一致性和计算效率的混合方案。

Method: 提出神经符号混合框架：使用CNN提取鲁棒特征表示，将其输入到可微的LK优化器中估计光流，实现端到端训练。通过隐式微分在迭代求解器间传播梯度，实现特征提取与光流估计的联合优化，可无缝集成到现有VO/VIO流水线中。

Result: 实验表明该方法在动态光照和低纹理等挑战性条件下优于传统基于优化的光流方法，同时比纯回归方法精度更高、延迟更低。在VIO系统中部署后，平均误差减少42%，并增强了跟踪稳定性，且能在嵌入式平台上实时运行。

Conclusion: CNN特征提取与可微优化的结合是有效的，能够兼顾学习的鲁棒性和优化的几何一致性，为高效VO/VIO前端提供了实用且先进的解决方案，代码已公开。

Abstract: Sparse optical flow provides stable inter-frame correspondence, playing a key role in Visual Odometry (VO) and Visual-Inertial Odometry (VIO). Classical optimization-based methods, such as Lucas-Kanade (LK), perform well under small displacements but are sensitive to large motions and illumination changes. Modern regression-based learning methods, while more robust in complex scenes, are often computationally heavy and lack explicit geometric consistency, making them less suitable for efficient VO/VIO front-ends. To bridge this gap, we propose a hybrid neuro-symbolic framework that combines the strengths of both paradigms. Our method uses a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) to extract robust feature representations, which is fed into a differentiable LK optimizer to estimate optical flow in an end-to-end trainable manner. Through implicit differentiation, gradients are propagated across the iterative solver, enabling joint optimization of feature extraction and flow estimation. The resulting system integrates seamlessly into existing VO/VIO pipelines and runs in real-time on embedded platforms. Experiments show that our method outperforms conventional optimization-based flow in challenging conditions such as dynamic lighting and low texture, while also achieving higher accuracy and lower latency than purely regression-based alternatives. When deployed in a VIO system, our method demonstrates significant performance improvement, achieving an average error reduction of 42\% on challenging datasets while enhancing tracking stability. The code is publicly available.

</details>


### [62] [Report of the 8th LSVOS Challenge: Complex and Multimodal Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06078)
*Chang Liu,Henghui Ding,Lingyi Hong,Ning Xu,Linjie Yang,Yuchen Fan,Canyang Wu,Jinrong Zhang,Xusheng He,Ce Bian,Xianjing Han,Jianlong Wu,Mingqi Gao,Sijie Li,Jungong Han,JeongRae Kim,Chaehyun Kim,Changwon Lim,Jungyoon Lee,Gyuil Lim,Doeon Kim,Seong-heum Kim,Pranjal Aggarwal,Sean Welleck,Yiwen Ren,Jianing Liu,Yingxin Wang,Kexin Zhang,Licheng Jiao,Lingling Li,Xu Liu,Jinxing Zhou,Suiyi Zhao,Yanghao Zhou,Ruohao Guo,Liangtao Shi,Jinxia Xie,Xiantao Hu,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文总结了ECCV 2026上举办的第8届大规模视频目标分割（LSVOS）挑战赛，涵盖三个互补场景：MOSEv2上的复杂半监督视频目标分割、MeViSv2-Text上的文本引导指代视频目标分割，以及MeViSv2-Audio上的音频引导指代视频目标分割。文中描述各任务与评估协议，并综述每个赛道前三名团队的方法。九个领先方案普遍将基础分割模型与目标感知记忆、多模态推理、显式目标存在性验证、智能体交互和校正跟踪相结合，展示了从单模型掩码传播向模块化管线演进的趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视频目标分割任务的日益复杂化，挑战赛需要系统性评估不同引导模态（半监督、文本、音频）下的分割性能，总结前沿方法并识别领域发展趋势，为社区提供方法论参考。

Method: 设置三个互补的评估赛道：MOSEv2上的复杂半监督VOS、MeViSv2-Text上的文本引导RVOS、MeViSv2-Audio上的音频引导RVOS；每个赛道按评估协议排名，并邀请前三名团队提交方法报告进行集中综述。

Result: 九个领先方案均采用基础分割模型与多种增强模块的组合，包括目标感知记忆、多模态推理、目标存在性验证、智能体交互及校正跟踪等策略。

Conclusion: 视频目标分割领域正从依赖单一模型的掩码传播范式，转向模块化管线架构，这类系统能够对目标身份、查询有效性及时间可靠性进行推理，体现更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: This report summarizes the 8th Large-scale Video Object Segmentation (LSVOS) Challenge, held in conjunction with ECCV 2026. The challenge evaluates video segmentation in three complementary settings: complex semi-supervised video object segmentation on MOSEv2, text-guided referring video object segmentation on MeViSv2-Text, and audio-guided referring video object segmentation on MeViSv2-Audio. We describe the tasks and evaluation protocols and review the methods of the top three teams in each track. Across the nine leading solutions, foundation segmentation models are combined with target-aware memory, multimodal reasoning, explicit target-existence verification, agentic interaction, and corrective tracking. These systems illustrate a broader transition from single-model mask propagation toward modular pipelines that reason about object identity, query validity, and temporal reliability.

</details>


### [63] [Automatic Red Teaming for Implicit Vulnerabilities of Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06094)
*Chang Ma,Junlin Han,Shuo Chen,Runjia Li,Philip Torr,Jindong Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdvPIE框架，一种无需目标模型参数访问权的多智能体对抗探测框架，用于揭露文本到图像（T2I）模型中隐式对抗提示的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红队测试难以应对隐式对抗提示，这类提示在文本表面看似无害却仍能诱导生成不当视觉内容，且比显式对抗提示更难检测和防御。

Method: AdvPIE采用多智能体框架：策略智能体负责生成和优化隐式对抗提示，裁判智能体提供跨迭代的全局与相对级别的模态特定安全评估反馈；并提出累积对抗解码（Cumulative Adversarial Decoding）策略，通过动态重新加权token分布来偏向生成更有害图像的token，同时保持采样多样性。

Result: 在标准和安全对齐的T2I模型上的大量实验表明，AdvPIE能有效揭露隐式脆弱性，性能优于多种基线方法。

Conclusion: AdvPIE为黑盒T2I模型提供了一种有效的隐式脆弱性探测方法，弥补了现有红队方法在隐式对抗提示检测方面的不足。

Abstract: Red-teaming Text-to-Image (T2I) models is essential for safe deployment, yet it remains particularly challenging against implicit adversarial prompts. Unlike explicit adversarial prompts that can be readily identified and blocked, implicit ones are much harder to detect: the prompts appear benign on the text surface yet still lead to inappropriate visual content. To address this, we propose Adversarial Probing for Implicit VulnErabilities (AdvPIE), a multimodal agentic framework to expose implicit vulnerabilities without requiring access to the parameters of target models. AdvPIE adopts a policy agent to generate and refine implicit adversarial prompts based on the feedback from a judge agent. To construct informative feedback, the judge agent provides modality-specific safety evaluation at both global and relative levels across iterations. To effectively leverage the feedback, we propose a novel Cumulative Adversarial Decoding strategy for the policy agent, which dynamically reweights token distributions to favor tokens that lead to more harmful images while preserving sampling diversity. Extensive experiments on standard and safety-aligned T2I models show that AdvPIE1 effectively uncovers implicit vulnerabilities, outperforming various baseline methods.

</details>


### [64] [PASTEL: Panoramic Alignment for Monocular 4D Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06099)
*Yuankun Yang,Yi Wei,Bo Bai,Wenyang Zhou,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PASTEL框架，通过全景场景对齐将单目视频的4D重建从可见区域扩展到不可见区域生成，实现超越相机视野的4D场景合成，并在DyCheck IPhone数据集上以0.9dB的全图像PSNR优势超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D重建方法无法恢复超出相机可见范围的区域，限制了其在VR和具身AI等应用中的能力，因此需要一种新范式来同时处理可见区域的4D重建与不可见区域的场景生成。

Method: PASTEL提出全景场景对齐表示，将不可见区域的3D探索问题转化为2D方向轨迹规划，通过将6自由度视角规划降维为2D方向搜索并利用显式可见性边界，结合生成先验策略性地规划相机轨迹，在最大化探索不可见区域的同时最小化视角偏离。

Result: PASTEL不仅能外推出输入单目视频可见边界之外合理的场景内容，还能显著提升单目4D重建性能，在DyCheck IPhone数据集上全图像PSNR比先前最优方法高0.9dB。

Conclusion: 通过将不可见区域探索转化为全景空间中的2D轨迹规划问题，PASTEL有效结合了单目可见区域重建与生成先验驱动的不可见区域外推，为4D场景合成提供了一种可行且性能优越的新范式。

Abstract: Reconstructing 4D scenes from casually captured monocular video is vital for applications in virtual reality (VR) and embodied AI. Recent advances in 4D reconstruction and novel view synthesis have substantially propelled this capability. However, existing reconstruction methods generally cannot recover regions beyond visible camera limits. Consequently, we introduce a new paradigm that achieves 4D scene synthesis by combining visible-region reconstruction from monocular input with invisible-region generation beyond observable camera boundaries. We present Panoramic Alignment for Strategic Exploitation of Generative Priors (PASTEL). Specifically, PASTEL proposes panoramic scene alignment, a novel representation that reformulates the intractable 3D "invisible region" exploration into a tractable 2D directional trajectory planning. This is achieved by reducing the viewpoint planning from 6-DoF search to a 2D directional search with explicit visibility boundaries. By operating within this panoramic space, our method strategically identifies camera trajectories that maximize exploration beyond observable boundaries while minimizing viewpoint deviation. Experimental results show that PASTEL can not only extrapolate plausible scene content beyond the observable boundaries of input monocular videos, but also substantially boost monocular 4D reconstruction performance. PASTEL outperforms the previous state-of-the-art method by 0.9dB in full-image PSNR on the DyCheck IPhone dataset.

</details>


### [65] [TBDub: Production-Oriented Visual Dubbing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06144)
*Bihan Li,Xinyang Li,Zeran Xu,Meiguang Jin,Junfeng Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TBDub是X-Dub的生产级扩展，通过任务自适应后训练和任务感知少步蒸馏，将视频扩散Transformer（DiT）模型从30步教师模型压缩为两步学生模型。在38个TalkVid片段上，教师模型在8项指标上全面超越X-Dub，学生模型达到7.13有效FPS，DiT阶段加速42.49倍，总延迟降低13.93倍，同时保持生成质量和音视频同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X-Dub虽然提供了强大的免遮罩视频编辑基线，但在直播和生成视频内容上存在生产域鲁棒性不足、时间与运动稳定性差、身份和口腔细节保真度不够、推理效率低等问题，需要更高效且适用于生产环境的视觉配音方案。

Method: 采用两阶段策略：(1)任务自适应后训练：使用生产域数据、生产特定条件和过滤、增强音频特征对视频DiT进行后训练，得到30步教师模型；(2)任务感知少步蒸馏：将DMD/DMD2适配到条件视频编辑任务，将教师模型压缩为两步学生模型。

Result: 教师模型在38个TalkVid片段上全面优于X-Dub的8项重构/感知/身份/同步指标；MOS评估中唇形同步、身份一致性和视觉质量分别提升0.14、0.95和0.90分。学生模型在H20 GPU上512x512分辨率下达到7.13有效FPS，DiT阶段加速42.49倍，总延迟降低13.93倍。

Conclusion: TBDub通过后训练与蒸馏的组合方案，在显著提升推理效率的同时保持甚至超越原有生成质量，证明了该框架在生产场景下的实用价值，且代码和模型权重均已开源。

Abstract: Visual dubbing must synchronize mouth motion with replacement speech while preserving identity, appearance, and temporal consistency. Although X-Dub provides a strong mask-free video-editing baseline, its application to livestream and generated-video content reveals limitations in production-domain robustness, temporal and motion stability, identity and oral-detail preservation, and inference efficiency. We present \textbf{TBDub}, a production-oriented extension of X-Dub that combines task-adaptive post-training with task-aware few-step distillation. Post-training adapts the video DiT using production-domain data, production-specific conditioning and filtering, and enhanced audio features to obtain a 30-step Teacher. Distillation adapts DMD/DMD2 to conditional video editing and compresses the Teacher into a two-step Student. On 38 TalkVid clips, the Teacher improves all eight reported reconstruction, perceptual, identity, and synchronization metrics over X-Dub. In the MOS evaluation, it improves lip-sync consistency, identity consistency, and visual quality over X-Dub by 0.14, 0.95, and 0.90 points, while the Student achieves the highest lip-sync and visual-quality scores and remains close to the Teacher in identity consistency. In paired end-to-end generation timing from the first VAE encode through the final VAE decode on a single NVIDIA H20 GPU at $512\times512$, the Student reaches 7.13 effective FPS and reduces total latency by $13.93\times$; the DiT stage alone is accelerated by $42.49\times$. The Student largely retains the Teacher's generation quality and audiovisual synchronization. The code is available on GitHub at \https://github.com/TaoLiveAIGC/TBDub, and the 30-step Teacher and two-step Student weights are available on Hugging Face at https://huggingface.co/TaoLiveAIGC/TBDub.

</details>


### [66] [Multiple Myeloma Lesion Segmentation on Whole-Body Diffusion-Weighted Imaging via Efficient Anatomical Anticipation and Multimodal Confirmation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06165)
*Mengmeng Zhang,Shengqian Huang,Junde Zhou,Xiaoping Wu,Hao Luog,Jing Wanga,Yicheng Sun,Jiao Li,Haibo Zhang,Sheng Xie,Fan Wangg,Qin Wangc,Huadan Xue,Yisheng Lv,Fei-yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段框架用于WB-DWI图像中的多发性骨髓瘤病灶分割：第一阶段利用ADC图像训练骨ROI生成模型，无需骨骼标注即可提供解剖先验；第二阶段提出解剖引导的多模态U-Net（AMU-Net），以符合临床病灶评估方式利用ADC信息。实验取得最佳综合性能，平均Dice分数达76.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WB-DWI广泛用于多发性骨髓瘤评估，但自动化病灶分割面临挑战：骨髓高信号特异性低且缺乏解剖结构划分。现有方法引入骨ROI和ADC图来缓解这些问题，但骨ROI构建依赖昂贵的人工标注、图像配准或专用骨骼模型，而ADC通常仅通过简单通道融合使用，未能充分发挥其结构性和病灶判别性信息。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：(1) 第一阶段从ADC图像训练骨ROI生成模型，无需专用的骨骼标注即可获得解剖先验；(2) 第二阶段提出AMU-Net（解剖引导的多模态U-Net），以与临床病灶评估方式一致的方式利用ADC信息，而非将其视为普通的辅助模态。

Result: 大量实验证明了所提方法的有效性和实用性，在所有评估方法中取得最佳综合性能，平均Dice分数达到76.2%。

Conclusion: 该工作为解决WB-DWI骨髓瘤病灶分割中解剖信息不足和ADC利用不充分的问题提供了高效实用的解决方案，通过无监督式骨ROI生成和临床一致的ADC融合策略提升了分割性能。

Abstract: Whole-body diffusion-weighted imaging (WB-DWI) is widely used for multiple myeloma (MM) assessment, yet automated lesion segmentation remains challenging due to limited anatomical delineation and the low specificity of marrow hyperintensity. Existing studies have introduced bone region-of-interest (ROI) information and apparent diffusion coefficient (ADC) maps to mitigate these ambiguities, but practical limitations remain. Bone ROI construction often relies on costly manual annotation, image registration, or dedicated bone models, while ADC is usually incorporated only through simple channel fusion, limiting its ability to provide complementary structural and lesion-discriminative cues. To address these limitations, we propose a two-stage framework for MM lesion segmentation on WB-DWI. In the first stage, we train a bone ROI generation model from ADC images without dedicated bone labels, providing an efficient and practical anatomical prior for lesion analysis.
