Even the strongest VLMs, Gemini 3.0 Flash and Qwen2.5 VL 7B, succeed on only 26.6% and 21.6% of pairs, struggling most with identifying violating and culturally benign visual behaviors. Towards bridging this, we introduce NormViz-Train, a training dataset of 64k images paired with explanations. Though absolute performance remains low (<30%), finetuning on NormViz-Train improves pair accuracy relatively by up to 125% and 36% Qwen3-VL 4B and 8B respectively, showing a path forward to teach models to connect visual perception to cultural significance. Together, NormViz-Bench and NormViz-Train establish visual norm understanding as a challenging and consequential frontier for multimodal AI.

</details>


### [169] [Learning transferable human physiology from two million hours of sleep with SleepFM-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06849)
*Rahul Thapa,Christopher Sun,William Theodor Lehn-Schioler,Sophia Claire Kivelson,Umaer Hanif,Hyatt Moore,Harrison G. Zhang,Hafsa Ahmed,Marcus Dige,Niels R. Lorenzen,Elisabeth Roxane M. Heremans,Adrien Specht,Ulysse Gimenez,Robin Guillard,Andreas Brink-Kjaer,James Zou,Emmanuel Mignot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SleepFM-2，一个基于282,511份多导睡眠监测记录（涵盖26个队列、超过两百万小时多模态生理数据）训练的睡眠基础模型。相比前代SleepFM，模型在疾病预测和睡眠评分上均有提升，支持多种睡眠事件的检测，并能迁移到可穿戴传感和主观睡眠表型。该模型在疾病预测上达到了预设的区分度和显著性标准，其表征在人口统计学信息之外提供了可复现的增量信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠期间大脑、心脏、肌肉和呼吸系统的协同活动承载着丰富的健康信息，但现有的睡眠分析工具大多局限于单一任务（如睡眠分期），缺乏能够跨疾病、跨任务、跨传感器泛化的统一基础模型。作者旨在构建一个能够捕获睡眠多模态生理信号通用表征的大规模基础模型。

Method: 作者收集并整合了来自26个队列的282,511份多导睡眠监测记录（其中235,865份用于预训练），覆盖超过两百万小时的多模态生理数据。在此大规模数据上训练SleepFM-2睡眠基础模型，并系统评估其在疾病预测、睡眠评分、睡眠事件检测以及跨传感器迁移（包括可穿戴设备与主观睡眠报告）上的表现。

Result: SleepFM-2在疾病预测和睡眠评分上优于前代SleepFM；结合年龄、性别和BMI后，在215个EHR表型预测中达到预设标准；对155个表型，PSG表征在人口统计信息之外提供了可复现的增量价值。模型在睡眠事件检测上达到专家评分者水平，成功迁移到多种可穿戴传感器（包括腕部PPG、加速度计、头部/耳内EEG等），并在六个加速度计队列中改善睡眠分期，在UK Biobank上达到与直接预训练的加速度计模型相当的疾病预测性能。此外，模型还能捕获常规PSG摘要无法反映的主观睡眠信息。

Conclusion: 多模态睡眠生理信号能够提供一种可迁移的人类健康表征，泛化于疾病预测、临床任务、多种传感器和主观睡眠体验，验证了以睡眠为窗口构建医学基础模型的可行性与巨大潜力。

Abstract: Sleep provides a nightly window into health by capturing coordinated activity across the brain, heart, muscles and respiratory system. We introduce SleepFM-2, a sleep foundation model developed and evaluated on 282,511 polysomnography recordings from 26 cohorts, including 235,865 used for pretraining. These data span more than two million hours of multimodal physiology. Compared with SleepFM, SleepFM-2 improves disease prediction and sleep scoring, supports arousal, limb movement and respiratory event detection, and transfers to wearable sensing and subjective sleep phenotypes. A model combining its PSG representation with age, sex and BMI met a prespecified discrimination and significance criterion for 215 subsequently recorded EHR phenotypes in two held-out cohorts, including one health system unseen during pretraining. For 155 phenotypes, the PSG representation added reproducible information beyond demographics. SleepFM-2 also outperformed a 480-feature baseline derived from the same recordings. Its disease scores revealed a reproducible principal component associated with reduced sigma-band spatial coupling and increased hypnodensity entropy. The frozen encoder performed within the observed range of expert scorers for sleep events and transferred to wakeful EEG, headband and in-ear EEG, wrist PPG and wrist accelerometry. It improved sleep staging across six accelerometry cohorts and achieved disease-prediction performance in UK Biobank similar to models pretrained directly on accelerometry. Finally, SleepFM-2 captured aspects of subjective sleep not recovered by conventional PSG summaries, particularly reports of the recorded night. These results show that multimodal sleep physiology can provide a transferable representation of human health across diseases, clinical tasks, sensors and subjective experience.

</details>


### [170] [A visual large language foundational model for medical image recognition using clinician-oriented social media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06914)
*Lingxuan Hou,Yuhua Xie,Yue Hu,Yan Zhuang,Junqi Li,Chengzhi Xia,Binh Phu Nguyen,Abubakar Siddique,Minh Nguyen,Yao Hou,Yanju Bao,Kexin Liu,Ke Chen,Jianjun Sun,Zeqi Li,Trung Nguyen,Jiangli Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ThoughtMed-1M，一个包含超过一百万对医学视觉问答（VQA）数据的大规模长格式数据集，通过利用社交媒体上医生分享的去标识化医学图像和专家评论，结合先进LLM与临床医生参与验证的流程构建。基于该数据集训练的FOLTMed模型在42个医学VQA基准上取得了最先进性能，宏准确率达85.4%，在事实性和相似性指标上超出SOTA模型3-5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在医学领域展现出潜力，但其应用受限于缺乏能够捕捉临床推理和显式图像-文本对齐的医学视觉问答数据集。现有数据集的稀缺阻碍了医学多模态LLM的发展。

Method: 1) 利用面向临床医生的社交媒体平台上共享的去标识化医学图像和专家评论作为数据源；2) 结合先进LLM与临床医生参与验证（clinician-in-the-loop）构建严谨的数据处理管线；3) 构建ThoughtMed-1M医学VQA数据集；4) 在该数据集上训练FOLTMed基础模型。

Result: FOLTMed在42个医学VQA基准数据集上达到最先进性能，宏准确率85.4%；在ThoughtMed-1M测试集上生成的临床响应更具一致性，在事实性和相似性指标上超过现有最先进模型3-5%。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种可扩展的范式，通过挖掘真实临床环境中医生分享的病例内容来构建大规模医学视觉问答数据集，为推进临床基础的多模态LLM研究提供了新路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities across diverse domains, showing considerable potential in medicine. However, their application in medical settings remains limited by the scarcity of visual question answering (VQA) datasets that capture clinical reasoning and explicit image-text alignment. Here, we leverage de-identified medical images and expert commentaries shared on clinician-oriented social media. By combining an advanced LLM with clinician-in-the-loop verification, we established a rigorous pipeline to construct ThoughtMed-1M, a long-form medical VQA dataset containing over one million VQA pairs and designed to capture structured clinical logic and medical image-text alignment. To demonstrate its utility, we developed a FOundational LLM Trained on ThoughtMed-1M (FOLTMed). FOLTMed achieved state-of-the-art performance across 42 medical VQA benchmark datasets, with a macro accuracy of 85.4%, and generated more clinically coherent responses on the ThoughtMed-1M test set. It outperformed state-of-the-art models by 3--5% across factuality and similarity metrics, highlighting a scalable paradigm for advancing research on clinically grounded multimodal LLMs.

</details>


### [171] [When and Why LLM Causal Priors Help: Closed-Loop Prior Selection for Amortized Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06941)
*Haohao Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个闭环先验选择框架，将LLM蒸馏因果图的先验注入形式化为预算受限的优化问题。在Do-PFN模型上，该框架选出的最优先验在主评估域上取得2.75x的显著提升，且不损害其他能力。机制实验表明增益来自图的语义内容而非结构多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果效应估计是医学、经济学和公共政策等领域的基础任务。PFN通过大量合成因果任务进行摊销式训练，但其合成训练先验目前依赖人工设计，这是Do-PFN和CausalPFN共同承认的瓶颈。虽然LLM可以生成域相关的因果图作为先验素材，但注入是否有帮助、增益来源及何时有效均依赖人工试错。

Method: 提出闭环先验选择框架：将先验注入形式化为预算受限的组合优化问题。候选先验经低成本后训练，用以真实域泛化为主的复合指标打分，胜者获得完整训练并进行配对统计验证。在7.34M参数的Do-PFN上进行实现。

Result: 框架胜出者在主评估域上取得统计显著的2.75倍增益，误差低于未注入的官方基线。相邻监测域的泛化显著改善，且所有监测能力未退化。机制实验（方向性证据）表明增益依赖于蒸馏图的语义内容，而非仅结构多样性。

Conclusion: 该框架将LLM因果先验的使用从人工试错转变为经验可验证的选择问题，为PFN先验设计提供了系统化路径。

Abstract: Causal effect estimation asks how an outcome would change under an intervention, and medicine, economics, and public policy all treat it as a foundational task. Prior-data fitted networks (PFNs) amortize the task: a model trained on large numbers of programmatically generated synthetic causal tasks reads a new problem's observational data into context and returns an interventional-effect estimate in a single forward pass. The capability of such models is largely determined by the synthetic training prior, which is currently designed by hand, a bottleneck acknowledged by both Do-PFN and CausalPFN. Large language models (LLMs) can now ``draw'' plausible causal graphs for a given domain, suggesting that LLM-distilled graphs could serve as prior material. Whether injecting such graphs helps at all, where any gain comes from, and when injection helps. Practice has so far relied on manual trial and error. We propose a \emph{closed-loop prior selection framework} that casts prior injection as a budget-constrained optimization over a candidate prior pool. Candidates undergo cheap post-training and are scored by a composite metric dominated by real-domain generalization; the winner then receives full training and paired statistical validation. On a 7.34M-parameter Do-PFN, the framework's winner attains a formally significant $2.75\times$ gain on the primary evaluation domain, and its error falls below that of the uninjected official base. Generalization on an adjacent monitoring domain improves significantly, and no monitored capability degrades. Mechanism experiments show that the gain depends on the semantic content of the distilled graph rather than its structural diversity alone does not produce it (directional evidence). With this framework and this regularity in hand, the use of LLM causal priors stops being manual trial and error and becomes an empirically verifiable selection problem.

</details>


### [172] [iBrain: A Unified Foundation Model Reading the Brain from Surface to Spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06960)
*Ying Chen,Tiou Wang,Zhifeng Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出iBrain，一个统一的基础模型，通过信号特化编码器和共享时空Transformer主干，在超过7000小时的异质侵入性神经记录（iEEG和尖峰活动）上进行联合预训练，在多个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经基础模型大多针对单一侵入性记录范式独立开发，忽略了iEEG和皮层内尖峰活动等异质侵入性神经信号之间的联合预训练潜力，限制了跨记录设置的可扩展建模和可迁移表征。

Method: iBrain采用信号特化编码器适配不同信号特征，使用共享的时空Transformer骨干建模跨通道和时间维度的依赖关系；通过掩码信号重建和通道视图对齐两种预训练任务，在超7000小时异质神经记录上进行联合预训练。

Result: iBrain在多基准上一致优于单信号预训练基线并达到最先进性能；实验进一步表明其在多种记录设置中展现出良好的可迁移性和数据效率。

Conclusion: 对异质侵入性神经记录的联合预训练具有巨大潜力，有望支持可扩展的神经建模以及跨记录设置和下游任务的迁移表征。

Abstract: Invasive neural recordings provide high-fidelity measurements of brain activity, with signals such as intracranial EEG (iEEG) and intracortical spiking activity capturing neural dynamics at different spatial and temporal scales. Yet existing neural foundation models have largely been developed independently for different invasive recording paradigms, leaving joint pretraining across heterogeneous invasive signals underexplored. In this work, we introduce iBrain, a unified foundation model that jointly learns from iEEG and spiking activity. iBrain employs signal-specific encoders to accommodate their distinct signal characteristics and a shared spatiotemporal Transformer backbone to model dependencies across recording channels and time. We pretrain iBrain on over 7,000 hours of heterogeneous neural recordings using masked signal reconstruction and channel-view alignment, promoting contextual modeling of neural dynamics and robustness across different channels. iBrain consistently outperforms single-signal pretraining baselines and achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple benchmarks. Further experiments demonstrate that iBrain exhibits transferability and data efficiency across diverse recording settings. These results highlight the potential of joint pretraining on heterogeneous invasive neural recordings to support scalable neural modeling and transferable representations across recording settings and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [173] [SSP-DMGTimeNet: Physics-Constrained Learning for Spatiotemporal Trajectory Prediction of Vehicle Platoons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06961)
*Yuhang Wang,Kailang Ma,Zirui Li,Mingfeng Fan,Kitae Jang,Changju Lee,Heye Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SSP-DMGTimeNet，一种物理约束的车辆队列时空轨迹预测框架，通过传播延迟感知的因果注意力机制和时频域弦稳定性损失，在保持轨迹预测精度的同时确保扰动传播的稳定性，在HighD、NGSIM等数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车辆跟驰预测方法主要优化轨迹精度，很少考虑预测扰动是否沿队列真实传播，可能导致预测"精准但不稳定"（string-unstable）的问题。作者旨在同时兼顾轨迹精度和扰动传播稳定性。

Method: 1) 多尺度时间表示与跨车辆交互特征结合，捕捉复杂时变队列动力学；2) 传播延迟感知的因果注意力机制，显式建模上游到下游的扰动传播，学习相邻车辆间响应延迟并沿队列累积；3) 引入时域和频域的弦稳定性（string-stability）损失，在训练中抑制相邻车辆及任意子队列的扰动放大。

Result: 在HighD数据集上，五车队列的不稳定窗口率仅为0.65%，真实激励子集的最大首尾放大系数为0.898，同时保持有竞争力的轨迹预测性能。在NGSIM US-101和I-80上的零样本评估中，速度MAE分别为1.316 m/s和1.252 m/s，不稳定窗口率分别为3.90%和4.10%。

Conclusion: 在预测框架中引入队列级物理约束可以有效平衡轨迹预测精度与扰动传播稳定性，验证了物理约束与深度学习方法结合在车辆队列预测中的价值。

Abstract: Existing car-following prediction methods mainly optimize trajectory accuracy, while rarely considering whether predicted disturbances propagate realistically along a vehicle platoon. This limitation may lead to accurate but string-unstable predictions. We propose SSP-DMGTimeNet, a physics-constrained learning framework for spatiotemporal trajectory prediction of vehicle platoons. The model combines multi-scale temporal representations with cross-vehicle interaction features to capture complex and time-varying platoon dynamics. A propagation-delay-aware causal attention mechanism explicitly models upstream-to-downstream disturbance propagation by learning response delays between adjacent vehicles and accumulating them along the platoon. In addition, time- and frequency-domain string-stability losses relieve disturbance amplification across both adjacent vehicles and arbitrary sub-platoons during training. Experiments on HighD show that SSP-DMGTimeNet achieves an unstable-window rate of 0.65\% for five-vehicle platoons and a maximum head-to-tail amplification of 0.898 on the ground-truth excitation subset, while maintaining competitive trajectory prediction performance. In zero-shot evaluation on NGSIM US-101 and I-80, the model achieves velocity MAEs of 1.316~m/s and 1.252~m/s, with unstable-window rates of 3.90\% and 4.10\%, respectively. These results demonstrate that incorporating platoon-level physical constraints can effectively balance trajectory prediction accuracy and disturbance propagation stability.

</details>


### [174] [RedKnot-MLA: Multi-Head Offline-Online Reuse for DeepSeek-V4 Long-Context Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07008)
*Yang Liu,Zhaokai Luo,Huayi Jin,Ruozhou He,Chenchen Hong,Mingxiao Ma,Biao Zhang,Zhiyong Wang,Boyu Wang,Guanjie Chen,Yifei Liu,Tao Xie,Junhao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个DeepSeek-V4系统，实现了RedKnot的"头感知复用"原则。系统将不可变文档离线处理，保留Local-head贡献为MLA-Off；服务时通过RoPE位置修复恢复请求位置，仅重算少量Global-head与受保护的Local token行（MLA-Online），两条路径合并后共享输出投影，且从不拆分打包的MLA潜在表示。DeepSeek-V4-Flash配置（37层复用、56/8 Local/Global划分）达到75.29%分析性逻辑头行上限；Pro-0813配置（55层、112/16头）达到78.89%。冷启动Flash操作点在热工件TTFT上取得2.02-3.84倍加速。256K下归档三数据集研究报告聚合F1提升+3.24个百分点、EM提升+4.16个百分点、分析性主要算子算术节省78.7-79.5%，但一个数据集F1下降2.81点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多头潜在注意力（MLA）通过单一的打包潜在KV流暴露多个逻辑查询头，这种表示虽然内存高效，但移除了传统逐头复用所依赖的物理每头缓存边界。这为文档级的头级缓存复用带来了挑战，需要新的设计方案来在保持MLA内存高效性的同时实现头感知的复用优化。

Method: 系统采用双路径架构：离线阶段将不可变文档在规范位置零处理，保留认证的Local-head贡献为MLA-Off；服务时通过查询侧RoPE位置重定位恢复文档请求位置，仅对少量Global-head和受保护Local token行进行在线重算（MLA-Online），最后合并两条路径并通过单一共享输出投影。整体MLA潜在表示从不拆分。文中描述了分解、位置修复、token行闭包、稀疏MoE支持、TP8集成等关键设计。提供了两种配置：DeepSeek-V4-Flash（37个可复用层、56/8 Local/Global划分）和Pro-0813（55层、112/16头）。

Result: Flash配置达到75.29%的分析性逻辑头行上限，Pro-0813配置达到78.89%。冻结Flash操作点在热工件TTFT上实现2.02-3.84倍加速。256K归档研究显示聚合F1提升+3.24个百分点、EM提升+4.16个百分点、分析性主要算子算术节省78.7-79.5%，但有一个数据集F1下降2.81点。另有作者报告的256K热工件QPS约2.0x，因缺乏原始并发追踪痕迹被标记为初步结果而非归档证据。

Conclusion: 该方案展示了在不拆分打包MLA潜在向量的前提下实现头级复用的可行路径，通过Local/Global头划分和离线-在线双路径机制获得显著的服务加速与算术节省。论文明确区分了归档证据与初步测量结果，界定了结果解释所需的测量边界。

Abstract: Multi-head latent attention (MLA) exposes many logical query heads through one packed latent KV stream. This representation is memory efficient, but it removes the physical per-head cache boundary assumed by conventional head-wise reuse. We present our system, a DeepSeek-V4 realization of RedKnot's head-aware reuse principle. Each immutable document is processed offline at canonical position zero; certified Local-head contributions are retained as MLA-Off. At serving time, query-side RoPE relocation restores the document's request position, a small Global-head set and protected Local token rows are recomputed as MLA-Online, and the two paths are merged before a single shared output projection. The packed MLA latent is never split. DeepSeek-V4-Flash uses 37 reusable layers and a 56/8 Local/Global partition, giving a 75.29% analytic logical head-row ceiling; the Pro-0813 profile uses 55 layers and 112/16 heads, giving 78.89%. Frozen Flash operating points show hot-artifact TTFT speedups of 2.02-3.84x. At 256K, the archived three-dataset study reports an aggregate F1 change of +3.24 percentage points, an EM change of +4.16 points, and a 78.7-79.5% analytic major-operator arithmetic saving, while one dataset decreases by 2.81 F1 points. A separate author-reported 256K hot-artifact QPS measurement is approximately 2.0x; because its raw concurrency trace is not included in this bundle, we mark it as preliminary rather than archived evidence. We describe the factorization, position repair, token-row closure, sparse-MoE support, TP8 integration, and the measurement boundaries needed to interpret these results.

</details>


### [175] [Beyond One-Shot Expansion: Contrastive Evidence Exploration for Multi-Hop Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07050)
*JungMin Yun,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个无需训练的检索增强生成（RAG）多跳检索框架，该框架结合证据条件探索、段落特定对比精炼和覆盖感知最终排序，通过迭代检索与对比语义信号来提升多跳问答中的证据检索质量及下游问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答中的检索高度依赖逐步揭示经由中间实体和关系连接的支撑段落，但现有方法依赖于单一检索意图或一次性查询扩展，难以适应新检索到的证据，并可能引入噪声或冗余信号。

Method: 提出一个无需训练的多跳检索框架，包含三个核心模块：1）离线索引阶段构建段落特定的对比面（contrastive facets），刻画每个段落与其语义相似邻居的细微差异；2）推理阶段迭代检索证据并生成针对未解决信息需求的探针（probes）；3）利用对比面精炼候选段落相关性，并通过覆盖感知的最终排序选择互补的段落集合以覆盖多样的证据寻求意图。

Result: 在MuSiQue、HotpotQA和2WikiMultihopQA三个多跳问答数据集上，所提方法在检索质量和下游问答性能上均持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 证据条件探索、对比精炼与覆盖感知排序相结合的策略，能够有效解决多跳检索中证据逐步发现和候选区分的问题，在不依赖额外训练的前提下显著提升RAG系统的多跳检索与推理表现。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) critically depends on retrieving the evidence necessary for effective reasoning. However, this remains particularly challenging in multi-hop question answering (QA), where supporting passages are often linked through intermediate entities and relations that must be progressively uncovered. Existing retrieval approaches typically rely on a single retrieval intent or one-shot query expansion, limiting their ability to adapt to newly retrieved evidence and potentially introducing noisy or redundant retrieval signals. To address these limitations, we propose a training-free multi-hop retrieval framework that integrates evidence-conditioned exploration, passage-specific contrastive refinement, and coverage-aware final ranking. During offline indexing, the framework constructs passage-specific contrastive facets that characterize each passage relative to its semantically similar neighbors, providing fine-grained signals to distinguish closely related candidates. At inference time, the framework iteratively retrieves evidence, generates probes targeting unresolved information needs, refines candidate relevance using the contrastive facets, and selects a complementary set of passages that collectively cover diverse evidence-seeking intents. Experiments on MuSiQue, HotpotQA, and 2WikiMultihopQA demonstrate consistent improvements in retrieval quality and downstream QA performance over baselines.

</details>


### [176] [VST: Verifiable Structured Transport for Auditable Agent-to-Agent Alpha Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07065)
*Yuqi Li,Siyuan Liu,Bingjun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM多智能体系统中智能体间的自由文本通信重构为结构化A2A协议（类型化、可因果寻址的unicast记录），使提交流形成因果轨迹；在此基础上，一个带四个类型化头的预测器预测若干周期后的累积指引，并由事务性verify-leap控制器决策是否提交多周期投机结果。结构化的核心价值并非提升准确率（消融显示与自由文本命中率相同），而是提供可架构检查、确定性回放、且由构造上防止泄漏的审计性。CSI 1000样本外测试中，该方法是八种方法中唯一在因子层面保持正的中位年化收益和Sharpe的方法，但其基准超额中位收益仍为负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代LLM多智能体系统中，智能体间以自由形式自然语言消息传递信息，缺乏稳定契约且不可重放，严重拖慢了A2A（agent-to-agent）阿尔法发现的反复反馈循环过程。

Method: 首先将智能体间通信重构为结构化A2A协议（类型化、因果可寻址、单播记录），使提交流构成因果轨迹；然后在该轨迹上用一个带四个类型化头的预测器预测数周期后矿工将收到的累积指引；最后由事务性verify-leap控制器在通过四层门控时提交多周期投机结果，否则回滚到精确的先前状态。整个系统按构造保证可审计性（可架构检查、确定性重放、防止预测泄漏给评估器），而非靠经验压力测试。

Result: 消融对照显示，同等信息的自由文本通道能达到相同的预测器命中率（即结构化不提升准确率）。在CSI 1000样本外数据上，该方法是八种方法中唯一在因子层面保持正的中位年化收益和Sharpe的方法；其开发选择的前20组合在优化期内分割上达到0.71的中位样本外Sharpe。但其基准超额的中位收益对包括本方法在内的所有方法仍为负。

Conclusion: 结构化是使能性的贡献，其价值不在于准确率而在于由构造保证的可审计性。作者将该研究描述为单一运行的描述性报告（未计成本），明确承认其局限性，特别是未将leap机制的效果与继承的搜索基座分离开来，这留待未来工作。基准超额收益为负也表明方法的绝对alpha能力尚存疑问。

Abstract: Agent-to-agent (A2A) alpha discovery is slowed by repeated feedback cycles between mining and evaluation agents, whose hand-offs, in contemporary LLM multi-agent systems, are free-form natural-language messages that carry no stable contract and cannot be replayed. We first restructure this communication as a structured agent-to-agent protocol of \emph{typed, causally addressable, unicast records}, so that the committed stream forms a causal trajectory. On that trajectory a single predictor with four typed heads forecasts the accumulated guidance the two miners would receive several cycles ahead; a transactional verify--leap controller then commits a multi-cycle speculative outcome only when it passes a four-level gate, and otherwise rolls back to the exact prior state. Structure is the enabling contribution, and its value is not accuracy. A controlled ablation shows an equal-information free-text channel reaches the same predictor hit rate. What typing provides is a state that can be schema-checked, replayed deterministically, and prevented by construction from leaking a forecast to an evaluator: auditability by construction, not an empirically stress-tested guarantee. On a CSI~1000 out-of-sample holdout, our single run is the only one among eight methods (seven baselines and ours) to hold a positive median annualized return and Sharpe at the factor level, though the median return \emph{in excess} of the benchmark stays negative for every method including ours; its development-selected top-20 portfolios reach a $0.71$ median holdout Sharpe, selected on a split inside the optimization horizon. We report these single-run results descriptively, gross of costs, and are explicit about their limits throughout; in particular we do not isolate the effect of the leap machinery from the inherited search substrate, which we leave to future work.

</details>


### [177] [A Hierarchical Consistency Framework for Auditing Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07075)
*Ramon Gonzalez,Antonio Diaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出分层一致性框架（HCF），用于事后审计RAG流程的语料库、检索上下文和生成答案三个层级，将矛盾证据以源链接原子事实的形式呈现，并输出带解释的一致性分数（ACS）。实验在五个领域、100个查询-语料实例上进行，发现三个诊断层级可解耦，且HCF能在答案与参考答案匹配时识别出检索上下文中的矛盾证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG评估仅看最终答案是否正确，这不够充分——答案可以与参考答案匹配，但其生成所依据的上下文可能包含直接矛盾，这些争议性证据在仅审查答案或检索相关性分数时是不可见的。

Method: HCF是一种事后、模型无关的审计框架，对RAG的三个层级（知识语料库、最终检索上下文、生成答案）分别进行一致性诊断。将语料库冲突表示为带来源链接的原子事实以定位责任文档，并为每个生成答案返回带解释的答案一致性分数（ACS），说明支持性与矛盾性的上下文陈述。

Result: 在五个领域、100个查询-语料实例的多个受控语料库上，三个诊断层级可解耦：平均检索相似度最高的语料库其平均ACS最低，而结构性退化的语料库在语料层级表现更差但在答案层级表现更好。在多个答案仍与参考答案匹配的案例中，HCF识别出了检索上下文中的矛盾证据。

Conclusion: HCF并不认证事实真伪，其价值在于使支持和质疑某个答案的证据变得可检查、可归因，为RAG评估提供超越简单答案匹配的透明审计手段。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is commonly evaluated by whether the final answer is correct. That test is insufficient: an answer can match its reference while the context that produced it contains a direct contradiction, leaving the contested evidence invisible to answer-only review and retrieval relevance scores. This paper presents the Hierarchical Consistency Framework (HCF), a post-hoc, model-agnostic audit of three distinct levels of a RAG process: the knowledge corpus, the final retrieved context, and the generated answer. HCF represents corpus conflicts as source-linked atomic facts, thereby identifying the documents responsible, and returns each Answer Consistency Score (ACS) with an explanation of supporting and contradictory contextual statements. We evaluate HCF on several controlled corpora spanning five domains and 100 query-corpus instances. A human evaluator compares every generated response with its supplied ground-truth response. The results show that the three diagnostic levels can dissociate: the corpus with the highest mean retrieval similarity has the lowest mean ACS, while a structurally degraded corpus performs worse at corpus level but better at answer level. Most importantly, HCF identifies contradictory retrieved evidence in several cases where the answer still matches the ground truth. HCF does not certify factual truth; it makes the evidence supporting and challenging an answer inspectable and attributable.

</details>


### [178] [Risk Is Not Review Value: Wrong-Answer Exposure Under Bounded Review Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07095)
*SangJin Park,Myungsub Choi,Jineok Kim,Minseung Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM助手在有限审查预算下的答案审查优先级问题，提出将审查价值建模为"暴露减少"（结合错误概率、可修复性、影响和成本），并引入两个新指标WAER和PRRE来衡量审查队列质量。实验表明，考虑可修复性的排序在保持错误检测能力的同时，显著降低了修复后的残余暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的答案数量远超人类可审查的范围，现有评估大多关注答案是否正确、有无依据或置信度高低，但在固定审查预算下，需要确定哪些答案应优先被审查。仅依据风险排序不够充分，因为高风险答案可能难以修复，而中等风险答案可能根据已有证据直接纠正。

Method: 将审查优先级问题建模为暴露减少问题，审查价值综合考量估计错误率、干预可行性（affordance）、影响范围和成本四个因素。提出两个评估指标：WAER（错误答案暴露比）衡量未审查的错误答案比例；PRRE（修复后残余暴露）衡量经过确定性基准支持修复后仍暴露的错误比例。PRRE使用独立的可修复性规则，不直接复用在排序阶段使用的affordance分数。

Result: 在包含720个条目的TAT-QA/SciFact压力测试基准上，基于审查价值排序在20%预算下保持了与风险排序几乎相同的答案级WAER（0.605 vs 0.600），但将PRRE从0.881降至0.716，大幅改善了修复后的暴露情况。

Conclusion: 可信赖的LLM评估不仅应衡量错误检测能力，还应衡量有限审查能力如何有效减少暴露给用户的错误答案。考虑可修复性的审查价值排序能在不牺牲错误检测的前提下显著降低残余错误暴露，为高可靠LLM系统部署提供了实用指导。

Abstract: LLM assistants often produce more answers than humans can review before users see them. Most evaluations ask whether an answer is wrong, unsupported, or low-confidence. Bounded review budgets instead ask which answers should be checked first under a fixed review budget. Risk alone is not enough: a high-risk answer may be hard to repair, while a moderately risky answer may be directly correctable from available evidence. For generated-answer evaluation, we model review prioritization as exposure reduction, where review value combines estimated wrongness, intervention affordance, impact, and cost. We evaluate review queues with Wrong-Answer Exposure Ratio (WAER), the fraction of wrong answers left unreviewed, and post-repair residual exposure (PRRE), the fraction still exposed after deterministic benchmark-supported repairs. PRRE uses repairability rules that do not numerically reuse the affordance scores used for ranking. On a 720-item TAT-QA/SciFact stress benchmark, review-value ranking keeps answer-level WAER nearly unchanged at 20% budget (0.605 vs. 0.600) but lowers PRRE from 0.881 to 0.716. These results show that trustworthy LLM evaluation should measure not only error detection, but also how limited review capacity reduces exposed wrong answers.

</details>


### [179] [Beyond Sparse Rewards: A New Benchmark and Structure-Aware Graph Alignment for Micro-Drama Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07107)
*Yixin Qin,Shi-Zhe Chen,Zhiqi Yu,Siyuan Cheng,Tao Cheng,Jinwen Luo,Zheng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了M-Drama，首个大规模中英双语微短剧理解基准（含35K+实例、9,138个片段），并提出了SAGA（结构感知图对齐）奖励函数，通过将叙事建模为异构图进行语义三元组与结构时序匹配，有效提升VLM对微短剧中复杂角色和时序结构的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微短剧具有超短时长和超高密度情节的特点，对视频理解构成独特挑战，现有基准无法覆盖。同时，强化学习虽可增强VLM对复杂叙事的理解，但现有奖励指标常受限于稀疏和浅层信号，无法捕捉复杂的角色身份与时序结构。

Method: 1) 构建M-Drama，大规模双语微短剧理解基准；2) 提出SAGA奖励函数，将叙事建模为异构图，通过解耦的语义三元组匹配与结构时序匹配，计算密集且严格的奖励。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上的实验表明，SAGA优于现有基线，在开放式问答准确率和摘要质量上均取得显著提升，同时保持了具有竞争力的跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: SAGA通过结构感知的图对齐奖励，能有效应对微短剧中高密度的叙事理解挑战，为VLM的复杂视频叙事理解提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Micro-dramas, characterized by ultra-short durations and hyper-dense storylines, pose unique challenges for video understanding that conventional benchmarks fail to address. To bridge this gap, we introduce M-Drama, the first large-scale bilingual benchmark for micro-drama comprehension, featuring over 35K instances across 9,138 clips. Furthermore, while reinforcement learning can enhance VLMs on complex narratives, existing reward metrics often suffer from sparse and superficial signals, failing to capture intricate character identities and temporal structures. We propose SAGA (Structure-Aware Graph Alignment), a novel graph-matching reward function that models narratives as heterogeneous graphs. SAGA computes dense, rigorous rewards via decoupled semantic triplet and structural temporal matching. Extensive experiments on Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct demonstrate that SAGA outperforms existing baselines, delivering substantial improvements in open-ended accuracy and summary quality, while maintaining competitive out-of-domain generalization. Code is available at https://github.com/qyx1121/MDrama_SAGA.

</details>


### [180] [EEG-Driven Decoding Framework for Passenger Hazard Perception in Highly Automated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07128)
*Yingkai Yang,Ashton Yu Xuan Tan,Bowen Li,Xiaorong Gao,Sifa Zheng,Jianqiang Wang,Xinyu Gu,Yang Zhao,Yuxin Zhang,Sharon X. Huang,Tania Stathaki,Jun Li,Hong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种基于脑电（EEG）的脑机接口（BCI）系统，用于自动驾驶（AV）乘客的风险预测（RP）与危险识别（DI），通过3D-CRNN模型结合风险感知序列标注（RSL）等方法，在RP任务中达到95.3%的平衡准确率，并展现出良好的跨会话与跨被试泛化能力，为未来AV决策和SOTIF提供辅助监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆的安全风险评估仍是核心挑战，尽管自动化技术不断进步，乘客认知信号作为一种非侵入式的辅助信号，无需主动人工干预即可提升客观与感知安全性。现有研究鲜有以"乘客"身份建模人类认知来匹配真实AV使用场景，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出了三个核心组件：乘客认知模型（PCM）用于建模乘客认知过程；风险感知序列标注（RSL）用于增强序列风险评估；乘客EEG解码策略（PEDS）集成3D卷积循环神经网络（3D-CRNN）进行联合EEG解码，实现风险预测与危险识别两个任务。

Result: 3D-CRNN在风险预测中达到95.3%±2.7%的平衡准确率；危险识别在引入RSL后从80.9%±3.9%提升至85.0%±3.2%。跨会话泛化实验中达到77.0%±5.3%的平衡准确率，跨被试实验中已见被试为77.4%±1.1%，未见被试为64.9%±8.5%，展示了良好的泛化与迁移能力。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个面向AV乘客危险感知的EEG解码框架，证明了乘客认知信号能够为未来自动驾驶决策提供辅助监督支持，并有望在SOTIF（预期功能安全）方面提供支持。

Abstract: Reliable risk assessment remains a central challenge for Autonomous Vehicles (AVs). Despite advances in automation, passenger cognition provides a non-intrusive auxiliary signal that improves both objective and perceived safety without requiring active human intervention. We introduce an Electroencephalogram (EEG)-based Brain-Computer Interface (BCI) that decodes passenger neural responses for both Risk Prediction (RP) and Danger Identification (DI), explicitly modeling humans as passengers to match real-world AV use. To achieve this, we propose the Passenger Cognitive Model (PCM), Risk-aware Sequential Labeling (RSL), and the Passenger EEG Decoding Strategy (PEDS), which integrates a 3D Convolutional Recurrent Neural Network (3D-CRNN) model for joint EEG decoding. Experimental results show that 3D-CRNN achieves a Balanced Accuracy (BA) of $95.3\% \pm 2.7\%$ in RP and improves single-subject DI from $80.9\% \pm 3.9\%$ to $85.0\% \pm 3.2\%$ with RSL. Event-wise analyses further show that 3D-CRNN consistently outperforms other models across different event types in RP and DI. In generalization experiments, 3D-CRNN achieves $77.0\% \pm 5.3\%$ BA in cross-session DI and $77.4\% \pm 1.1\%$ BA on seen subjects in cross-subject evaluation, while maintaining a $64.9\% \pm 8.5\%$ BA on unseen subjects, demonstrating promising generalizability and transferability across both intra-subject and inter-subject variability. These findings establish an Electroencephalogram (EEG) decoding framework for AV passenger hazard perception and suggest that passenger cognitive signals can provide auxiliary supervision for future AV decision-making and Safety of the Intended Functionality (SOTIF) support.

</details>


### [181] [Encoded Early, Used Late: Where Transformers Begin to Act on an Inferred Partner's Expertise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07139)
*Mika Okamoto,Gabriele Sarti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探究在残差流中，模型对于需要逐步推断的属性（对话伙伴的专业水平）是否也存在"信息可解码"与"信息被因果使用"之间的鸿沟。实验表明，伙伴专业水平在早期层最易解码，到网络中部前降至接近随机；因果修补显示在峰值层注入差异几乎不影响后期读出，而穿过中点后注入则几乎完全传播。推断型关系属性在被因果激活之前就已很好地被表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究已证明，对于直接陈述在输入中的属性，Transformer中存在信息可读深度与实际使用深度之间的间隙。但尚不清楚对于模型需要在多轮对话中逐步推断的隐含属性（如对话伙伴的专业水平），这种间隙是否同样成立。理解这一间隙对于下游的可解释性与干预（读取或操控伙伴条件行为）具有重要指导意义。

Method: 使用ExpertCollab语料库（由模型扮演四种专业水平玩家的多轮科研规划对话）训练/测试，通过线性探针（linear probe）检测各层残差流中伙伴专业水平的可解码性；使用反事实修补（counterfactual patching）在峰值可解码层与中点后的层分别注入专家水平差异，观察对固定后期读出器的影响；并辅以内容匹配的随机对照实验与无探针诊断方法验证。

Result: 伙伴专业水平在早期层具有最高可解码性，在网络中点之前降至接近随机水平。在峰值可解码层注入专业水平差异仅导致后期读出几乎不变，而在中点之后注入相同差异则几乎完全传播，两者相差一个数量级以上。内容匹配的随机对照与无探针诊断均将转变点定位在同一早期层；而静态指定属性的对照组在整个网络中保持可解码。

Conclusion: 推断型关系属性在更深的层才被因果使用，但其表征在更浅的层就已经形成，即"读得到"远早于"用得着"。该间隙界定了任何尝试读取或操控伙伴条件化行为的干预必须作用的网络层范围。该研究为初步演示，仅使用单一模型与合成语料库。

Abstract: A transformer can make an attribute linearly decodable in its residual stream at a depth where that attribute does not yet influence the output. This gap between where information is readable and where it is used has been shown for attributes stated directly in the input. We ask whether it also holds for an attribute the model must infer gradually over a conversation, namely how expert its dialogue partner is. Using ExpertCollab, a corpus of multi-turn research-planning dialogues between model-played personas at four expertise levels, we find that partner expertise is most decodable in the early layers and falls to near chance before the midpoint of the network. Counterfactual patching shows that injecting the expertise difference at the layer of peak decodability barely changes a fixed late-layer readout, whereas the same difference injected past the midpoint propagates almost completely, a separation of more than an order of magnitude. A content-matched random control and a probe-free diagnostic place the transition at the same early layer, and a statically specified control attribute stays decodable throughout. An inferred relational attribute is therefore represented well before it becomes causally active, which bounds where any attempt to read out or steer partner-conditioned behavior must intervene. We use one model on a synthetic corpus as an initial demonstration.

</details>


### [182] [An Auditable Symbolic-RAG-Generative AI Architecture for Goal-Oriented Conversation Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07152)
*Ramon Gonzalez,Antonio Diaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于符号化RAG与生成式架构的目标导向对话系统——GRACE，该系统通过业务目标编译器将商业意图转化为不可变目标集，在运行中依据访客提供的证据更新状态，并在满足用户效用约束的前提下选择最大化业务进展的追问。在24个英语房地产和10个西班牙语专业清洁对话场景（共119个协议定义访客回合）中，GRACE实现了84.9%的状态转换准确率、91.6%的证据精确率、89.6%的证据召回率、100%的单调性和94.1%的终态准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标导向的对话系统需要在回答问题、理解访客信息与推进业务目标之间取得平衡，避免沦为僵化的问卷式交互。现有系统在这三方面往往难以兼顾，因此需要一种架构能够正式化状态管理、证据约束与策略优化，同时保持对话的自然性和业务效率。

Method: 提出Symbolic-RAG-Generative三层架构：核心为GRACE引擎。业务目标编译器将商业意图转化为不可变目标集、归一化优先级向量、规范问题和初始状态向量。运行时GRACE接收完整对话历史、最新访客消息、当前状态及独立RAG组件生成的接地回答，仅依据访客明确提供的证据更新完成度，并以最小访客效用约束下的期望业务进展最大化作为策略目标。文中正式定义了状态、单调转换、源隔离、问题调制和约束策略，并给出参考架构和包含多场景的评估方案。

Result: 在英语房地产和西班牙语专业清洁两个领域共34个对话中，GRACE在标准、多目标、RAG绕行、验证、拒绝和鲁棒性场景下取得：84.9%精确状态转换准确率、91.6%证据精确率、89.6%证据召回率、100%单调性和94.1%终态准确率，验证了符号化状态性能的有效性。

Conclusion: GRACE的符号化状态管理与策略优化在跨语言、跨领域的多类对话场景中展现了可靠的性能，为面向目标的一体化对话系统提供了有说服力的架构参考，但其实际部署效果仍需更大规模与更多样化场景的进一步验证。

Abstract: Goal-oriented conversational systems must answer factual questions, understand visitor-provided information, and advance business objectives without becoming rigid questionnaires. This paper proposes a Symbolic-RAG-Generative architecture centered on the Goal-oriented Retrieval-Augmented Conversation Engine (GRACE). An instruction-constrained Business Goal Compiler transforms business intent into an immutable objective set, normalized priority vector, canonical questions, and initial state vector. At runtime, GRACE receives the complete conversation history, latest visitor message, current state, and grounded answer generated by a separate RAG component. It updates completion only from visitor-authored evidence and selects one contextually modulated follow-up. The core policy maximizes expected business progress subject to a minimum visitor-utility constraint. We formalize the state, monotonic transitions, source separation, question modulation, and constrained policy; present the reference architecture; and define an evaluation comprising 24 English real-estate and 10 Spanish professional-cleaning conversations, totaling 119 protocol-defined visitor turns. Across both domains, GRACE achieves 84.9% exact state-transition accuracy, 91.6% evidence precision, 89.6% evidence recall, 100% monotonicity, and 94.1% terminal-state accuracy. The evaluation establishes compelling symbolic-state performance across standard, multi-goal, RAG-detour, validation, refusal, and robustness scenarios.

</details>


### [183] [PhysMAS: Physics-Grounded Multi-Agent Synthesis of Compositional 4D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07174)
*Jiang Qin,Chunji Lv,Yangguang Wei,Yang Gao,Ming Liu,Lizhong Ding,Ye Yuan,Yinjie Lei,Changsheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysMAS，一个物理驱动的多智能体框架，用于高效生成物理合理且语义对齐的4D高斯动态场景，支持异构多部件物体和交互式多物体场景，无需逐场景扩散分数反向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于物理的4D高斯生成方法（结合MPM）在处理异构多部件物体和交互式多物体场景时存在挑战：物体级物理分配会将不同部件合并为单一材料状态；基于LLM/VLM/智能体的一次性预测无法可靠地将不同材料绑定到已识别的部件，也无法验证MPM配置的可执行性；基于SDS的参数优化需要逐场景的分数评估和梯度反向传播，导致优化时间长且可能产生次优或不稳定的解。

Method: PhysMAS是一个物理基元的多智能体框架。从运动提示和四个场景视图出发，Object-Part Scene Agent建立持久身份并调用Material Reasoning Agent进行逐部件材料分析。框架调用求解器感知技能，将身份和材料配置绑定到逐粒子的MPM场中，并在共享域中执行所有物体，最后筛选候选前向仿真结果。

Result: 广泛实验表明，与依赖SDS的最新基于物理的4D高斯基线相比，PhysMAS在语义对齐和感知物理合理性方面表现更好，同时所需运行时间更少。

Conclusion: PhysMAS通过多智能体协作和求解器感知技能绑定，成功解决了异构多部件和交互式多物体4D动态场景的物理生成问题，在无需逐场景扩散分数反向传播的情况下实现了更高的语义对齐度、物理合理性和运行效率。

Abstract: Efficient, fully automatic, and physically plausible 4D Gaussian synthesis is an important goal for dynamic scene generation. Recent physics-based methods couple 3D Gaussians with the Material Point Method (MPM) to generate physically driven motion, but extending this paradigm to heterogeneous multi-part objects and interacting multi-object scenes remains challenging. Object-level physical assignment collapses distinct parts into a single material state, while one-shot predictions from large language models, vision-language models, or agents neither reliably bind different materials to identified parts nor verify that the resulting MPM configuration is executable. Score Distillation Sampling (SDS)-based parameter optimization, meanwhile, requires repeated per-scene score evaluations and gradient backpropagation, incurring lengthy optimization and potentially yielding suboptimal or unstable solutions. We therefore present PhysMAS, a physics-grounded multi-agent framework. From a motion prompt and four scene views, an Object-Part Scene Agent establishes persistent identities and calls a Material Reasoning Agent for part-wise profiles. It invokes solver-aware skills to bind these identities and profiles to per-particle MPM fields and execute all objects in a shared domain; the framework then screens candidate forward-simulation results. This supports heterogeneous multi-part and interacting multi-object scenes without per-scene diffusion-score backpropagation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, compared with recent physics-based 4D Gaussian baselines that rely on SDS, PhysMAS achieves better semantic alignment and perceived physical plausibility while requiring less runtime.

</details>


### [184] [EmoMed: An Emotionally-Aware Agent for Multimodal Medical Support with Real-Time Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07194)
*Ivan Nasonov,Nikita Glazkov,Ivan Makovetskiy,Mikhail Mozikov,Daniil Sukhorukov,Andrey Savchenko,Ilya Makarov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了EmoMed——一个多模态医疗咨询智能体，能够根据用户的情绪状态（如焦虑、困惑、紧迫感）动态调整回复的语气、结构和详细程度，同时通过双通道检索机制（网络事实核查+持续更新的API医学知识库）保证临床准确性。在七个先进大语言模型上评估并经过用户研究验证，情感自适应回复在各项指标上均优于中性基线，且不影响临床准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统医疗咨询系统往往只关注信息的准确性，忽视了用户的情感需求。然而在实际医疗场景中，患者常伴随焦虑、困惑等负面情绪，这可能影响他们对医疗信息的理解和信任。因此需要一种能够在保持临床准确性的同时，根据用户情绪状态进行适应性回应的医疗咨询智能体。

Method: EmoMed系统采用了多模态输入处理（文本+医学图像），首先检测用户输入中的情感指标（焦虑、困惑、紧迫感），然后据此动态调整回复的语气、结构与信息详细程度。为保证事实可靠性，系统使用双检索机制：网络事实核查与持续更新的API连接医学知识库。评估覆盖七个大语言模型，采用LLM-as-judge、MedQA风格准确率测试、BERT Score、安全/帮助性评分及多模态医学基准等多种指标。

Result: 实验结果表明，情感自适应回复在所有评估维度上一致优于中性基线，且没有损害临床准确性。受控用户研究进一步证实了这一发现，参与者报告提升了感知共情能力和沟通清晰度，同时保持了对事实准确性的信任。

Conclusion: EmoMed证明了医疗咨询智能体可以在不牺牲临床准确性的前提下，通过情感自适应机制显著改善用户体验。多模态情感识别与双通道事实核查架构为医疗AI助手提供了一种可行且有效的设计范式，为未来更具人文关怀的智能医疗系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present EmoMed - a multimodal medical consultation agent that adapts its responses based on users' emotional states while maintaining clinical accuracy. The system processes text and medical images, detects affect indicators (anxiety, confusion, urgency) from user input, and adjusts response tone, structure, and detail level accordingly. To ensure factual reliability, the agent grounds clinical information through a dual retrieval mechanism: web-based fact-checking and an API-connected, continuously updated medical knowledge base. We evaluate our approach across seven state-of-the-art language models (GPT-4/5, Qwen3, Llama 4, Gemini 2.5, Grok4, Claude3) using comprehensive metrics including LLM-as-judge assessments, MedQA style accuracy tests, BERT Score, safety/helpfulness ratings, and multimodal medical benchmarks. The results demonstrate that emotionally adaptive responses consistently outperform neutral baseline across evaluation dimensions, without compromising clinical accuracy. A controlled user study validated these findings, with participants reporting improved perceived empathy and communication clarity, while maintaining trust in factual accuracy. Source code: https://github.com/NasonovIvan/EmoMed-Agent

</details>


### [185] [Agentic Algorithm Engineering: Improving Shared-Memory Exact Minimum Cuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07204)
*David A. Bader,Adil Chhabra,Ernestine Großmann,Monika Henzinger,Alexander Noe,Christian Schulz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为"智能体算法工程"(AAE)的方法论，利用自主大语言模型智能体对现有代码库执行算法工程循环。作者将此方法应用于其已有的最小割算法VieCut上，尽管该算法已由人工精心调优，智能体仍然发现了显著的优化，在真实世界k-core实例上实现了1.28倍（串行）和1.63倍（32线程）的加速，在DIMACS核心实例上实现了6.26倍和127倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小割问题的高效求解具有重要意义，作者此前已开发了最先进的VieCut算法，但希望进一步突破人工调优的瓶颈，探索如何利用AI智能体自动化地进行算法工程优化。

Method: 作者提出了智能体算法工程(AAE)方法论：自主的大语言模型智能体运行算法工程循环——形成关于运行时间损失所在的假设、实现这些假设、在固定实例集上评估结果并决定保留或丢弃改动。该方法应用于现有的VieCut最小割求解器代码库。

Result: 智能体在已充分人工调优的算法上仍发现了显著优化：在真实世界k-core实例上取得1.28倍（串行）和1.63倍（32线程）加速；在DIMACS核心实例上取得6.26倍（串行）和127倍（32线程）加速。

Conclusion: 即使算法已经经过大量人工调优，自主LLM智能体的AAE方法论仍能发现人类未察觉的优化空间，证明了基于大语言模型的智能体算法工程在算法优化领域的巨大潜力。

Abstract: The minimum cut problem for an undirected edge-weighted graph asks us to divide its set of nodes into two blocks while minimizing the weighted sum of the cut edges. Over the last years, we engineered a range of fast algorithms for this problem. Our fastest exact algorithm uses an inexact algorithm to obtain a better bound for the problem, reductions that depend on this bound, improved data structures and parallel contraction routines. It is available in the open-source package VieCut and, on real-world instances, outperformed the previously fastest solvers by a factor of up to 2.5 sequentially and up to 12.9 when run in parallel. We improve this algorithm using agentic algorithm engineering (AAE), a methodology that we introduce here, in which autonomous large language model agents run the algorithm engineering cycle on an existing code base: they form hypotheses about where running time is lost, implement them, benchmark the result on a fixed instance set and keep or discard the change. Even though we had already tuned our algorithm by hand extensively, the agent finds significant optimizations, in particular on the DIMACS core instances: factors of 1.28 (sequential) and 1.63 (32 threads) on real-world k-cores, and 6.26 and 127 on the DIMACS core instances.

</details>


### [186] [Unraveling the Real Working Mechanism and Inherent Flaws of GAE: A Method for Interpreting Transformer Processes from an Economic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07213)
*Yongjin Cui,Xiaohui Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前可解释人工智能（XAI）领域被代理指标导向的研究范式所主导，导致XAI方法本身缺乏可解释性（即XXAI）。论文以Generic Attention-model Explainability（GAE）这一代表性的注意力过程解释方法为研究对象，揭示其真实工作机制与缺陷，并从经济零和博弈视角提出了一种融合过程与特征思想的更合理的Transformer解释方法——Cumulative Asset Holdings（CAH）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前XAI研究过度追求代理指标的性能提升，但这些指标本身存在缺陷，无法准确衡量方法质量，且指标导向的研究忽视了对方法本身合理性与可解释性的关注，因此需要对XAI方法本身进行可解释性研究（即XXAI）。

Method: 本文采用模型简化研究方法与加法操作分析，以GAE方法为研究对象，深入剖析其工作机制与缺陷；在此基础上，从经济学的零和博弈视角出发，提出融合过程导向与特征导向思想的CAH方法，用于对包含特殊token的Transformer模型进行更合理的解释。

Result: 研究揭示了GAE方法的真实工作机制及其固有缺陷，指出其所属技术路线可能存在问题，需要对GAE相关方法及其领域应用进行重新审视与验证；所提出的CAH方法能够克服现有方法在含特殊token模型上的局限性。

Conclusion: XAI方法本身的解释性（XXAI）研究具有重要意义，GAE相关方法需要被重新检验；本文提出的CAH方法以及所采用的模型简化研究和加法操作分析方法，可为其他XAI研究工作提供启发。

Abstract: We observe a phenomenon that current algorithmic research in the field of explainable artificial intelligence primarily pursues better performance on several proxy metrics. On the one hand, these proxy metrics themselves are more or less flawed and cannot properly measure the quality of methods. On the other hand, metric-oriented research approaches often lead to the neglect of the rationality and interpretability of the methods themselves. Explainable artificial intelligence is abbreviated as XAI. The metric-driven research paradigm has resulted in a lack of interpretability of the relevant XAI methods themselves. Accordingly, there is a need for interpretability research on XAI methods, which can be playfully referred to as XXAI. This paper is one of our works on XXAI. This paper takes Generic Attention-model Explainability (GAE), a widely influential model interpretation method , or rather, XAI method that represents an important technical route, as the research object, and explores the real working mechanism and flaws of this method as well as the technical route it represents. Based on the conclusions of this study, it may be necessary to re-examine or verify GAE-related methods and their domain applications. We argue that GAE is an interpretation method that focuses on the attention process. After pointing out the working mechanism and flaws of GAE, we propose Cumulative Asset Holdings (CAH), a more reasonable Transformer interpretation method integrating both process-based and feature-based ideas from an economic zero-sum games perspective. In addition, it is worth noting that our method is applicable to models with special tokens, where existing methods may suffer from limitations. The model simplification research method and the analysis of additive operations adopted in this study may provide inspiration for other research works in XAI.

</details>


### [187] [Distance-Aware Attention and Wall-Distance Expert Routing for Transformer-Based 3D Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07222)
*Sanghyeon Kim,Sunwoong Yang,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两种不依赖特定架构的物理感知条件化机制——距离感知交叉注意力（DA-CA）和表面-体积混合专家（SVMoE），用于改进3D流场预测Transformer模型。DA-CA根据点到壁面的距离重塑查询向量，SVMoE根据壁面距离（体积点）或局部几何（表面点）路由专家网络。在DrivAerML和DrivAerNet++数据集上，该方法显著降低了体积压力和速度预测误差，并在小样本（50例）和不同车身类型泛化场景下均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer替代模型进行3D流场预测时，将工业网格压缩为少量token后，检索（cross-attention）和前馈层对查询点所处的流场位置（如边界层内部还是外部自由流）不敏感，缺乏物理条件约束，导致预测精度受限。

Method: 提出两种条件化机制：(1) DA-CA（距离感知交叉注意力）：在检索前用壁面距离重塑每个体积点的查询向量，使边界层内的点与外部流场的点获取不同的几何信息；(2) SVMoE（表面-体积混合专家）：用少量按壁面距离（体积点）或局部几何（表面点）路由的专家替代共享前馈层。两种机制均可直接应用于AB-UPT和Transolver-3等不同架构。

Result: 在DrivAerML的50个训练样本上，DA-CA使体积压力误差降低10.1%，两者联合降低12.5%；扩展到300个训练样本后，AB-UPT的体积压力和速度误差分别降低33.1%和18.6%，Transolver-3分别降低21.4%和21.3%。在DrivAerNet++的留一车身类型评估中，对未见车身类型的体积压力误差最多降低14.2%。

Conclusion: 通过将壁面相关的物理信号注入检索和前馈阶段，Transformer代理模型能够更好地感知流场中的空间物理位置，显著提升预测精度和跨域泛化能力，且方法具有架构无关性，可广泛适用。

Abstract: Transformer surrogates for 3D flow prediction compress an industrial mesh into a small set of tokens from which every prediction point reads. Two operations follow: the retrieval step in which a point gathers information from the compressed representation, and the feed-forward layer that transforms what it retrieved. In current backbones both are blind to where the point sits in the flow. We condition both on wall-related physical signals. Distance-aware cross-attention (DA-CA) reshapes each volume query by its wall distance before retrieval, so that a point deep in the boundary layer draws different geometric information than one in the outer flow. Surface-volume mixture-of-experts (SVMoE) replaces the shared feed-forward layer with a small set of experts, routed by wall distance for volume points and by local geometry for surface points. Neither mechanism is tied to one architecture, so we apply both unchanged to AB-UPT and Transolver-3. On DrivAerML with 50 training cases, DA-CA reduces the volume pressure error by 10.1%, and DA-CA and SVMoE together reduce it by 12.5%; DA-CA improves the near-wall region at some cost in the far region, which SVMoE recovers, and the volume experts settle into near-wall, transition, and free-stream bands without routing supervision. Retrained on 300 cases, the conditioning improves every field quantity, reducing volume pressure and velocity errors by 33.1% and 18.6% on AB-UPT and by 21.4% and 21.3% on Transolver-3. Under Leave-One-Body-Out evaluation on DrivAerNet++, it reduces the volume pressure error on unseen body types by up to 14.2%.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [MVWeaver: A Hierarchical Music Video Generation Agent with a Learned Song-to-Visual Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06478)
*Sifei Li,Minyan Luo,Xu Li,Guodong Qi,Xincan Wang,Hanwen Wang,Chen Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Oliver Deussen,Weiming Dong*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出MVWeaver，一个音乐视频生成智能体，通过分层规划与学习到的"歌-视觉桥梁"将歌曲理解转化为可执行的镜头计划，从而提升音乐视频生成的长期连贯性与歌曲驱动的视觉发展。实验表明其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动音乐视频生成系统虽能生成视觉上合理的镜头，但往往缺乏长时连贯性，且无法生成与歌曲内容深度绑定的视觉发展。核心挑战在于如何让LLM具备音乐视频特有的"歌曲到视觉"映射知识。

Method: MVWeaver架构包含三个部分：(1) 综合歌曲分析模块；(2) 构建分层计划的视觉规划器；(3) 下游图像与视频生成模型。关键创新是学习歌曲分析到视觉规划之间的"桥梁"——从真实MV中提取1,861对歌曲-MV数据，配以结构化标注（歌曲侧、MV侧及教师推断的歌曲到视觉推理标注），通过LoRA监督微调LLM来预测引导分层视觉规划的桥梁。

Result: 实验证明MVWeaver在歌曲驱动的视觉转换、更丰富的视觉发展以及概念和镜头间连贯性方面均优于现有方法；消融实验支持了学习到的桥梁条件化的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将"歌曲到视觉"的先验知识以监督微调形式注入通用LLM，并将其与分层视觉规划相结合，可以显著提升AI音乐视频生成的质量与连贯性。

Abstract: Music videos are an important form of audiovisual expression in contemporary culture. They translate and extend the expressive content of songs through deliberate visual design. Existing automatic music video (MV) generation systems can generate visually plausible shots, yet often struggle with long-form coherence and song-grounded visual development. We present MVWeaver, a music video generation agent that integrates hierarchical planning with a learned song-to-visual bridge that translates song understanding into executable shot plans. The MVWeaver architecture comprises a comprehensive song analysis module, a visual planner that constructs hierarchical plans, and downstream image and video generation models that render the planned content. To equip a general-purpose LLM with MV-specific song-to-visual knowledge, we learn a bridge between song analysis and visual planning from real-MV-derived supervision and curate 1,861 real-world song--MV pairs with structured song-side, MV-side, and teacher-inferred song-to-visual rationale annotations. Using these annotations, we perform LoRA-based supervised fine-tuning (SFT) of a large language model to predict song-to-visual bridges that guide hierarchical visual planning. Our experiments demonstrate stronger song-grounded visual translation, richer visual development, and greater conceptual and shot-to-shot coherence, while ablations support the benefits of learned bridge conditioning.

</details>


### [189] [Vision-Guided Text Prompt Tuning for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06497)
*Xiaoran Kou,Jingyi Wu,Peng Sun,Yang Liu,Hong Chen*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出Vision-Guided Text Prompt Tuning (VG-TPT)，通过可学习的分层自适应提示将视觉情感线索注入冻结的BERT文本编码器中，实现视觉引导的文本表征可控校准，仅更新240万参数即可在多模态情感分析任务上取得竞争性或更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感分析需要有效建模文本语义和非语言情感线索，但盲目融合视觉信息可能引入噪声并扭曲文本语义；同时大规模视觉和文本编码器的全量微调成本高且容易在有限且场景依赖的MSA基准上过拟合。现有方法缺乏可控、自适应且参数高效的视觉文本融合方式。

Method: 提出VG-TPT，将视觉-文本情感建模转化为对冻结文本表征的可控视觉校准。具体方法包括：(1) 通过逐层自适应提示（layer-wise adaptive prompts）将视觉情感线索注入冻结的BERT编码器，而非晚期特征融合或全量骨干网络微调；(2) 设计共引导路由器（co-guided router），根据演化中的文本状态和视觉引导特征，从可训练提示库中组合提示，实现样本特定和层特定的调制。

Result: 在CMU-MOSEI和CMU-MOSI基准上的实验表明，VG-TPT一致优于纯文本基线，并且与多种全模态方法相比达到竞争性或更优的性能，同时仅更新240万个可训练参数。

Conclusion: VG-TPT证明在冻结编码器上通过视觉引导的分层提示调优是一种有效的参数高效多模态情感分析方法，能够以极低的参数成本实现可控的视觉校准，避免视觉噪声对文本语义的干扰。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis requires effective modeling of both verbal semantics and non-verbal affective cues. A central challenge is to calibrate text-centered sentiment understanding with visual facial evidence in a controlled, adaptive, and parameter-efficient manner. Text usually serves as the semantic anchor, whereas visual cues provide complementary evidence for ambiguous or implicit expressions; however, indiscriminate fusion may introduce visual noise and distort textual semantics. Moreover, fully fine-tuning large visual and textual encoders is costly and prone to overfitting on limited and scenario-dependent MSA benchmarks. To address these issues, we propose Vision-Guided Text Prompt Tuning (VG-TPT), which formulates visual-text sentiment modeling as controllable visual calibration of frozen text representations. VG-TPT injects visual affective cues into a frozen BERT encoder through layer-wise adaptive prompts, rather than relying on late-stage feature fusion or full backbone tuning. A co-guided router composes prompts from a trainable prompt bank according to both the evolving text state and the visual guidance feature, enabling sample-specific and layer-specific modulation. Experiments on CMU-MOSEI and CMU-MOSI show that VG-TPT consistently improves over text-only baselines and achieves competitive or superior performance compared with several full-modality methods, while updating only 2.4M trainable parameters. The code is available at https://github.com/ma-tubu/VG-TPT.

</details>


### [190] [AdoDAS: A Privacy-Preserving Multimodal Challenge for Adolescent Depression, Anxiety, and Stress Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07038)
*Zhaojie Luo,Junkun Wang,Tianhua Qi,Yuxuan Wu,Xin Zhao,Tetsuya Takiguchi,Tomoko Matsui,Kun Qian,Fei Wang,Shuqiong Wu,Zhengjun Yue,Hiroshi Ishiguro,Xinyuan Qian,Haizhou Li*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AdoDAS大挑战赛，该挑战针对青少年抑郁、焦虑和压力（D/A/S）筛查，在隐私保护政策下使用6,000名参与者的24,000个音视频及文本片段，提供两个赛道（多任务二分类筛查和21项DASS-21条目序数预测），最终95个筛查团队和64个条目预测团队进入排行榜，最优结果分别达到0.5921的平均F1和0.2776的平均二次加权Kappa。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青少年抑郁、焦虑和压力问题需要可扩展的自动化筛查工具，这类工具应补充而非取代专业评估；同时必须保护未成年人的原始录音等隐私数据，因此需要一个在隐私保护框架下兼顾有效性与安全性的基准挑战。

Method: 在隐私保护政策下，赛事不公开未成年人原始录音，仅提供匿名化音视频表征和ASR文本。数据涵盖6,000名参与者的24,000个片段（一个朗读任务和三个开放式对话任务）。设置两个赛道：多任务二分类D/A/S筛查和DASS-21量表的21个条目序数预测任务。从191个注册团队中筛选出95个合格筛查团队和64个条目预测团队进入最终排行榜。

Result: 音视频基线达到0.4604的平均F1和0.2675的平均二次加权Kappa；排行榜领先提交分别达到0.5921和0.2776。优胜系统的共同特点包括跨会话建模、时间多模态融合、利用心理测量结构以及任务感知校准。

Conclusion: AdoDAS挑战赛证明了在严格的隐私保护限制下开展青少年心理状态自动化评估的可行性，领先系统的技术思路（跨会话建模、多模态融合、心理测量结构利用和任务感知校准）为未来青少年心理健康自动化筛查研究提供了有价值的参考方向。

Abstract: Adolescent depression, anxiety, and stress (D/A/S) call for scalable tools that complement, rather than replace, professional evaluation. Under a privacy-preserving policy, the AdoDAS Grand Challenge withholds minors' raw recordings and distributes anonymized audio-visual representations and ASR-derived text. Its 6,000 participants provide 24,000 segments across one scripted-reading and three open-response sessions. Two tracks assess multi-task binary D/A/S screening and ordinal prediction of 21 DASS-21 item responses. From 191 registrations, the final leaderboards included 95 eligible screening teams and 64 item-prediction teams. Audio-visual baselines achieved 0.4604 mean F1 and 0.2675 mean Quadratic Weighted Kappa; leading submissions reached 0.5921 and 0.2776. Representative systems emphasize cross-session modelling, temporal multimodal fusion, psychometric structure, and task-aware calibration.

</details>


### [191] [Can Agents Win the Video Browser Showdown?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07311)
*Bastian Jäckl,Zuzana Vopálková,Daniel A. Keim,Jakub Lokoč*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 该论文研究了现代视觉语言模型（VLM）与智能体方法能否完全自主地完成传统上需要用户交互的大规模视频检索任务，实验表明智能体系统仅凭初始意图描述即可在许多搜索任务上达到与历史专家操作系统相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视频检索通常是一个需要用户全程参与的交互式过程，用户需要扮演搜索意图持有者和查询执行者两种角色。作者希望探究是否仅凭初始的搜索目标描述，智能体系统就能自主完成整个交互式检索流程，减少人工参与。



Method: 作者将现有交互式视频搜索系统与VLM智能体相结合：搜索系统负责视频数据的索引和高效查询，而基于VLM的智能体则分析检索结果的排名列表并自主决策下一步操作（如优化查询、浏览候选等），从而以完全自主的方式模拟人类用户的交互搜索循环。

Result: 结果表明，现代智能体能够自主操作交互式视频检索系统，仅凭初始意图描述即可解决许多搜索任务，在多种设置下取得了与历史专家操控系统相当甚至具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 这项工作证明了VLM智能体在自主视频检索方面的潜力，即交互式搜索中的用户角色可以被智能体取代，为未来全自动视频搜索系统的发展提供了可行性依据。

Abstract: Searching large video collections is typically an interactive process in which users play two roles. First, they hold the search intent: the underlying goal that determines what content they seek and why. Second, users must operationalize this intent through an iterative search loop. Users translate their intent into queries, browse the retrieved candidates, and refine their queries based on the results. In this paper, we investigate the capabilities of modern Vision Language Models (VLM) and agentic approaches to reach search goals interactively and fully autonomously. Specifically, we study whether a provided initial specification of a search goal might be sufficient to solve traditionally interactive search tasks with an agentic system. Provided that the involved VLMs are not aware of the whole large video dataset in advance, the key challenge lies in the effective combination of an existing interactive video search system and a smart VLM agent controlling the system. While the search system provides indexing and efficient querying, the VLM-based agents analyze top-ranked items and make decisions about next actions. Our results show that modern agents can autonomously operate interactive video retrieval systems to solve many search tasks from an initial intent description, achieving performance competitive with strong historical expert-operated systems in several settings.

</details>


### [192] [Concept-Level Risk and Calibration for Governance in Diffusion Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08517)
*Kun Xu,Yushu Zhang,Tao Wang,Shuren Qi,Barbara Carminati,Elena Ferrari,Yuming Fang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出CLRC（概念级概率审计与报告框架），用于统一评估扩散模型的安全与治理相关概念行为。将治理相关概念建模为伯努利语义事件，定义概念风险算子（Concept Risk Operator）将模型-通道配置映射为结构化风险画像，支持跨提示接口、嵌入通道、模型和部署条件的系统比较。实验证明嵌入访问和混淆提示常低估标准评估的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着扩散模型的语义控制从自然语言提示扩展到学习嵌入和干预流水线，不同条件通道下的安全与治理评估缺乏统一方法。现有研究依赖启发式审计、对抗探测或任务特定的擦除基准，无法支持跨模型、跨条件通道和部署条件的系统性比较。

Method: 将治理相关的概念行为形式化为随机生成所诱导的伯努利语义事件，定义概念风险算子将模型-通道配置映射到结构化风险画像。应用样本级后验校准和配置级风险聚合，支持跨提示接口、嵌入通道、模型和记录条件的比较。提出池化多协议校准器提升保留集的概率可靠性。

Result: 在SD1.5、SD2.1和SDXL上的实验显示概念族、通道、记录条件和转移协议之间存在一致但非均匀的操作风险模式。嵌入通道访问和混淆提示暴露的风险往往被标准提示评估低估。池化多协议校准器能改善保留集概率可靠性，但标准校准器不能直接迁移。

Conclusion: CLRC为多媒体生成系统提供了概率性和决策感知治理的统一审计模式，概率误差在策略边界附近可能改变阈值化决策，需谨慎处理校准与风险评估。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become a core paradigm for multimedia generation, offering powerful concept-driven controllability for personalization, semantic editing, and selective unlearning. However, as semantic control extends beyond natural-language prompts to learned embeddings and intervention pipelines, the safety and governance of these systems become increasingly difficult to evaluate in a unified manner, especially for safety-sensitive, identity-linked, and other privacy-relevant concepts. Existing studies mainly rely on heuristic audits, adversarial probing, or task-specific erasure benchmarks, and therefore provide limited support for systematic comparison across models, conditioning channels, and deployment conditions. We present a concept-level probabilistic audit and reporting framework for diffusion models. We formalize governance-relevant concept behaviors as Bernoulli semantic events induced by stochastic generation, and define a Concept Risk Operator that maps model-channel configurations to structured risk profiles, enabling comparison across prompting interfaces, learned embedding channels, models, and recorded conditions. We apply sample-level post-hoc calibration and configuration-level risk aggregation, and show that probability error can change thresholded actions near policy boundaries. Experiments on SD1.5, SD2.1, and SDXL reveal consistent yet non-uniform operational risk patterns across concept families, channels, recorded conditions, and shifted protocols. In particular, embedding-based access and obfuscated prompts expose risks often understated by standard-prompt evaluation. A pooled multi-protocol calibrator improves held-out probability reliability, but we do not claim transfer from a standard-only calibrator. CLRC provides a common audit schema for probabilistic and decision-aware governance of multimedia generation systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [AhaBench: Do Agents Learn from Prior Experience? A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05435)
*Zerui Cheng,Jiawei Xu,Huacan Chai,Jiayang Sun,Pramod Viswanath,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AhaBench是一个用于评估语言智能体"经验学习"能力的基准测试套件，包含三个组件（Aha-Puzzle、Aha-Euler、Aha-Vending），通过"初始分数"、"体验后分数"和"学习提升"三维度评分卡来衡量固定模型在收到有用经验后是否能在相关但移除/改变/延迟支持的条件下的行为改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言智能体评估大多在提示后重置智能体或仅评分离散轨迹的最终状态，无法回答核心操作性问题：固定模型在获得有用经验后，是否能在相关评估条件下（支持被移除、改变或延迟时）有所改进。这一缺失阻碍了对智能体长期学习能力的有效衡量。

Method: 构建三组件基准套件：Aha-Puzzle测试解决隐藏状态谜题后的无提示探索；Aha-Euler将Project-Euler式数学思想转为生成的教学/留出任务并使用精确验证器；Aha-Vending通过开源模拟器测试智能体在延迟反馈和运营事故下的盈利能力。采用三分评分卡（初始分数、体验后分数、学习提升之差）进行分解评估。

Result: 在八模型面板上，Claude Opus 4.6在总体验后分数（64.3）和学习提升（+25.8）上领先，Gemini 3.1 Pro以63.4紧随其后。组件结果显示：谜题轨迹提高受支持分数但难以转化为无提示探索行为；Aha-Euler完整教学达78.6-100.0%而仅答案迁移仅为0.0-73.9%；Aha-Vending有效区分了盈利性事故处理与破产/无订单失败。

Conclusion: 关键发现是：善用可见支持的模型、达到高体验后分数的模型、以及运行期间提升最大的模型并非总是同一批模型。AhaBench通过三维评分卡揭示了这种分解，并发布了完整的基准任务、评分标准、验证器、模拟器代码和评估接口供社区使用。

Abstract: Modern language agents are expected to operate over long horizons: they ask follow-up questions, reuse worked examples, handle tool feedback, and adapt to delayed consequences. Most evaluations still reset the agent after a prompt or score only the final state of one trajectory. AhaBench asks a more operational question: when a fixed model receives useful experience, does its later behavior improve under a related evaluation condition where the obvious support has been removed, changed, or delayed? The suite contains three components. Aha-Puzzle tests no-hint exploration after solved hidden-state puzzles; Aha-Euler turns Project-Euler-style mathematical ideas into generated taught/held-out tasks with exact validators; and Aha-Vending, an open-source implementation inspired by Vending-Bench, tests whether a simulated vending agent remains profitable while handling delayed feedback and operational incidents. AhaBench reports a three-part scorecard: Initial Score measures starting competence, Post-Experience Score measures the later empirical outcome, and Learning Lift is their difference. This decomposition is the main empirical message: models that use visible support well, models that reach high post-experience scores, and models that improve most during a run are not always the same. On the common eight-model panel, Claude Opus 4.6 leads aggregate Post-Experience Score at 64.3 and aggregate Learning Lift at +25.8, with Gemini 3.1 Pro close behind at 63.4. The component results explain the split: puzzle traces raise supported scores but often fail to become no-hint exploration behavior; Aha-Euler full teaching reaches 78.6-100.0% while answer-only transfer ranges from 0.0 to 73.9%; and Aha-Vending separates profitable incident handling from bankruptcy and no-order failure. We release benchmark tasks, rubrics, validators, simulator code, and interfaces for evaluating new agents.

</details>


### [194] [When Do Options Help? Policy Necrosis and Redundant Coverage in Option-Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05508)
*Bingyun Liu,Yuheng Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论和实验解释option-critic算法中"增加option数量可提升性能"的结论。作者发现其终止规则本质上等同于每步终止，内部策略几乎不探索导致"策略坏死"现象，且额外option并不直接改善单个option，而是降低了所有option在同一状态同时失败的概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Option-critic算法声称增加option数量可以提升性能，但这一结论缺乏深入解释。作者希望揭示其背后的真正机理，而不是简单接受表面结论。

Method: 通过理论分析（证明终止规则的冗余性、构造遗憾上界对比）和实验验证（状态级坏死检测、option数量变化实验），系统拆解option-critic的工作机制。

Result: 1) 终止规则学到的内容与每步强制终止无异，当测试与策略读取相同值时每步触发，且在某些场景下导致Ω(T)遗憾而每步终止仅O(log T)；2) option内部策略几乎不探索，导致"策略坏死"，实测典型option中五分之三状态坏死；3) 增加option将"所有option在同一状态同时失败"的概率从59%降至4%，性能提升源于此联合失败率而非单个option的改进。

Conclusion: Option-critic的性能提升并非来自option结构本身的学习优势，而是源于多样化的失败概率分摊。恢复内部探索后单个option即可解决问题，挑战了"更多option必然更好"的直觉认知。

Abstract: Option-critic learns options: sub-policies together with a learned rule for when each one hands control back. Its headline result is that performance improves as options are added. We explain that result, with theory and experiment. First, the termination rule option-critic learns by maximising return contributes nothing. When the termination test and the policy that picks options read the same values, the test fires at every step, so the learned rule is identical to always terminating. When that policy explores and the test does not, as in option-critic itself, the rule can block the exploration; there are instances where it suffers $Ω(T)$ regret while always terminating holds to $O(\log T)$. Forcing termination at every step leaves the option-count curve intact. Second, the policy inside an option barely explores at all, so a state locks onto the first action that looked good and never updates again. We name this policy necrosis, give a state-level test for it, and find three fifths of states necrotic in a typical option. Restoring exploration repairs those states, and one option then solves the task. Third, extra options improve no option; what falls is the chance that all of them fail in the same state, from $59\\%$ to $4\\%$, and performance follows that joint quantity.

</details>


### [195] [Multi-granularity Adaptive Hypergraph Representation Learning via Granular-ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05574)
*Sen Zhao,Yifan Guan,Jinyuan Ni,Gaojie Xu,Zhang Xu,Xiaoyu Lian,Yi Liu,Yi Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MGHRL框架，通过自适应粒度球分裂策略在多个粒度级别生成超边，并用多粒度超图网络集成不同粒度的特征，从而有效捕获图数据中的高阶关系信息，在基准数据集上显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超图表示学习方法大多依赖预定义规则生成超边，忽略了图拓扑结构的多样性以及超边的多粒度特性，导致无法有效且自适应地发现高阶关系和处理复杂结构信息。

Method: 提出MGHRL框架，包含两大核心组件：(1) 自适应粒度超图生成策略，通过粒度球的自适应分裂在多个粒度级别生成超边，基于图的拓扑结构捕获高阶关系；(2) 多粒度超图网络，由多个子网络组成，捕获不同粒度超边的特征并通过分层可逆连接进行特征融合。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验结果表明，MGHRL在性能上显著优于现有基线模型。

Conclusion: MGHRL通过自适应多粒度超边生成与多粒度子网络集成，有效解决了传统方法在超边定义和粒度多样性方面的局限，为超图表示学习提供了一种更灵活有效的新框架。

Abstract: Hypergraph representation learning aims to capture high-order information in graphs by constructing hyperedges that simultaneously connect multiple nodes. These hyperedges adapt to the graph's topological features, facilitating the extraction of high-order relationships at multiple granularities. Most prior work relies on predefined definitions to generate hyperedges, overlooking the diversity in graph topological structures and the multi-granularity characteristics of hyperedges. As a result, this limits their ability to effectively and adaptively discover high-order relationships and efficiently process complex structural information. To address this limitation, we propose a novel framework called \underline{M}ulti-\underline{G}ranularity \underline{H}ypergraph \underline{R}epresentation \underline{L}earning (MGHRL). MGHRL introduces an Adaptive Granular Hypergraph Generation strategy, which generates hyperedges at multiple levels of granularity through the adaptive splitting of granular-ball, effectively capturing high-order relationships based on the graph's topological structure. Additionally, we propose a Multi-Granularity Hypergraph Network with multiple sub-networks, capturing features from hyperedges at different granularities and integrating them via hierarchical reversible connections. Experimental results show that MGHRL significantly outperforms baseline models on benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [196] [Capsule Lens: Locating and Tracking Concept Geometry in Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05575)
*Yiming Tang,Harshvardhan Saini,Samyak Jha,Huaming Chen,Xufeng Duan,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Capsule Lens 框架，用胶囊（capsule）这一简单可追踪的几何形式来匹配概念在模型表示中所占据的区域，通过闭式拟合与留出验证实现概念几何的定位与分析，并同时适用于静态和动态两种表示场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解机器学习模型内部表示中概念如何编码，是机械可解释性的核心问题，对深度学习的科学理解和可信部署都至关重要。现有方法大多将表示映射到更可解释的空间，未直接刻画概念如何占据表示空间；已有假设缺乏严格验证且多聚焦于静态表示。

Method: 提出 Capsule Lens 框架，为每个概念所占据的区域匹配一个简单可追踪的几何形式——胶囊（capsule），该形式由若干可解释参数定义，以闭式解拟合各概念的几何结构，并在留出样本上进行验证。框架应用于两类场景：静态表示（定位不同模型的概念几何、分析span和norm曲线以发现几何特征）和动态表示（三个案例追踪不同训练设置引起的表示漂移）。

Result: 在静态表示上成功定位了不同模型中的概念几何并揭示重要的几何特征；在动态表示上，三个案例（CLIP预训练、视觉问答RL后训练、数学推理RL后训练）揭示了截然不同的几何动态——CLIP预训练表现为广泛的网络级重构，而RL后训练表现为局部且概念特异的变化。发现既有与现有文献一致的结果，也有新颖的观察。

Conclusion: Capsule Lens 是定位、分析与追踪静态和动态表示中概念几何的有力工具，为机械可解释性提供了新的方法论视角。

Abstract: Understanding how concepts are encoded in the internal representations of machine learning models is a central problem in mechanistic interpretability, essential both for the science of deep learning and for the trustworthy deployment of increasingly capable models. Existing approaches to interpret model representations mainly map representations onto more interpretable spaces and do not directly characterize how concepts occupy representation space; various hypotheses have been proposed, but often lack of rigorous validation and largely focus on static representations. In this work, we introduce Capsule Lens, a framework that matches the region a concept occupies with a simple, trackable geometric form, a capsule, defined by several interpretable parameters, fitted in closed form to each concept's geometry and validated on held-out samples. We apply Capsule Lens in two major settings: static and dynamic representations. On static representations, we demonstrate how to locate concept geometry across various models, and how the span and norm curves uncover important geometric characteristics. On dynamic representations, we present three case studies tracking representation drifts induced by distinct training settings, CLIP pretraining, RL post-training on visual question answering, and RL post-training on mathematical reasoning. These analyses reveal qualitatively different geometric dynamics, ranging from broad network-wide restructuring in CLIP pretraining to localized and concept-specific changes in RL post-training. Our results include findings aligned with existing literature as well as novel observations. We believe Capsule Lens stands as a promising tool for locating, analyzing, and tracking concept geometry in both static and dynamic representations.

</details>


### [197] [HB-PVI: A Hierarchical Bayesian Personalization and Value-of-Information Framework for Complex Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05582)
*Hammed A. Olayinka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HB-PVI框架，一个层次贝叶斯个性化与信息价值框架，用于联合建模参与者异质性、四种个性化机制的利弊以及额外标签的经济价值。在47名参与者的MUSIC-CAR复杂活动数据集上评估后，发现适配器个性化带来的F1增益极小（1个标签时0.00099，10个标签时0.00198），而适配器加头部及原型残差个性化平均为负。在主要实际效益阈值下，一步EVSI在所有决策状态均为零，因此策略不购买任何标签，与永远停止策略完全一致，相比固定10次适配器个性化减少100%标注量，同时保持后验均值F1损失仅0.00217。HB-PVI在216种成本阈值设置中的199种下为效用最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化可以提升活动识别性能，但参与者专属的增益存在异质性，且每个额外的校准标签都有获取成本。现有方法缺乏一个统一的框架来同时建模参与者差异、个性化机制的潜在利弊以及标签的经济价值，因此需要一种基于信息价值而非原始预测精度的决策方法来指导健康传感应用中的个性化决策。

Method: HB-PVI采用层次贝叶斯个性化与信息价值框架，包含：1) 泄漏安全的留一参与者评估协议；2) 序贯蒙特卡洛参与者效应更新器；3) 学生t分布的层次增益模型；4) 一步期望样本信息价值(EVSI)停止准则。框架联合建模参与者异质性以及四种个性化机制的收益与伤害，并在经济价值框架下评估额外校准标签的获取决策。

Result: 适配器个性化产生微小正均值F1增益（1个标签0.00099→10个标签0.00198），适配器加头部和原型残差个性化平均为负。在主要实际效益阈值和成本设定下，一步EVSI不断建议购买标签，匹配永远停止策略（ROPE概率=1）。相比固定10次适配器个性化，减少100%标注量，后验均值F1损失0.00217（95% CI: 0.00048-0.00389），后验概率0.9992低于0.005容忍度。HB-PVI在216种成本阈值设置中的199种以及所有等于或高于主标签成本的设置下为效用最优。

Conclusion: 当个性化增益相对于标注、计算和伤害成本较小时，应采取以人口为先的部署策略。信息价值推理而非原始预测精度应驱动健康传感应用中的个性化决策，这一结论支持在增益有限时不采购个性化标签、保留群体推断的策略。

Abstract: Personalization can improve activity-recognition performance, but participant-specific gains are heterogeneous, and every additional calibration label has an acquisition cost. This study presents HB-PVI, a hierarchical Bayesian personalization and value-of-information framework jointly modeling participant heterogeneity, the benefit and harm of four personalization mechanisms, and the economic value of an additional label, for the 47-participant MUSIC-CAR complex-activity cohort. A leakage-safe, leave-one-participant-out evaluation combines a sequential-Monte-Carlo participant-effect updater with a Student-$t$ hierarchical gain model and a one-step expected-value-of-sample-information (EVSI) stopping rule. Adapter personalization produced small positive mean F1 gains, growing from 0.00099 at one label to 0.00198 at ten, while adapter-plus-head and prototype-residual personalization were negative on average. Under the primary practical-benefit threshold ($Δ_{\min}=0.01$) and cost setting, one-step EVSI was zero at every decision state, so the policy purchased no labels and retained population inference for all 47 participants, matching always-stop exactly (region-of-practical-equivalence probability $=1$). Relative to fixed ten-shot adapter personalization, this reduced labeling by 100\% while keeping the posterior mean F1 loss at 0.00217 (95\% credible interval, 0.00048 to 0.00389), with posterior probability 0.9992 of remaining below the 0.005 tolerance. HB-PVI was utility-optimal in 199 of 216 cost-threshold settings and in every setting at or above the primary label cost. These results argue for a population-first deployment policy whenever personalization gains are small relative to labeling, computation, and harm costs, and show that value-of-information reasoning, not raw predictive accuracy, should drive personalization decisions in health-sensing applications.

</details>


### [198] [Endogenous Exploration in Reinforcement Learning with Intrinsic Curiosity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05650)
*Armando Vieira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于内在好奇心的强化学习框架，专为非平稳、奖励稀疏或缺失的环境设计。该框架将外部奖励与认知动机机制相结合，通过液态状态机（LSM）实现，并在LunarLander和BipedalWalker基准上取得了与PPO、ICM等主流算法相当的竞争性能。核心假设是"好奇心窗口"——中等程度的非相干性下探索效率最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习在面对非平稳环境、稀疏或缺失奖励时表现不佳，现有探索策略往往难以在过度刚性（利用过度）与过度无序（探索过度）之间取得平衡。作者假设在中等程度的动态非相干性下，探索能够最有效地涌现，从而提出一种可量化验证的探索机制。

Method: 框架将动作选择构建为外部奖励与认知动机（epistemic motivation）机制的联合驱动；实现上采用液态状态机（LSM）作为底层计算基质，以产生可控的非相干动态；在LunarLanderv2（离散动作）和BipedalWalkerv3（连续控制）两个标准基准上评估。

Result: 在两项基准任务上取得了与PPO和ICM等既有深度RL算法相当的竞争性能；同时通过对照组实验发现，Active Inference代理在相同分析下未恢复出"好奇心窗口"，说明该探索动态捕捉了一种独特的探索机制。

Conclusion: 中等程度的非相干性是有效探索的关键区间，过度刚性或过度无序都会导致性能下降。基于LSM的好奇心驱动框架在复杂环境中具备竞争力，且其所捕获的探索模式与Active Inference存在本质差异，为探索机制设计提供了新的理论视角和实用框架。

Abstract: We propose a reinforcement learning framework in which exploration is driven by intrinsic curiosity, designed for scenarios where environments are non-stationary and rewards are sparse, delayed, uninformative, or absent. In our model, action selection is guided by a combination of external rewards and an epistemic motivation mechanism that biases the agent toward structured exploratory directions. The central hypothesis is that effective exploration emerges at intermediate levels of incoherence, while performance degrades under both overly rigid and overly disordered dynamics. To test this idea, we implement the framework on top of a Liquid State Machine (LSM) substrate and evaluate it on two standard benchmarks: the discrete-action LunarLanderv2 and the continuous-control BipedalWalkerv3. The proposed method achieves competitive performance on both tasks relative to established deep RL algorithms, including Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and Intrinsic Curiosity Module (ICM). We further show that the curiosity window is not recovered in Active Inference agents under the same analysis, suggesting that the proposed dynamics capture a distinct exploration regime

</details>


### [199] [Robustness of LLM-Generated SystemVerilog Assertions to Semantics-Preserving RTL Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05658)
*FNU Aditi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受控蜕变式评估方法，检验大语言模型在语义保持的RTL变换下的SystemVerilog Assertion生成稳定性。研究发现，在六个模型-变换条件组合下，9.7%-27.0%原本正确的行为在变换后变得不正确，表明单点准确率不足以表征LLM在断言生成中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成SVA的评估大多只报告单一语法表示下的正确率，无法揭示当同一RTL行为以不同方式编写时模型输出的稳定性。单点准确率可能掩盖显著的生成不稳定性，需要对LLM在硬件验证任务中的鲁棒性进行更严格的评估。

Method: 从VERT数据集出发构建质量过滤的条件控制池和分层40程序评估集（含295个赋值行为）；评估Qwen2.5-Coder-7B和DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite两个开源代码模型，使用相同评估提示和贪婪解码。研究三种语义保持变换：操作数重排、确定性标识符重命名、冗余括号化。除基线准确率和变换后准确率外，还测量条件鲁棒性、不变性失败和任意翻转率，并使用10,000样本聚类自助法区间进行RTL程序级别分析。

Result: 在所有六个模型-变换条件下，9.7%-27.0%在原始RTL上正确的行为在语义保持变换后变得不正确。标识符重命名下，DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite准确率从53.9%提高到63.7%，但19.5%的原本正确行为失败。人工审查30个正确到错误的转变样本，识别出丢失路径谓词、分支极性错误、布尔结构破坏和输出契约违规四类错误模式。

Conclusion: 单点准确率不足以表征LLM在断言生成中的可靠性，聚合准确率的提升可能同时伴随着大量原本正确行为的失败。研究结果为AI辅助硬件验证提出了鲁棒性感知评估的必要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being explored for automating SystemVerilog Assertion (SVA) generation, yet most evaluations report correctness on a single syntactic representation of an input. Such point accuracy does not reveal whether a model's correct output is stable when the same RTL behavior is written differently. This paper presents a controlled metamorphic evaluation of LLM-based SVA generation under semantics-preserving RTL transformations. Starting from the VERT dataset, we construct a quality-filtered conditional-control pool and a stratified 40-program evaluation set containing 295 assignment behaviors. We evaluate two open code models, Qwen2.5-Coder-7B and DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite, with an identical evaluation prompt and greedy decoding. Three transformations are studied: operand reordering, deterministic identifier renaming, and redundant parenthesization. Beyond baseline and transformed accuracy, we measure conditional robustness, invariance failure, and any-flip rate, with 10,000-sample clustered bootstrap intervals at the RTL-program level. Across all six model-transformation conditions, 9.7%-27.0% of behaviors that were correct on the original RTL become incorrect after a semantics-preserving transformation. Aggregate accuracy can therefore hide substantial instability: under identifier renaming, DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite improves from 53.9% to 63.7% accuracy while 19.5% of its originally correct behaviors fail. Manual review of 30 sampled correct-to-wrong transitions identifies dropped path predicates, branch-polarity errors, Boolean-structure corruption, and output-contract violations. The results show that point accuracy alone is insufficient for characterizing LLM reliability in assertion generation and motivate robustness-aware evaluation for AI-assisted hardware verification.

</details>


### [200] [PAC-Private Autoregressive Generation: Calibrating Noise to Ensemble Disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05676)
*Mina Mirzadehsarcheshmeh,Amir Keyvan Khandani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将PAC隐私（PAC privacy）从分类任务扩展到自回归生成任务。通过从私有语料库构建128个重叠世界（每篇记录出现在64个世界中），在冻结的公开模型上为每个世界训练适配器，利用公开模型和世界投票之间的分歧来确定PAC噪声。作者证明了I(S;Y_1:T) ≤ bT，并提出了耦合解码以避免贪婪退化。在WikiText-103与GPT-2-small上，该方法在每token预算2^-32下保留了74%的微调增益，且在10^6 token后成员推理成功率上限为51.08%。与PMixED相比，在相同成员推理边界下，该方法在10^2至10^6 token范围内保留了98%的非私有headroom（PMixED至多56%），且没有交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有私有预测方法（如PMixED）在每次发布时都会产生隐私成本，且长期依赖公开模型导致效用退化。PAC隐私虽然能根据输出在不同秘密间的变异性来校准噪声，但此前仅适用于分类任务，未扩展到自回归生成（如语言模型按token生成文本的典型场景）。

Method: 1) 从私有语料库构造m=128个重叠"世界"，每篇记录恰好出现在m/2个世界中，在冻结的公开模型上为每个世界训练一个适配器，真实世界作为秘密。2) 在每个token生成时，公开模型定义候选集，各世界投票，用后验加权分歧决定PAC噪声大小；若所有世界一致同意则无需校准噪声。3) 证明互信息界I(S;Y_1:T) ≤ I(S;H_T) ≤ bT。4) 提出耦合解码策略，在处理自适应自生成上下文的同时保持隐私核算，避免贪婪退化。
