Calendar-SPCA is evaluated on two independent smart-meter datasets with different sample sizes and temporal resolutions: GoiEner and Low Carbon London. A factorial experiment characterizes the complementary effects of sparsity and calendar coherence and examines robustness across sample size, latent dimensionality, and repeated fits. At rank 15, Calendar-SPCA retains 96.92% and 82.90% of the explained variance of rank-matched PCA in GoiEner and Low Carbon London, respectively, while producing mean loading sparsities of 61.95% and 81.50%. Comparisons with classical sparse PCA and SPCA-TV further show that Calendar-SPCA adds a systematic organization of the latent factors in the original calendar coordinates while preserving substantial low-rank information. The resulting components form coherent and complementary daily, weekly, seasonal, and jointly localized calendar patterns, with dataset-specific geometries across the two datasets.

</details>


### [235] [ACE: Adapter Consolidation across Experts for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of MoE LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06072)
*Ahin Lee,Sehyun Yun,Joonha Park,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ACE（Adapter Consolidation across Experts）方法，针对MoE模型下参数高效微调（PEFT）中专家级LoRA适配器分解冗余的问题，对冗余专家进行分组，用组共享的高秩LoRA模块替代专家专属适配器，并提出分组适配器执行方式将零散的GEMM运算合并为更大的组级运算。在12个数据集和4个MoE骨干网络上，ACE在参数匹配的PEFT方法中取得了最高平均精度，相较专家级LoRA训练速度提升1.31~1.48倍，且不增加峰值内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型上的PEFT方法通常为每个专家单独挂载低秩适配器，这种专家级设计带来三方面碎片化问题：容量被分割到狭窄的低秩更新中、稀疏路由下梯度监督稀疏且不均衡、执行被分解为大量小GEMM运算。作者发现专家间适配器在微调中会趋于功能相似、存在冗余，因此这种专家级拆分往往不必要，有必要加以整合优化。

Method: 先识别微调过程中功能相似的冗余LoRA适配器对应的专家，对这些专家进行分组；随后在相同PEFT参数预算下，用组间共享的更高秩LoRA模块替代各专家专属的低秩适配器；同时设计分组适配器执行策略，将碎片化的专家级适配器计算合并为更少的、更大规模的组级GEMM运算。

Result: 在12个数据集和4个MoE骨干网络上的评估表明，在完整基线覆盖的三个骨干网络上，ACE在参数匹配的PEFT方法中取得最高平均准确率；与专家级LoRA相比，训练墙钟时间加速1.31倍至1.48倍，且未增加峰值内存。

Conclusion: ACE验证了MoE微调中专家专属适配器冗余的普遍性，通过将冗余专家适配器整合为组共享的高秩模块，既能提升精度又加速训练，为MoE模型的高效参数微调提供了一种更简洁高效的替代方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of mixture-of-experts (MoE) models commonly attaches a separate low-rank adapter to each expert. This expert-wise design fragments adaptation in three ways: capacity is split across narrow low-rank updates, gradient supervision becomes sparse and imbalanced under sparse routing, and execution is decomposed into many small GEMMs. We find that such expert-wise separation is often unnecessary, as subsets of LoRA adapters become functionally similar during fine-tuning, revealing redundancy among expert-specific adapters. Based on this redundancy, we propose ACE (Adapter Consolidation across Experts), which groups redundant experts and replaces their expert-specific adapters with group-shared higher-rank LoRA modules under the same PEFT budget. ACE further introduces grouped adapter execution, which consolidates fragmented expert-wise adapter computations into fewer, larger group-level GEMMs. Across evaluations covering 12 datasets and four MoE backbones, ACE achieves the highest observed mean accuracy among the parameter-matched PEFT methods on the three backbones with complete baseline coverage, while providing $1.31\times$ to $1.48\times$ wall-clock training speedup over expert-wise LoRA without increasing peak memory. Our code is available at https://github.com/UbiquitousAILab/ACE.

</details>


### [236] [FedSubMuon: Communication-Efficient Federated LLM Fine-Tuning via Structured Subspace Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06073)
*Shaolong Chen,Youming Tao,Shuzhen Chen,Falko Dressler,Qingqing Ye,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedSubMuon和其精度优化变体FedSubMuon-GT，一种通信高效的联邦Muon微调方法。通过在共享结构化子空间中优化紧凑系数矩阵，保留了Muon对单一矩阵值参数更新的几何特性，同时将客户端上传量从完整的层大小更新和优化器状态缩减为紧凑系数矩阵。实验表明，FedSubMuon-GT在五个数据集-模型对中的四个上取得最佳整体精度，FedSubMuon在所有匹配通信预算下表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调在大语言模型跨设备训练中面临高通信成本瓶颈。现有联邦Muon方法虽展示了矩阵感知优化的优势，但仍需传输完整的层大小更新和优化器状态，通信开销巨大。直接对LoRA因子应用Muon会改变优化目标并削弱其矩阵感知的更新几何特性，因此需要一种既保持Muon特性又显著降低通信成本的方法。

Method: FedSubMuon的核心思想是在共享的结构化子空间内优化紧凑系数矩阵，使Muon始终作用于单一矩阵值可训练对象，同时将客户端上传内容压缩为紧凑系数矩阵。在此基础上，FedSubMuon-GT作为精度导向的扩展，利用投影梯度来调整跟踪的子空间基，使其朝向与任务相关的梯度方向适应。

Result: 在指令微调和数学推理任务上，FedSubMuon-GT在五个数据集-模型配对中的四个上取得最佳整体精度；FedSubMuon在所有匹配通信预算下表现最佳。在Dolly-15K数据集上，与通信量最接近的基线相比，Llama-1B和Qwen-4B的总通信量分别减少了5.5倍和1.4倍。

Conclusion: FedSubMuon系列方法通过共享结构化子空间策略，成功实现了通信高效与优化性能的兼顾，验证了在保持Muon矩阵感知更新特性的同时大幅降低联邦微调通信开销的可行性，为跨设备大模型联邦微调提供了新的优化范式。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning adapts large language models (LLMs) to decentralized client data, but its scalability in cross-device training is often limited by the high communication cost. Muon is an optimizer that improves optimization performance by orthogonalizing momentum for matrix-valued parameters. Existing federated Muon methods demonstrate the benefit of matrix-aware optimization in federated learning, but still require transmitting full layer-size updates and optimizer state. A natural way to reduce communication is to directly apply Muon to LoRA factors, but this changes the optimized object and weakens Muon's matrix-aware update geometry. We propose FedSubMuon, a communication-efficient federated Muon fine-tuning method that optimizes compact coefficient matrices within shared structured subspaces. This design keeps Muon on a single matrix-valued trainable object, while reducing the client upload to compact coefficient matrices. We further introduce FedSubMuon-GT, an accuracy-oriented extension that uses projected gradients to adapt tracked subspace bases toward task-relevant gradient directions. Experiments on instruction tuning and mathematical reasoning show that FedSubMuon-GT achieves the best overall accuracy on four of five dataset-model pairs, while FedSubMuon performs best under all matched communication budgets. On Dolly-15K, the closest communication baseline requires 5.5 times and 1.4 times more total communication on Llama-1B and Qwen-4B, respectively.

</details>


### [237] [Beyond Retraining-Free MoE Compression: A Cost-Normalized Study of Post-Compression Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06076)
*Sieun Hyeon,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出压缩后的MoE检查点不应被视为最终产物，而应视为压缩初始化，通过极小的后压缩微调阶段即可显著恢复因压缩损失的性能。仅用3,000个C4样本和单轮epoch的调整，全参数微调平均能恢复原始与压缩模型之间性能差距的37.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的免重训MoE压缩方法（如剪枝或专家合并）通常将压缩后的检查点视为最终产物，忽略了压缩后的检查点仍具有进一步恢复性能的潜力。作者认为这一视角不完整，应重新审视压缩检查点的本质。

Method: 作者在两种MoE LLM骨干、四种剪枝/合并方法、三种专家保留比率和28个基准上进行了系统比较，对比LM微调和基于教师的知识蒸馏（KD），在匹配的小数据预算和GPU成本条件下评估了不同调整策略的效果。

Result: 仅使用3,000个C4样本和单轮epoch微调，全参数微调平均可恢复原始与压缩模型间性能差距的37.3%。此外，LM微调比标准token级KD更具成本效益，全参数调整在所测范围内提供了最强的成本-恢复权衡。

Conclusion: 免重训压缩应与小的后压缩微调阶段相结合，以恢复压缩过程中丢失的相当一部分性能。这一发现为MoE模型的高效部署提供了更完整的实践指导。

Abstract: Retraining-free MoE compression reduces deployment memory by pruning or merging experts, but often treats the compressed checkpoint as the final artifact. We argue that this view is incomplete: compressed MoE checkpoints are better understood as compressed initializations that benefit from a tiny post-compression adjustment stage. Across two MoE LLM backbones, four pruning/merging methods, three expert-retention ratios, and 28 benchmarks, we compare LM fine-tuning and teacher-based KD under matched small-data budgets and measured GPU costs. Using only 3,000 C4 examples and a single epoch of adjustment, Full FT recovers 37.3% of the original-to-compressed performance gap on average. Moreover, LM fine-tuning is more cost-effective than standard token-level KD, and full-parameter adjustment gives the strongest cost--recovery trade-off among the tested scopes. These results suggest that retraining-free compression should be paired with small post-compression adjustment to recover a substantial portion of the performance lost during compression.

</details>


### [238] [PhenoBench: Mapping What a Deeply Phenotyped Human Cohort Can Tell Us](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06080)
*Gal Sapir,Alon Diament,Adva Wolf,Doron Yaya-Stupp,Dikla Gelbard Solodkin,Dana Azouri,Anat Etzion-Fuchs,Guy Lutsker,Eran Segal,Hagai Rossman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PhenoBench，一个基于Human Phenotype Project（超过13,000名参与者）的可执行基准测试系统，定义了90项涵盖15个领域和26种输入模态的临床任务，用于系统评估多模态纵向队列数据中不同测量和模型对健康相关问题的预测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度表型队列整合了临床、影像、分子和可穿戴设备等多时间尺度数据，但不同分析之间缺乏直接可比性，难以判断哪些测量对哪些健康相关问题最有信息价值。需要一个统一、可执行、可审计的基准测试框架来解决这一问题。

Method: PhenoBench通过固定每个问题的目标、合格人群、时间和允许信息，并定义包含数据划分、评估指标、基线和声明边界的评估契约。基准定义了90项任务、15个领域和26种输入模态，并利用它评估了表格基础模型（160项回归比较，52项任务）和14种语言模型（40项任务）的性能。

Result: 测量显示与问题和表征相关的预测价值，包括相对于匹配基线有正向、接近零和负向的留出性能变化。表格基础模型在总体上优于标准任务特定模型，但三个预训练模型平均仅比岭回归改善0.004 R²（95% CI, 0.002-0.006）。语言模型在部分任务上可做信息性预测，但存在任务特定能力差距、共同的规模失败，且很少超过在相同字段上拟合的模型。

Conclusion: PhenoBench将多模态纵向队列转化为一个版本化、可审计的评估系统，允许在不重新定义现有比较的情况下添加新问题、测量和模型。它揭示了新兴基础模型在该领域预测上虽有一定价值但增益有限，为未来模型发展和评估提供了可靠基准。

Abstract: Deeply phenotyped cohorts combine clinical, imaging, molecular, and wearable observations across timescales from seconds to years. This breadth can reveal which measurements inform which health-related questions, but heterogeneous analyses are not directly comparable. We present PhenoBench, an executable benchmark built around the Human Phenotype Project, in which more than 13,000 participants have completed the initial visit. Each question fixes the target, eligible population, timing, and allowed information; its evaluation contract specifies the split, metric, baseline, and claim boundary. The benchmark defines 90 clinically grounded tasks across 15 domains and 26 input modalities. Measurements showed question- and representation-dependent predictive value, including positive, near-zero, and negative changes in held-out performance relative to matched baselines. We used PhenoBench to evaluate emerging tabular foundation models across 160 matched regression comparisons spanning 52 tasks. These models ranked above standard task-specific models in aggregate but, averaged across the three pretrained models within each cell, improved on ridge by a median of only 0.004 $R^2$ (95% CI, 0.002--0.006). We then used the same cohort data and evaluation contracts to evaluate 14 language models, collectively covering 40 tasks spanning phenotype recovery, classification, follow-up forecasting, and participant ordering. Without cohort-specific fitting, language models made informative predictions on some tasks, but showed task-specific capability gaps, shared failures of scale, and rarely surpassed models fitted on the same fields. PhenoBench turns a multimodal longitudinal cohort into a versioned, auditable evaluation system where new questions, measurements, and models can be added without redefining existing comparisons.

</details>


### [239] [Learning to Price and Stock Under Contextual and Censored Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06083)
*Zean Han,Zezhen Ding,Jiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联合定价与库存控制框架，将需求建模为基函数的线性组合以利用上下文信息，并设计了高效算法来处理需求被截断（lost sales）的问题，实现了接近最优的遗憾界（regret bound）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售商的定价与库存决策面临两大实际挑战：市场条件动态变化（受多种上下文因素影响），以及因销售损失（lost sales）导致需求信息被截断而难以获取真实需求。现有方法未能同时考虑上下文信息和需求观测被截断的问题，存在研究空白。

Method: 将需求建模为基函数的线性组合（包含未知系数），以适应上下文变化。设计了一个高效在线学习算法，在凹收益条件下实现O(K√T log T)的遗憾界，在一般情形下实现O(K^{2/3}T^{2/3}(log T)^{1/2})的遗憾界，并给出匹配的下界以证明最优性。

Result: 算法在凹收益条件下达到O(K√T log T)遗憾界，在一般条件下达到O(K^{2/3}T^{2/3}(log T)^{1/2})遗憾界，且下界匹配验证了算法的最优性。大量跨场景数值实验验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究首次将上下文信息与需求截断问题统一纳入联合定价与库存控制框架，所提算法在理论上达到了最优（或接近最优）的遗憾界，且在实际数值实验中表现良好，为现代零售商在动态市场环境中进行自适应决策提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: To make optimal joint pricing and inventory control decisions is a critical challenge for modern retailers. In practice, retailers face changing market conditions where demands are influenced by various contextual factors, while simultaneously dealing with the difficulty of lost sales that obscure true demand information. However, existing approaches often fail to account for both contextual information and censored demand observations. We address this gap by presenting a framework where we model demand as a linear combination of basis functions with unknown coefficients, allowing for adaptive pricing and inventory decisions that respond to changing contexts. We propose an efficient algorithm to achieve regret bound $\mathcal{O}(K\sqrt{T}\log T)$ under concave revenue conditions and $\mathcal{O}(K^{2/3}T^{2/3}(\log T)^{1/2})$ for the general case, with matching lower bounds confirming optimality. Extensive numerical experiments across diverse scenarios demonstrate our algorithm's effectiveness.

</details>


### [240] [Connectome-to-Function: Conditional Generative Latent Representations for Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06093)
*Zhuolin Yu,Xingyu Liu,Yuanhao Jia,Yunhang Xiao,Hairuo Xue,Feihan Sun,Guozhang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种条件生成潜变量框架，将连接组图编码为紧凑结构空间，既能以高精度（AUC最高0.910）重建观测连接，又能生成新候选连接组，并通过储备池计算实验证明该潜空间能捕捉功能变异（交叉验证R²最高约0.87），且可解释性分析揭示了任务特异的神经结构机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接组图是理解大脑回路支持功能与计算的基础，但其高维、稀疏、对局部结构变异敏感的特性使得将结构映射到计算十分困难。现有方法依赖手工设计的结构描述符或任务特定预测器，限制了连接组在兼具生成性和功能意义的形式上进行表征的能力。

Method: 提出一个条件生成潜变量框架：将连接组图编码为紧凑的结构潜空间，利用可用的节点级条件信息指导重建和生成过程。该框架能够从潜空间中重建观测连接，生成新的候选连接组，并将连接组衍生的图作为储备池计算的计算基底进行功能分析，同时结合可解释性分析揭示结构与功能之间的关系。

Result: 模型实现了平均边重建AUC高达0.910，在储备池计算实验中潜空间捕捉功能变异的交叉验证R²高达约0.87。可解释性分析显示：记忆性能与互惠循环连接相关，而预测和分类任务与循环网络的谱属性更密切相关。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一种AI驱动的科学方法，将神经连接性与计算联系起来，为研究不同结构机制如何塑造计算能力提供了生成性和可解释性的基础，有望统一分析图结构特征与计算行为之间的关系。

Abstract: Connectomes, graph-level maps of neurons and their synaptic connections, provide a structural basis for understanding how brain circuits support function and computation. However, mapping connectome structure to computation remains difficult because these graphs are high-dimensional, sparse, and sensitive to local structural variation. Existing approaches often depend on hand-crafted structural descriptors or task-specific predictors, which limits their ability to represent connectomes in a form that is both generative and functionally meaningful. We propose a conditional generative latent framework that encodes connectome graphs into a compact structural space while using available node-level conditions to guide reconstruction and generation. From this space, the model can reconstruct observed connectivity with a mean edge-reconstruction AUC up to 0.910 and generate new candidate connectomes, enabling a unified analysis of graph structure and computational behavior. Using connectome-derived graphs as recurrent computational substrates, we found that the learned latent space captures functional variation across reservoir-computing experiments, with cross-validated $R^2$ values up to approximately 0.87. Interpretability analysis further revealed task-specific structural mechanisms: in our examples, memory performance is associated with reciprocal recurrent connectivity, whereas prediction and classification are more strongly associated with spectral properties of the recurrent network. These findings suggest an AI-for-science approach to linking neural connectivity to computation and provide a generative and interpretable basis for studying how distinct structural mechanisms shape computational capacity.

</details>


### [241] [VERPO: Verified Evidence Regularized Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06100)
*Haijiang Li,Chengyu Lv,Yi Zhang,Zhibing Zhang,Rui Qian,Yuchen Zhang,Xiaofan Zhang,Mingshan Wang,Xiaofei Jing,Yu Tong,Cangqi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VERPO是一个基于已验证证据正则化的策略优化框架，通过分离证据无关的参考恢复和有符号的token级证据校正，配合Fisher证据对比与ZPD控制器，在科学推理和工具使用任务上显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列级奖励无法精确定位哪些token级决策应当保留或修正；已有的证据条件教师方法虽能提供密集监督，但盲目模仿可能引入对任务成功无帮助的格式或推理风格偏移。

Method: VERPO将证据视为策略修正的提议而非直接模仿目标，保留结果目标的同时引入三部分机制：分离证据无关参考恢复与有符号token级证据校正；Fisher证据对比机制沿估计的证据存在方向衰减校正；停止梯度的token级ZPD控制器根据局部奖励对齐和Fisher移动成本调节接受度，参考通道独立于接受度。

Result: 在五个科学推理和工具使用任务上，各骨干网络上的最佳变体均超过最强对比基线：Qwen3-4B平均分从0.6826提升至0.6857，Qwen3-8B从0.6895提升至0.7058，Llama-3.2-1B从0.4751大幅提升至0.5657。

Conclusion: VERPO通过将证据作为有监督的校正信号而非全面模仿目标，更精确地引导策略优化，在保留结果奖励目标的同时实现token级别的细粒度校正，有效提升了模型在科学推理和工具使用任务上的表现。

Abstract: Verifiable outcome rewards guide language-model post-training, but sequence-level advantages do not identify which token-level decisions should be preserved or revised. Evidence-conditioned Teachers provide denser supervision by replaying sampled trajectories with privileged feedback. Yet indiscriminate imitation risks transferring formatting or reasoning-style shifts that do not support task success. We introduce VERPO, a Verified Evidence Regularized Policy Optimization framework that treats evidence as a proposal for policy correction while retaining the outcome objective. It separates evidence-free reference restoration from signed token-level evidence corrections. Fisher Evidence Contrast attenuates corrections along an estimated evidence-presence direction. A stopped token-wise ZPD controller scales acceptance according to local reward alignment and Fisher movement cost, while the reference channel remains independent of acceptance. Across five scientific-reasoning and tool-use tasks, the best variant on each backbone exceeds the strongest compared baseline in average score. The averages rise from 0.6826 to 0.6857 on Qwen3-4B, from 0.6895 to 0.7058 on Qwen3-8B, and from 0.4751 to 0.5657 on Llama-3.2-1B.

</details>


### [242] [FANS: Federated Adaptive Network Search Learning for Heterogeneous Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06106)
*Jiaxin Zhang,Xingwei Wang,Bo Yi,Liang Zhao,Alireza Furutanpey,Ziyi Chen,Qiang He,Keqin Li,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FANS提出了一种基于超网络的异构联邦学习框架，通过学习共享的架构空间而非固定模型配置集合，配合FPS并行缩放算法和自蒸馏机制，在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和MNLI基准上实现了数量级的子网络扩展（如ResNet-18从4个扩展到4,680个候选）并显著改善了精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构联邦学习方法通常将训练绑定到少量预定义模型配置上，导致架构覆盖范围有限，无法适应设备多样化的资源预算需求。

Method: 提出FANS框架，采用超网络学习共享架构空间而非固定客户端模型集；并提出FPS算法，通过并行训练多个采样子网络并结合自蒸馏，使较大的采样子网络在本地更新中监督较小的子网络。

Result: 在CIFAR-10（ResNet-18）、CIFAR-100（DenseNet-121）和MNLI（BERT-base）三个基准上的实验表明，FANS将可行子网络池扩展了数个数量级（如ResNet-18候选数从4增至4,680），并在平均精度-效率权衡上优于代表性HFL基线。评估覆盖了精度、参数数量和MACs等指标。

Conclusion: 通过学习连续可扩展的架构空间，FANS克服了传统HFL中预定义配置菜单的局限性，为异构设备环境下的联邦学习提供了更广泛的架构覆盖和更优的精度-效率折中方案。

Abstract: Heterogeneous Federated Learning (HFL) aims to train models across devices with diverse resource budgets while preserving data privacy. Existing HFL methods typically bind training to a small predefined menu of model configurations, which limits architectural coverage. To address this bottleneck, we introduce Federated Adaptive Network Search (FANS), a hypernetwork-based framework that learns a shared architecture space rather than a fixed set of client models. To optimize this shared space efficiently, we propose the Federated Parallel Scaling (FPS) algorithm, which jointly trains multiple sampled subnetworks in parallel with self-distillation so that larger sampled subnetworks can supervise smaller ones during local updates. We evaluate FANS on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and MNLI using ResNet-18, DenseNet-121, and BERT-base, respectively. Across all benchmarks, FANS expands the feasible subnetwork pool by orders of magnitude (e.g., 4,680 candidates for ResNet-18 vs. 4 in existing methods) and improves the average accuracy-efficiency trade-off relative to representative HFL baselines. Device heterogeneity is emulated through resource tiers, and evaluation covers accuracy, parameter count, and MACs.

</details>


### [243] [DataFlex-RL: An Evaluation Platform for RLVR Data Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06107)
*Hao Liang,Mingrui Chen,Hengyi Feng,Meiyi Qiang,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DataFlex-RL平台，系统评估RLVR中的数据策略选择。实验表明，均匀GRPO训练在12个基准上比未训练检查点提升7.76个百分点，但所有8种rollout选择/重加权方法和3种自适应混合策略均未在95%置信区间内显著优于均匀采样。此外，不同评价摘要（数学密集型6基准vs领域均衡12基准）产生负相关的排名（相关系数-0.33），表明评价敏感性深刻影响结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR中的数据策略（选择哪些rollout、如何加权、哪些领域进入训练批次）对训练效果有重要影响，但缺乏系统性的量化比较平台和严格的统计评估，现有研究往往在小规模或单一设置下得出结论，难以确定数据策略是否真正优于简单均匀采样。

Method: 构建DataFlex-RL统一评估平台，在共同GRPO配方下比较13种数据策略配置，使用12个匹配种子（Qwen2.5-7B-Base，12个数学/逻辑/科学基准），并用12种子扩展（Llama-3.1-8B-Base）进行修正验证；另外对9个Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct运行进行重打分以量化评价敏感性。

Result: 均匀GRPO比未训练基线提升7.76个百分点；8种rollout选择方法和3种自适应混合方法均无配对95%置信区间排除零；在Llama-3.1-8B-Base上的扩展未发现一致的优胜者；数学密集型摘要与领域均衡摘要产生的排名负相关（r=-0.33），而保留全部12个基准的摘要高度一致。

Conclusion: 在所研究的受控设置中，改变数据策略确实可测量地改变了训练过程，但未产生可复现的优于均匀训练的效果。研究同时揭示评估基准选择本身会显著影响策略排名，呼吁对RLVR数据策略结论采取更审慎的统计标准和评价设计。

Abstract: Data policies for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) determine which rollouts are used, how strongly they are weighted, and which domains contribute to subsequent training batches. We introduce DataFlex-RL, an evaluation platform for comparing these choices under a common GRPO recipe. Our primary experiment evaluates 13 configurations across 12 matched seeds using Qwen2.5-7B-Base and 12 mathematics, logic, and science benchmarks. Uniform GRPO improves the domain-balanced average accuracy by 7.76 percentage points over the untrained checkpoint. None of the eight rollout-selection or reweighting methods achieves a paired 95% confidence interval that excludes zero relative to uniform sampling, and none of the three adaptive mixtures outperforms a fixed equal mixture at the same level of precision. A corrected 12-seed extension on Llama-3.1-8B-Base places the additional methods on the same score scale as the original controls, but does not reveal a consistent winner in terms of observed mean performance. We also quantify evaluation sensitivity by rescoring nine Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct runs using a math-heavy six-benchmark summary, consisting of five mathematics benchmarks and GPQA-Diamond but no logic benchmark, and comparing it with the domain-balanced 12-benchmark summary. The resulting rankings are negatively correlated, with a correlation coefficient of -0.33, whereas summaries that retain all 12 benchmarks largely agree. Across the controlled settings studied here, changing the data policy measurably changes the training process but does not produce a reproducible improvement over uniform training.

</details>


### [244] [Sparse Incident-Cluster Learning for 12-hour Port Flood Pre-warning in Digital-Twin Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06109)
*Jie Zhang,Qiang Ni,David Windridge,Huan X. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究将12小时港口洪水预警建模为事件簇学习问题，提出了一套包含折叠特定稀疏特征选择、预警簇分组、阴性标签对照、100次随机top-k对照及预警片段评估的综合验证协议。在利物浦四簇案例中，top-10 ElasticNet模型达到F2=0.696，超过无截断版本（0.633）并可与全特征XGBoost（0.681）竞争，最终形成可集成到港口数字孪生的离线评估分析与验证模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 港口洪水数字孪生需要能在中断发生前向操作人员发出警告的分析方法，但官方警告事件往往稀少且相邻观测存在时间依赖性，导致行级分类会因同一事件窗口同时出现在训练和评估数据中而高估模型性能。

Method: 将港口洪水预警建模为事件簇学习问题，采用8点水位历史、预测时点上下文协变量和可解释的短窗口动态特征；评估协议包括折叠特定的稀疏特征选择、警告簇分组、阴性标签对照、100次重复随机top-k对照以及警报片段评估。以利物浦为主案例（4个簇），并使用Humber/Hull代理和Wessex South数据进行协议迁移性检验。

Result: 在利物浦各折叠中，top-10 ElasticNet模型平均F2=0.696，优于无top-k截断的0.633和全特征加权XGBoost的0.681；该模型是ElasticNet系列中最强的变体，仅用10个预测变量即可与非线性参考模型竞争，并超过重复级别95百分位的广泛及同族随机子集。历史回放将风险评分转换为警报片段并测定警报持续时间与误报负担。

Conclusion: 通过事件簇级别的严格评估协议，证明了轻量级可解释模型在港口洪水预警中的有效性，并提供了一个经过离线评估、可集成到港口数字孪生的分析与验证模块。

Abstract: Port flood digital twins require analytics that warn operators before disruption, but official warning incidents are often few and adjacent observations are temporally dependent. Row-level classification can therefore overstate performance by placing windows from the same event in both model-development and evaluation data. We formulate 12-hour port flood pre-warning as an incident-cluster learning problem and evaluate a digital-twin analytics module using eight-point water-level histories, prediction-time contextual covariates, and interpretable short-window dynamics. The protocol combines fold-specific sparse feature selection, warning-cluster grouping, negative-label controls, 100-repeat random top-k controls, and alert-episode evaluation. Liverpool is the primary four-cluster case study, with harmonised Humber/Hull-proxy and Wessex South data used for protocol-transfer checks. Across the Liverpool folds, the top-10 ElasticNet model achieves mean F2 = 0.696, compared with 0.633 without top-k truncation and 0.681 for full-feature weighted XGBoost. It is the strongest ElasticNet variant, remains competitive with the nonlinear reference using only ten predictors, and exceeds the repeat-level 95th percentile of broad and same-family random subsets. Contextual covariates provide a strong prediction-time anchor, complemented by physically interpretable local dynamics. Historical replay converts risk scores into alert episodes and measures alert duration and false-episode burden. The result is an offline-evaluated analytics and validation module designed for integration into a port digital twin.

</details>


### [245] [Compressed Recurrent Feedback in Tsetlin Machines: A Reproducible Boolean-FSM Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06133)
*Ankit Kumar,Utkarsh Raj,Rishad Shafik,Sudip Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种固定宽度的循环Tsetlin机（RTM）设计，通过异或（XOR）折叠将480个clause激活压缩为96个循环位，并在双时间尺度下保留阈值化后的二值状态。在可复现的布尔有限状态机基准上（144次运行），压缩模型在两个任务族上分别获得61.47%±6.74%和62.94%±9.92%的准确率，与原始clause反馈差距不足一个百分点，但循环宽度缩小了十倍，执行时间降低了约4倍。值得注意的是，无反馈对照模型表现相当或更优，强调了在序列模型基准测试中设置无反馈对照的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTM通过将上一时刻的clause输出作为下一时刻输入来提供记忆，但直接反馈的宽度随clause库规模增长，导致循环输入过宽，不适用于小型设备上的序列推理。作者旨在探索一种固定宽度的替代方案，以在不显著损失性能的前提下缩小循环接口。

Method: 作者利用异或（XOR）操作折叠clause激活，将压缩后的位以两个时间尺度保留，再阈值化回二值状态。每个原始循环输入（采用480个clause激活）被压缩为96个循环位，从而在不依赖原始clause反馈列表的情况下维持序列记忆能力。

Result: 在包含明确转移规则、数据划分和随机种子的可复现布尔有限状态机基准上，跨144次运行的实验中，压缩模型获得61.47%±6.74%和62.94%±9.92%的准确率。原始clause反馈的改变在均值上不足一个百分点，但将循环宽度增大十倍，且实测主机执行时间增加4.38倍和3.71倍。此外，无反馈对照在保留短输入历史的情况下达到相当或略高的准确率。

Conclusion: 在该基准上，XOR折叠方法与原始反馈在小经验范围内表现匹配，同时实现了更窄的循环接口。然而，门控神经模型仍具更高准确率，且无反馈对照模型的表现说明当前基准可能不足以证明RTM循环记忆的独特价值，凸显了序列模型评估中加入无反馈循环对照的关键必要性。

Abstract: Sequential inference on small devices requires a model to retain useful history without repeatedly processing a long input record. A Recurrent Tsetlin Machine (RTM) provides this memory by returning Boolean clause outputs from one time step as inputs to the next. Direct feedback, however, grows with the clause bank and can make the recurrent input unnecessarily wide. This paper investigates a fixed-width alternative. We combine clause activations by exclusive-OR (XOR) folding, retain the folded bits at two time scales, and threshold them back to a binary state. The resulting design reduces 480 clause activations to 96 recurrent bits. We evaluate the method on a reproducible Boolean finite-state-machine benchmark with explicit transition rules, data splits, and random seeds. Across 144 runs, the compressed model obtains $61.47 \pm 6.74\%$ and $62.94 \pm 9.92\%$ accuracy on the two task families. Raw clause feedback changes these means by less than one percentage point, while increasing the recurrent width tenfold and measured host execution time by $4.38\times$ and $3.71\times$. Gated neural models remain more accurate, and a no-feedback control retaining only short input history achieves comparable or slightly higher accuracy. On this benchmark, folding matches raw feedback within small empirical margins at a much narrower interface; these findings also underscore the critical necessity of no-feedback recurrence controls when benchmarking sequence models.

</details>


### [246] [Rethinking One-Shot Federated Graph Learning: Training-Free Statistical Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06154)
*Shutong Zheng,Sijia Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPEAR，一种完全无需训练的联邦图学习框架，将一次性联邦图学习转化为统计估计问题，通过自适应收缩估计器聚合拓扑平滑的类原型，在极端非同分布条件下显著提升精度并实现数量级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一次性联邦图学习方法都假设局部GNN训练不可或缺，但在极端非IID条件下，局部GNN训练会导致严重的跨客户端表示错位，反而成为主要误差来源而非有效手段。因此需要重新审视该问题范式。

Method: 将一次性联邦图学习重构为统计估计问题，提出SPEAR框架：在原始特征空间中直接从局部图计算拓扑平滑的类原型（无需任何训练），服务器端使用样本量自适应的收缩估计器聚合这些原型，以降低不可靠局部估计的权重，最终生成鲁棒的全局类原型。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SPEAR在极端异质性下持续取得最先进的精度，且相比所有基线至少快一个数量级，对比生成式和蒸馏类方法可达数个数量级的加速。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，无需训练的统计估计方法而非局部GNN优化，才是实现鲁棒高效的一次性联邦图学习的关键所在。

Abstract: One-shot federated graph learning generally aims to train Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) across clients with disconnected subgraphs in a single communication round. Existing methods predominantly design advanced optimization strategies under the premise that local GNN training is indispensable. However, empirical observations reveal that under extreme non-IID conditions, local GNN training suffers from severe cross-client representation misalignment, becoming a major source of error rather than a remedy. Motivated by this, we reformulate one-shot FGL as a statistical estimation problem. We propose SPEAR (Statistical Prototype Estimation with Adaptive Reliability), a completely training-free framework that directly computes topology-smoothed class prototypes from local graphs in the original feature space. The server then aggregates these prototypes using a sample-size-adaptive shrinkage estimator that down-weights unreliable local estimates, producing robust global class prototypes. Extensive experiments across seven benchmarks demonstrate that SPEAR consistently achieves state-of-the-art accuracy under extreme heterogeneity. Moreover, SPEAR delivers at least an order-of-magnitude speedup over all baselines, reaching several orders of magnitude against generative and distillation-based methods. Our findings suggest that training-free statistical estimation, rather than local GNN optimization, provides the key to robust and efficient one-shot federated graph learning. The code is available at https://github.com/Yodeesy/SPEAR .

</details>


### [247] [All for 1-Bit: Towards Genuine 1-Bit Post-Training Quantization for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06161)
*Zhixiong Zhao,Zukang Xu,Guangyu Sun,Lifeng Liu,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AF1框架，实现LLM真正的1-bit后训练量化（PTQ），通过Null-space-Aware Binary Factorization和Hierarchical Shapley Allocation两个组件，在严格的1.0-BPW预算下保持模型精度，相比BF16可实现2.5倍推理加速和90%以上的内存缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型参数量大、存储和内存带宽需求高，部署效率受限。权重二值化是可行方案，但现有基于二值化的PTQ方法由于隐藏开销往往远超名义上的1-bit存储目标，无法实现真正的1-bit压缩。

Method: AF1包含两个互补组件：(1) Null-space-Aware Binary Factorization (NABF)，通过Hessian感知的替代重参数化、零空间感知的二值因式分解和仅规模全局重建来提升二值重建质量；(2) Hierarchical Shapley Allocation (HiSA)，利用层次化Shapley敏感性分配结构容量。

Result: 在LLaMA、Qwen和Gemma系列模型上的实验表明，AF1在困惑度和零样本准确率上一致优于现有二值化PTQ方法。相比BF16，AF1平均实现2.5倍推理加速和超过90%的内存缩减。

Conclusion: AF1提供了一条在PTQ设置下实现真正1-bit LLM压缩的实用路径，在严格1.0-BPW预算内保持模型精度并显著提升部署效率，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable progress, yet their massive storage and memory-bandwidth demands still hinder efficient deployment. Weight binarization is a promising solution, but existing binarization-based post-training quantization (PTQ) methods usually far exceed the nominal 1-bit storage target due to hidden overhead. To address this gap, we propose All for 1-Bit (AF1), a genuine 1-bit PTQ framework for LLMs. AF1 comprises two complementary components: (1) Null-space-Aware Binary Factorization (NABF) for improving binary reconstruction through Hessian-aware surrogate reparameterization, null-space-aware binary factorization, and scale-only global reconstruction; and (2) Hierarchical Shapley Allocation (HiSA) for assigning structural capacity using hierarchical Shapley sensitivity. Together, they preserve model accuracy under a strict 1.0-BPW budget in the PTQ setting. Experiments on LLaMA, Qwen, and Gemma families show that AF1 consistently outperforms existing binarization-based PTQ methods in perplexity and zero-shot accuracy. Compared with BF16, AF1 achieves an average 2.5 times inference speedup and over 90% memory reduction across evaluated models, providing a practical path toward deployable genuine 1-bit compression for LLMs. The code for reproducibility is available at https://github.com/Kishon-zzx/AF1.

</details>


### [248] [Decision-Aware Suffix Prediction and Reasoning of Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06169)
*Henryk Mustroph,Stefanie Rinderle-Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将决策挖掘与后缀预测相结合的新型框架（neuro-symbolic方法），通过挖掘决策规则来增强神经网络后缀预测器的推理能力。实验表明，该框架在四个事件日志中的三个上显著改善了后缀预测效果，尤其对短前缀和稀有流程变体表现突出，同时引入了内在可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经网络的后缀预测方法在短前缀或稀有流程变体上表现不佳，因为这类场景需要依据案例和事件级别的属性进行多重分支决策，而基于密集事件标签训练的NN模型往往低估了这些属性信号。决策挖掘虽然能从事件日志中提取此类决策规则，但此前仅用于事后分析和假设分析，尚未被用于后缀预测任务。

Method: 作者提出了一个决策感知的后缀预测框架，将决策挖掘集成到后缀预测流程中。该框架采用神经符号（neuro-symbolic）方法，一方面利用神经网络进行序列学习，另一方面通过决策挖掘得到的规则对预测事件进行逻辑推理和约束，从而在分支决策点上提供更符合日志真实逻辑的预测引导。

Result: 在三个后缀预测器和四个事件日志上的实验显示，该框架在四个事件日志中的三个上能够显著提升后缀预测效果，特别是对短前缀的预测提升明显，同时也改善了稀有流程变体的预测准确性，并额外带来了内在的可解释性。

Conclusion: 决策挖掘技术可以有效地扩展到后缀预测任务中，通过与神经网络的结合形成神经符号框架，能够弥补纯神经网络模型在属性信号利用不足方面的缺陷，为流程预测领域提供了兼具性能与可解释性的新思路。

Abstract: Suffix prediction forecasts the remaining sequence of events of a running case until completion. Most approaches rely on neural networks trained on event logs, which, on average, perform well but struggle with short prefixes or targets belonging to a rare process variant. In such scenarios, the correct path may cross multiple branching decisions, determined primarily by case- and event-level attributes, a signal that NN-based suffix prediction models tend to underweight because they may heavily weight (dense) event labels. Decision mining extracts rules for such decisions from the event log, but has so far been applied only to post-hoc and what-if analysis, not suffix prediction. We therefore extend suffix prediction with decision mining, introducing a decision-aware suffix prediction framework, a neuro-symbolic approach that enables reasoning about predicted events via mined decision rules. Experiments on three of four event logs and three suffix predictors show that the framework can improve suffix prediction, especially for short prefixes but also for rare process variants, and adds intrinsic interpretability.

</details>


### [249] [FMMO: Detecting the Divergence Between Local Attribution and Global Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06173)
*Muhammad Rehman Zafar,Ali El-Sharif,Naimul Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模型监控与可观测性框架（FMMO），用于暴露局部解释稳定性与全局分布漂移之间的分歧。研究发现局部XAI方法（如TreeSHAP）在性能退化时可能呈现误导性的稳定性，无法检测到受保护群体假阳性率骤增的公平性盲点，而FMMO通过整合全局代理模型与模型利用率测量来缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型部署后发生漂移时，特别是当真实标签延迟导致性能退化成为"静默失败"时，会严重威胁算法问责制。虽然可解释AI（XAI）常被用于审计这些漂移，但流行的局部归因方法可能表现出误导性的稳定性，即使模型可靠性已经崩溃。这导致公平性退化（如对受保护群体的歧视性影响）无法被现有局部XAI工具及时发现。

Method: 提出FMMO（模型监控与可观测性框架），通过将全局代理模型与模型利用率测量相结合，来揭示局部解释稳定性与全局分布漂移之间的差异，从而弥补局部XAI方法在监测漂移引起的差异性影响方面的盲区。

Result: 在基准、合成及真实世界数据集上的实验表明，局部XAI方法（特别是TreeSHAP）在特征归因保持不变的场景下，无法标记出受保护群体假阳性率骤升的漂移所导致的差异性影响；而FMMO能够有效检测这类标准局部XAI工具所忽视的歧视性退化。

Conclusion: FMMO提供了一个更可靠的模型漂移监测方案，使利益相关者能够检测到被标准局部XAI工具遗漏的歧视性模型退化，弥补了现有局部解释方法在公平性监测上的关键盲点。

Abstract: Post-deployment drift poses a critical risk to algorithmic accountability, particularly when ground truth labels are delayed and performance degradation becomes a "silent failure". While Explainable AI (XAI) is often relied upon to audit these shifts, we demonstrate that popular local attribution methods (e.g., TreeSHAP) can exhibit misleading stability even as model reliability collapses. In this paper, we propose a Framework for Model Monitoring and Observability (FMMO) designed to expose the divergence between local explanation stability and global distribution shifts. Using benchmark, synthetic, and real-world datasets, we show that local XAI methods fail to flag drift-induced disparate impact, specifically where False Positive Rates spike for protected groups while feature attributions remain unchanged. By integrating global surrogate models with model utilization measurements, FMMO mitigates this fairness blind spot, ensuring that stakeholders can detect discriminatory deterioration that standard local XAI tools overlook.

</details>


### [250] [Spectral Prioritized Sweeping in Nonstationary Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06186)
*Hung Pham,Tuan Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出图拓扑增强框架（GTA-PS，亦称谱优先清扫SPS），通过扩散Bellman残差信息来解决非平稳奖励设定下优先清扫（PS）算法中优先级评分目光短浅的问题，在多步推进效率上优于标准PS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准优先清扫（PS）算法在非平稳奖励环境中存在短视问题：当局部奖励发生偏移后，残差只能通过已实现的备份逐步传播，导致受瓶颈限制或拓扑距离较远的状态估计在有限重规划预算下可能长期保持静态，算法无法快速适应变化。

Method: 提出Graph Topology Augmentation框架：(1)利用图的自伴算子（Resolvent）及其扩散语义扩充查询信号；(2)采用平滑化策略诱导转移链，并结合链的入/出Laplacian；(3)将正则化Laplacian逆的混合扩散残差幅度作为标准优先级键的增强项；(4)基于第二大特征值模（SLEM）的调度器对拓扑贡献进行退火，使其适应链的混合状态；(5)该排序分数可直接替换标准PS的排序分数，适用于固定动力学、奖励变化的设定。

Result: 理论证明了前向势能与几何折扣残差传播一致，并说明GTA-PS能即时赋予所有状态活跃优先级。在FourRooms和GARNET域的表格实验中，GTA-PS在精确动态规划（DP）和Dyna风格宿主规划器下均展现出优于标准PS的重规划效率。

Conclusion: GTA-PS通过图谱理论与扩散语义有效克服了标准PS在非平稳奖励环境中的残差传播瓶颈，提供了一种可直接落地的改进版优先级排序方案，显著提升重规划效率。

Abstract: Prioritized Sweeping (PS) accelerates model-based reinforcement learning by selecting backups according to Bellman residual magnitude. In nonstationary reward settings, however, the canonical priority score is shortsighted: after a localized reward shift, residuals propagate only through realized backups, so bottlenecked or topologically distant state estimates may remain static under a limited replanning budget. We introduce the Graph Topology Augmentation framework, which employ the graph's resolvent and its diffusion semantic, to augment the inquired signal. Our application, Graph Topology Augmentation for Prioritized Sweeping (GTA-PS), or which the alias Spectral Prioritized Sweeping (SPS) might be more universal, provides a drop-in ordering score for the setting of fixed dynamics and changing state rewards. GTA-PS uses a smootherized policy, inducing a transition chain, with its in- and out-Laplacian. The standard priority key is augmented with a mixing of regularized Laplacian inverses diffusing the residual magnitude. Furthermore, the topology contribution is annealed by a scheduler based on the Second Largest Eigenvalue Modulus (SLEM), allowing its scale to adapt to the chain's mixing regime. We prove that the forward potential coincides with geometric discounted residual propagation and show that GTA-PS gives active priority instantly to all states. Tabular experiments on FourRooms and GARNET domains demonstrate improved replanning efficiency over standard PS under both exact DP and Dyna-style host planners.

</details>


### [251] [EgoNeMo: Transferable Map of Pedestrian Dynamics via Egocentric LiDAR Scan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06195)
*Azusa Sawada,Allan Wang,Hideo Saito,Aaron Steinfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可迁移的动力学地图（MoD）框架，仅利用以自我为中心的3D LiDAR点云即可泛化到未知环境，解决了传统MoD方法依赖于逐地点积累轨迹数据的局限。通过神经隐式建模、位置平衡采样策略和多任务学习架构（联合预测运动分布与空间频率得分图），该方法即使在训练数据高度稀疏的情况下，也能从单次瞬时LiDAR扫描中有效重建未知位置的运动地图，并提升下游轨迹预测的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoD方法虽然对人运动特征编码和行人轨迹预测至关重要，但具有站点特异性，需要在每个新位置进行详尽的轨迹数据积累，这严重限制了其在实际应用中的泛化能力，无法适应未知环境。

Method: 1) 利用神经隐式建模训练一个连续的、基于LiDAR的MoD估计器，覆盖多样环境；2) 提出位置平衡采样策略，缓解真实轨迹数据的稀疏性和时间偏差；3) 设计多任务学习架构，联合预测运动分布和空间频率得分图；4) 引入可见性感知损失，补偿不完整观测数据带来的影响。

Result: 实验表明该方法即使在高度稀疏的训练数据下，也能从未知位置的单次瞬时LiDAR扫描中有效重建底层运动地图，且改进措施增强了下游轨迹预测的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功打破了传统MoD方法的站点依赖限制，证明了从单帧LiDAR感知即可实现跨未知环境的运动地图迁移与重建，为行人轨迹预测和机器人导航提供了更具泛化能力的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a transferable Map of Dynamics (MoD) framework that generalizes to unknown environments using only egocentric 3D LiDAR point clouds to overcome the long-standing limitation of traditional MoD methods. While MoDs are essential for encoding human motion characteristics to enable accurate pedestrian trajectory prediction or safe robot navigation, traditional approaches suffer from site-specificity, requiring exhaustive trajectory accumulation at every new location. Extending recent advances in neural implicit modeling, our framework trains a continuous, LiDAR-based MoD estimator across diverse environments. To mitigate the inherent sparsity and temporal bias of real-world trajectory data, we introduce a position-balanced sampling strategy and a multi-task learning architecture that jointly predicts motion distributions and a spatial frequency score map. The latter is further augmented by visibility-aware losses to compensate for incomplete observation data. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our method effectively reconstructs underlying motion maps even in unknown locations from a single instantaneous LiDAR scan, despite highly sparse training data. Finally, we show that our improvements enhance the reliability of downstream trajectory prediction.

</details>


### [252] [SeaCausal-FL: Federated Fuzzy Causal Learning for Maritime IoT Fault Diagnosis and Counterfactual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06257)
*Yuhang Qiu,Haihan Zhu,Koteeswaran Seerangan,Longsheng Zhu,Xiong Wang,Yijun Lu,Zheng Lin,Fangmin Ren,Jialiang Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SeaCausal-FL，一种用于海事物联网环境下船舶发动机故障诊断的联邦模糊因果学习框架。它将共享的时间诊断路径与机制条件因果推理相结合，通过区间二型模糊层刻画不确定且重叠的运行机制，每个机制关联一个受物理约束的结构因果模型。实验表明其在F1、AUROC、AUPRC等指标上表现优异，并在未见负载、故障类型缺失及因果基准测试中保持良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事物联网中的船舶发动机故障诊断面临三大挑战：数据分布式拥有导致的数据孤岛问题、不同客户端间故障分布异构问题，以及运行工况持续变化带来的诊断模型泛化难题。现有联邦学习方法难以应对这些复杂约束。

Method: 提出SeaCausal-FL联邦模糊因果学习框架，包含四个核心组件：共享时间诊断路径用于提取时序特征；区间二型模糊层表示不确定且重叠的运行机制；每个机制关联一个物理约束的结构因果模型；聚合前通过运行上下文、因果结构和条件干预响应特征对齐局部学习到的机制。模型参数按样本、类别、机制及机制类别证据进行加权聚合，并通过溯因-行动-预测流程实现区间反事实推理。

Result: 在船舶发动机故障数据集上，SeaCausal-FL在四个客户端分区下平均F1达到87.07%，AUROC为98.98%，AUPRC为94.81%。在未见负载和训练时故障类型缺失的情况下仍保持强性能。在因果基准上，Edge-F1约0.58，Edge-AUPRC为0.68，系数RMSE降至约0.14，反事实估计和干预决策效果良好。

Conclusion: SeaCausal-FL通过融合联邦学习、模糊推理与因果建模，有效解决了分布式、异构和动态工况下的船舶发动机故障诊断问题，在诊断准确性和因果可解释性之间取得了良好平衡，为海事物联网中的可靠故障诊断提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reliable marine-engine fault diagnosis in maritime IoT is challenged by distributed data ownership, heterogeneous fault distributions, and continuously changing operating conditions. This paper proposes SeaCausal-FL, a federated fuzzy causal learning framework that combines a shared temporal diagnostic path with mechanism-conditioned causal reasoning. An interval type-2 fuzzy layer represents uncertain and overlapping operating mechanisms, while each mechanism is associated with a physics-constrained structural causal model. Before aggregation, locally learned mechanisms are aligned using operating context, causal structure, and conditional intervention-response signatures. Model parameters are then aggregated according to sample, class, mechanism, and mechanism-class evidence instead of client sample size alone. The learned structural equations further support interval counterfactual reasoning through abduction, action, and prediction. Experiments on a marine-engine fault dataset and a real-data-calibrated semi-synthetic causal benchmark show that SeaCausal-FL achieves an average F1 score of 87.07% across four client partitions, with AUROC and AUPRC of 98.98% and 94.81%, respectively. It also maintains strong performance under unseen loads and fault-type omission during training. On the causal benchmark, SeaCausal-FL reaches an Edge-F1 of approximately 0.58 and an Edge-AUPRC of 0.68, reduces coefficient RMSE to about 0.14, and provides favorable counterfactual estimation and intervention decisions.

</details>


### [253] [Representation Learning for Sample-Efficient CATE Estimation by Leveraging Multiple Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06294)
*Maitreyi Swaroop,Shikha Bhat,Samantha Rodriguez,Tamar Krishnamurti,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用历史数据中的多结果辅助信息，学习高维协变量的低维表示，以在有限实验样本下更高效地估计条件平均处理效应（CATE），并从理论（识别性、偏差-方差权衡）和实证（合成与半合成医疗数据）两方面验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计CATE对于精准干预至关重要，但在实验样本有限且协变量高维的情况下，直接估计异质性效应面临维度灾难，而现成的降维方法可能破坏处理异质性。然而，许多领域拥有大量测量多种结果的历史数据，这些监督信息在实际中很少被利用，本文旨在挖掘这一未被充分利用的监督来源。

Method: 遵循因果表征学习的思路，假设高维协变量域存在低维潜在动力学。利用历史数据中测量的多种辅助结果作为监督信号，学习协变量的低维表征，并用该表征替代原始高维协变量进行CATE估计。理论部分证明了在辅助结果满足代理条件和表征保留相关协变量信息时，CATE仍可被识别，并刻画了假设不完全成立时的偏差-方差权衡。

Result: 理论上证明了在给定代理条件和表征充分性的前提下，基于学习表征的CATE估计与原始CATE一致；结合已有维度相关收敛速率，表明在相同实验样本上具有更高的样本效率。同时，表征估计器在压缩引入的偏差小于方差缩减量时，仍可取得更低的整体误差。实证上在合成数据和半合成医疗数据上均验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 利用历史数据中的多样化结果作为辅助监督来学习低维表征，是应对高维协变量下CATE估计样本效率不足问题的一种有效途径，具有坚实的理论保障和良好的实证表现。

Abstract: Estimating conditional average treatment effects (CATE) enables efficient targeting of interventions, but many applications have limited experimental samples, making it difficult to estimate heterogeneous effects from high-dimensional covariates. In such settings, policymakers and medical practitioners often succumb to the curse of dimensionality or apply off-the-shelf dimension reduction methods that may not preserve treatment heterogeneity. Yet these domains often come with large historical datasets measuring a wide range of outcomes -- a source of supervision that is rarely exploited in practice. Following causal representation learning, we hypothesize that such domains with high-dimensional covariates have lower-dimensional underlying dynamics. We can thus leverage the diverse outcomes measured in historical data to learn a lower-dimensional representation of the covariates. Theoretically, we prove that when the auxiliary outcomes satisfy a set of surrogacy conditions and the representation retains relevant covariate information, the original CATE is identified when the high-dimensional covariates are replaced by the learned representation. Combined with existing dimension-dependent rates for CATE estimation, the result implies greater sample-efficiency on the same experimental sample. Additionally, we characterize the bias-variance tradeoff when the assumptions do not hold perfectly, and show that the representation-based estimator can still achieve lower error when the reduction in estimator variance outweighs the bias due to compression. Empirically, we evaluate the method on synthetic data and semi-synthetic medical data.

</details>


### [254] [Linear Algebra Foundations of Efficient Attention: A Phase Reversal in Rank Collapse Under SVD Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06341)
*Anjaneya Teja Sarma Kalvakolanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文统一了十四篇关于线性代数技术（矩阵秩、SVD、特征分解）在Transformer基础模型研究中的应用工作，聚焦于自注意力矩阵输出秩、利用该现象的压缩方法，以及低秩KV缓存投影与线性注意力/状态空间模型的半可分矩阵对偶性三大主题。作者发现了一个原始结论：使用SVD压缩注意力投影对网络秩坍缩的影响是双向的——在初始化时强烈抑制秩坍缩，但在预训练模型上反而加速；通过受控因果分解，发现该行为的根源在于SVD压缩时选择的子空间，而非算子范数的减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到该领域存在一个开放问题：现有压缩方法与网络自然秩坍缩现象之间的相互作用尚未被充分理解，因而开展本项工作来系统梳理并解答该问题。

Method: 作者统一分析了十四篇同行评审工作，并在GPT-2 124M、GPT-2 Medium 355M和Pythia-160M模型上进行了实验，使用SVD压缩注意力投影，通过四种秩估计方法评估秩坍缩变化，并采用受控因果分解来分离子空间选择与算子范数减少两个因素的影响。

Result: SVD压缩在初始化时强烈抑制网络秩坍缩，但在预训练模型上反而加速秩坍缩；在所有压缩比下均未出现目标混叠伪影。因果分解显示，SVD压缩时选择的子空间比算子范数的减少更能解释该行为，在初始化时解释了约76%的效果，在预训练权重上解释了约83%。

Conclusion: 该发现为校准感知压缩视角提供了细化，解释了为何该方法优于朴素的SVD截断，并为低秩压缩与秩坍缩之间的复杂交互提供了新的理论洞见。

Abstract: Linear algebra provides the framework of concepts (matrix rank, singular value decomposition (SVD), and eigendecomposition) that modern artificial intelligence employs to encode, compress, and propagate information through neural networks. This paper unifies fourteen separate peer-reviewed works analyzing the usage of these techniques in the context of transformer-based foundation model research, focusing on three areas of the topic: derivations and properties of self-attention matrices' output rank, compression methods that purposefully utilize this phenomenon, and the low-rank key-value (KV) cache projection and its semiseparable-matrix duality to linear attention and state-space structured models. We were motivated to conduct this work after observing an open problem in this literature: the interplay of the mentioned compression methods with natural rank collapse of the network. With this paper, we report an original finding that using SVD compression of attention projections actually has the opposite effect on the rank collapse of the network: while it strongly suppresses it at initialization, it accelerates on pretrained models (for GPT-2 124M, GPT-2 Medium 355M, and Pythia-160M) with minimal risk of object aliasing artifacts appearing (verified on all compression ratios) and is consistent across four rank estimation methods. A controlled causal decomposition of the effect in both settings showed that the reason for this behavior can be explained by the choice of the subspace SVD makes when compressing the matrix better than the reduction of the operator norm it achieves, explaining roughly 76% of the effect at initialization and 83% on the pretrained weights, providing a refinement to the calibration-aware compression viewpoint and an explanation of why it outperformed naive SVD truncation.

</details>


### [255] [Robust Dynamic Expansion for Continual Learning under Backdoor Attacks via Purification and Selective Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06346)
*Keyu Lin,Fei Ye,Qihe Liu,Shijie Zhou,Jiguo Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了持续学习在遭受后门攻击时的挑战性设定（CLUBA），提出一个动态扩展的鲁棒框架，通过样本纯化、选择性恢复和鲁棒专家路由三个模块，同时缓解灾难性遗忘、保持适应能力并防止恶意监督吸收。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际场景中从不可信来源收集的任务流可能包含被后门投毒的数据样本，这对持续学习者的稳定性、可塑性和安全性构成严重挑战。传统持续学习或后门防御方法无法同时解决遗忘、适应性和安全问题。

Method: 提出鲁棒动态扩展框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) Bi-Prototype Purification (BPP) 利用特征空间语义差异识别可疑样本；(2) Gradient Discrepancy-based Robustness Optimization (GDBRO) 通过伪标签校正和梯度一致性评估选择性恢复信息丰富的投毒样本；(3) Robust Feature Consistency-based Expert Selection (RFCBES) 构建扰动感知类别原型以实现在污染或偏移输入下的可靠专家路由。

Result: 论文摘要中未明确报告具体实验数值结果，但框架设计旨在同时解决三方面目标：缓解灾难性遗忘、保持模型适应能力（可塑性）以及防止恶意监督被吸收，从而在CLUBA设定下提升模型的鲁棒性和安全性。

Conclusion: 该工作将后门攻击防御与持续学习相结合，提出了一个统一的鲁棒框架来处理CLUBA问题，为在不可信数据流场景下构建安全可靠的持续学习系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) enables models to acquire new knowledge from sequentially arriving tasks while retaining previously learned knowledge. However, in practical scenarios, task streams collected from untrusted sources may contain backdoor-poisoned samples, posing a critical challenge to the stability, plasticity, and security of continual learners. In this work, we investigate a challenging setting termed Continual Learning Under Backdoor Attack (CLUBA), where each incremental task may involve a small proportion of maliciously manipulated training samples. Unlike conventional continual learning or backdoor defense scenarios, CLUBA requires models to simultaneously mitigate catastrophic forgetting, preserve adaptation capability, and prevent the absorption of malicious supervision during sequential updates. To address this challenge, we propose a robust dynamic-expansion framework that integrates sample purification, selective recovery, and robust expert routing into a unified continual learning paradigm. Specifically, we introduce Bi-Prototype Purification (BPP) to identify suspicious samples by exploiting semantic discrepancies in feature space. Based on purified data, Gradient Discrepancy-based Robustness Optimization (GDBRO) selectively recovers informative poisoned samples through pseudo-label correction and gradient consistency evaluation, improving robustness while maintaining model plasticity. Furthermore, Robust Feature Consistency-based Expert Selection (RFCBES) constructs perturbation-aware class prototypes to enable reliable expert routing under corrupted or shifted inputs.

</details>


### [256] [Robust Conformal Consensus: Multi-Agent LLM-as-a-Judge Interval Evaluation with Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06367)
*Lihui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多智能体LLM-as-a-Judge评估的鲁棒不确定性估计框架，通过从多个不同的LLM评估器中构建共形预测区间，获得更稳定可靠的评估不确定性估计，实验证明了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge范式在自然语言生成评估中前景广阔，但其评估相关的不确定性尚未被充分探索，限制了实际应用中的可靠性。现有共形预测方法通常仅应用于单个LLM评估器，忽视了不同LLM评估器之间引入的变异性。

Method: 提出一个用于多智能体LLM-as-a-Judge评估的鲁棒不确定性估计框架。该方法从多个不同的LLM评估器中构建共形预测区间（conformal prediction intervals），并综合考虑来自不同LLM评估器的区间以获得更稳定可靠的评估结果。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提方法能够产生具有覆盖率保证的有效预测区间，且基于区间的多评估器聚合策略带来了更稳定的评估结果。

Conclusion: 利用多LLM评估器并结合共形预测区间，可以显著提升LLM-as-a-Judge评估的可靠性和稳定性，为不确定性量化提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge has emerged as a promising paradigm for evaluating natural language generation. However, the uncertainty associated with such evaluations remains largely unexplored, which limits their reliability in real-world applications. Although conformal prediction offers a principled framework for uncertainty quantification, existing approaches typically apply it to a single LLM judge, overlooking the variability introduced by using different LLM evaluators. In this work, we propose a robust uncertainty estimation framework for multi-agent LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation. Our approach constructs conformal prediction intervals for LLM-based scores from multiple LLMs. By considering intervals from different LLM judges, we obtain more stable and reliable uncertainty estimates. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method produces valid prediction intervals with coverage guarantees, and that interval-based aggregation across multiple judges leads to more stable evaluation outcomes.

</details>


### [257] [Parameterized and Streaming Algorithms for Euclidean Fair $k$-Center Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06384)
*Zeyu Lin,Chaoqi Jia,Longkun Guo,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了欧几里得空间中公平k-中心聚类问题，提出了参数化近似算法（近似比为2.732），并将其集成到单遍流式框架中得到4.464的近似比；通过指数级运行时进一步改善至2.414和3.828，同时设计了多项式时间单遍流式算法，近似比可达4.732并进一步优化至4.42，优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中公平性日益重要，公平k-中心聚类作为基础问题受到广泛关注。现有研究多聚焦于一般度量空间，而欧几里得空间在机器学习应用中无处不在，需要针对该场景设计更高效的公平聚类算法，尤其是在大规模数据处理需求下。

Method: 首先为欧几里得公平k-中心问题设计了参数化近似算法（近似比2.732）；将其作为后处理阶段嵌入单遍流式框架，得到4.464的近似比；通过允许运行时在k上呈指数增长，将比值分别改善至2.414和3.828；为保证多项式时间复杂度，另外设计了单遍流式算法，实现4.732的近似比并进一步改进至4.42。

Result: 参数化算法达到2.732近似比，流式框架达到4.464；指数运行时版本分别达到2.414和3.828；多项式时间单遍流式算法达到4.732并可改进至4.42，超越当前最先进水平；实验表明所提方法在聚类精度上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地解决了欧几里得公平k-中心聚类问题，在近似比、时间复杂度和大规模数据处理之间取得了良好平衡，所提算法在理论和实验层面均优于现有最先进方法，为大规模公平聚类提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Motivated by the growing importance of fairness in machine learning, fair $k$-center clustering has attracted considerable research attention as a fundamental problem. In this problem, a dataset is partitioned into $m$ disjoint groups, and the objective is to select $k$ data points as centers, subject to upper bounds on the number of centers chosen from each group, aiming to minimize the maximum distance between any data point and its assigned center. Focusing on Euclidean spaces, which are ubiquitous in machine learning applications, we first develop a parameterized approximation algorithm for Euclidean fair $k$-center with an approximation ratio of $2.732$. By incorporating this algorithm as a post-processing stage into a one-pass streaming framework for large-scale data, we obtain an approximation ratio of $4.464$. These ratios can be further respectively improved to $2.414$ and $3.828$ with a runtime exponential on $k$. To ensure polynomial-time complexity, we further design a one-pass streaming algorithm with an approximation ratio of $4.732$, which can be further improved to $4.42$, outperforming the state-of-the-art ratio. Finally, extensive experiments show that our methods significantly outperform state-of-the-art approaches in terms of clustering accuracy.

</details>


### [258] [Are Verifier Errors Independent Within a GRPO Group? Evidence from Qwen2.5 Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06386)
*Esther Xin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对组级RLVR中的验证器误差相关性进行了实证分析。基于Qwen2.5-1.5B在MATH、GSM8K和DeepMath-103K上生成的24,998组（每组8个完整回答），发现组内验证器误差相关性为0.530（95% CI: 0.500-0.560），等效样本量为1.70。相关性随答案形式不同而变化，分数、根式、符号表达式和区间形式的聚类更强。研究结果主张采用提示和答案形式感知的验证器噪声分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的组级RLVR分析基于独立验证器误差假设，忽视了共享答案格式所引入的依赖关系，可能导致对系统性能的错误估计。

Method: 使用Qwen2.5-1.5B在三个数据集上生成24,998组、每组8个完整回答，估计合并的组内验证器误差相关性，并在可交换误差模型下计算等效样本量。通过重放四种基于规则的验证器配置的组级相对优势，分析答案形式间依赖差异及优势符号不一致情况。

Result: 组内验证器误差相关性为0.530（95% CI: 0.500-0.560），八完成组等效样本量为1.70。依赖程度因答案形式而异：分数、根式、符号表达式和区间表现出比单位标注和百分号更强的聚类。在最多0.83%的组中检测到至少一次优势符号不一致。

Conclusion: 组内聚类可能同时反映共享的提示难度和共享的答案形式，研究不试图区分二者。研究结果支持采用提示和答案形式感知的验证器噪声分析方法，而非仅基于聚合错误率的描述。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) scoresmultiple completions per prompt using automatic verifiers. Analysesbased on independent verifier errors may overlook dependence associatedwith shared answer formats. We investigate this dependence in24,998 groups of eight completions generated by Qwen2.5-1.5B onMATH, GSM8K, and DeepMath-103K. We estimate a pooled within-groupverifier-error correlation of 0.530 (95% confidence interval:0.500--0.560). Under an exchangeable-error model, this correspondsto a design-effect-adjusted effective sample size of 1.70 for aneight-completion group. Dependence varies substantially across answerforms: fractions, radicals, symbolic expressions, and intervals exhibitstronger clustering than unit annotations and percent signs. Replayinggroup-relative advantages across four rule-based verifier configurationsidentifies at least one advantage-sign disagreement in up to 0.83% ofgroups. Because a group is repeated sampling for one prompt, thiswithin-group clustering may reflect shared prompt difficulty as well asshared answer form, and we do not attempt to separate the two here.Unlike studies of correlated judgments across multiple evaluators, ouranalysis examines dependence across completions scored by the sameverifier. These findings motivate prompt- and answer-form-aware analysesof verifier noise rather than characterizations based solely onaggregate error rates.

</details>


### [259] [MetaRSI / RSI2: A Meta-Recursive Self-Improving System for Recursive Self-Improving Systems Themselves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06396)
*Zihan Tan,Leixin Sun,Zitong Shi,Yitao Liu,Jiajun Wu,Nathaniel Brooks,Jiaru Qian,Xiaoran Shang,Suyuan Huang,Yi Ding,Yangxu Liao,Mukai Li,Qiushi Sun,Shudong Liu,Xuankun Rong,Xiaohang Yu,Zhuo Chen,Hejia Geng,Chenxin Li,Aozhou Wang,Zengji Tu,Robert Tang,Yuxin Zhan,Eric Jiang,Yuxin Wu,Jianqing Zhang,Xiao Liang,Fang Wu,Haochi Zhang,Alexander Marlow,Guancheng Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaRSI-v1框架，将递归自我改进（RSI）从仅限于编码和形式化基准扩展到更广泛的科学、工程及元科学领域。该框架将改进定义为三个类型化算子（Data-RSI、Harness-RSI、Model-RSI）在统一范式上的调度组合，通过双轴优化器联合决定算子顺序与策略，验证了在无外部教师条件下代码与封闭式科学任务上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RSI研究几乎全部局限于编码和形式化基准（如科学问答和数学），这种格式束缚使RSI只能在机器可检查的范围内改进，无法推广到答案开放、需通过论证/复现/测量来验证正确性的一般性能力领域。作者认为RSI应扩展到真实多样的科学、工程和元科学领域。

Method: 提出MetaRSI-v1框架，包含三个类型化算子：(1) Data-RSI——放大现有能力并标记其边界；(2) Harness-RSI——编辑五槽脚手架而不触碰权重；(3) Model-RSI——通过有界训练将能力内化到参数中。三者共享同一循环内核和工件词汇表，使数据、脚手架和模型改动可组合。采用双轴优化器联合决定算子顺序和每个算子的提案策略，元级策略跨周期修订调度。验证中目标模型在自身循环中扮演所有角色，无外部教师。

Result: 在领域标准评估（代码和封闭式科学任务）上验证了MetaRSI-v1的有效性。该框架将自我改进从单一表面的编辑重构为整个模型生产流程的组合，开辟了两条路径：模型路线（通过训练内化能力）和脚手架路线（不触碰权重，因此可将自我改进延伸到任何可通过接口访问的模型），同时将Data-RSI重新定义为两条路径共享的基础数据源。

Conclusion: MetaRSI-v1将RSI从单一表面编辑转变为跨完整模型生产流水线的组合，并导出了关于循环存在位置、算子如何组合以及监督能带来什么的可反驳性规律，为RSI向一般性能力的扩展提供了统一的框架基础。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) lets a system improve the model-building machinery from its own failures, so every later model inherits the gain. Yet RSI has been validated almost exclusively on coding and formal benchmarks such as science QA and mathematics. This format bound limits RSI to improvement within a machine-checkable slice, not general capability where questions are open and correctness is settled by argument, replication, or measurement. We argue RSI must next operate across real, diverse scientific, engineering, and meta-scientific domains, not where formal evaluation is merely tractable. To that end we present MetaRSI-v1, where improvement is the scheduled composition of three typed operators over one unified paradigm. Data-RSI amplifies existing competence and marks its boundary; Harness-RSI edits a five-slot scaffold without touching weights; Model-RSI internalizes capability into parameters through bounded training. Sharing one loop kernel and artifact vocabulary, they make data, scaffold, and model changes composable rather than exclusive. A two-axis optimizer jointly decides operator order and each operator's proposal policy, while a meta-level policy revises the schedule across terms. We validate MetaRSI-v1 under the field's standard evaluations, on code and closed-form science, with no external teacher: the target model plays every role in its own loop. MetaRSI-v1 reframes self-improvement from a single-surface edit to a composition across the full model-production pipeline, opening two paths: a model route internalizing capability through training, and a harness route leaving weights untouched and thus extending self-improvement to any model reachable through an interface, with Data-RSI redefined as the shared substrate feeding both. The framework further yields refutable laws on where loops exist, how operators compose, and what supervision buys.

</details>


### [260] [On BatchNorm Forward Modes in Value-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06421)
*Daniel Palenicek,Mikael Henaff,Scott Fujimoto,Koustuv Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了批归一化（BN）在离散动作价值学习（Atari任务）中的配置问题。针对目标C51和无目标PQN算法，研究发现BN在特定前向传播中使用批量统计而非运行统计可以消除其性能退化问题。C51模型中，将自举前向切换到批量统计模式显著超越未归一化和LayerNorm基线，且能稳定扩展到12的更新-数据比；PQN中，动作选择和自举均使用批量统计可恢复性能。在26个Atari游戏、4亿帧下，该配置的总体得分高于使用LayerNorm的PQN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批归一化在连续控制actor-critic方法（如CrossQ）中显著提升样本效率，但在离散动作价值学习的Atari任务上出现性能退化，这与CrossQ识别出的动作输入分布失配无关（离散Q网络不存在此问题）。作者旨在探究这种退化的原因，并找到合适的BN前向传播配置来发挥其在离散动作价值学习中的优势。

Method: 论文系统性地分析了BN在不同前向传播阶段（动作选择、自举/引导计算等）选择运行统计（running statistics）或批量统计（batch statistics）对离散Q学习算法的影响。具体在C51（基于目标网络）和PQN（无目标网络）两种算法上配置BN的前向传播模式进行对比实验，并在26个Atari游戏上以4亿帧进行大规模评估，同时也考察了不同更新-数据比（UTD）下的扩展稳定性。

Result: 在C51中，将BN自举前向切换到批量统计模式显著提升了性能，超越了未归一化和LayerNorm基线，并在更新-数据比高达12时仍能稳定扩展。在PQN中，使用批量统计进行动作选择和自举可从失败的运行统计配置中恢复性能。在26个Atari游戏400M帧的评估中，该配置的最终聚合得分高于使用LayerNorm的PQN。

Conclusion: 精心配置的BN能够显著改善离散动作价值学习的性能，BN的前向传播协议（即在哪些前向传播阶段使用运行统计还是批量统计）是算法规范中不可或缺的重要组成部分，并非简单的归一化层添加即可生效。

Abstract: Batch normalization (BN) substantially improves sample efficiency in continuous-control actor-critic methods such as CrossQ, yet recent studies report performance degradation in discrete-action value learning on Atari. These failures are surprising because discrete Q-networks lack the action-input distribution mismatch identified by CrossQ. We show for target-based C51 and target-free PQN that the simple choice between running and batch statistics at specific forward passes can reverse this degradation. In C51, switching the BN bootstrap forward to batch-statistic mode significantly improves performance over unnormalized and LayerNorm baselines and scales stably with update-to-data ratios up to 12. In PQN, using batch-statistics for both action selection and bootstrapping recovers performance from the failing running-statistic configuration. Across 26 Atari games at 400M frames, this configuration achieves a higher final aggregate score than PQN with LayerNorm. Our results show that carefully configured BN can substantially improve discrete-action value learning, and that its forward protocols are an essential part of the algorithm specification.

</details>


### [261] [Sparse Oblique Rule Boosting for Simpler Additive Rule Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06426)
*Shahrzad Behzadimanesh,Pierre Le Bodic,Geoffrey I. Webb,Mario Boley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种扩展的符号规则集成方法，将传统规则条件从单变量的轴平行阈值命题（x ≥ t）扩展为带可学习稀疏线性变换的命题（x^T w ≥ t），使得决策区域变为具有斜面的广义多面体，从而在保持可解释性的同时降低模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统规则集成依赖轴平行的决策区域，需要精心构造的特征才能以少量规则获得良好精度；当缺少此类特征时，需要增加规则数量和复杂度，损害模型可解释性。

Method: 将规则条件扩展为可学习稀疏权重的线性命题，并提出基于加权逻辑回归的梯度提升学习方法以训练该规则集成模型。

Result: 在14个回归和分类任务上的实验表明，该方法相比竞争基线在保持相当或更好预测精度的同时，实现了更低的模型复杂度。

Conclusion: 该方法在可解释性与准确性之间取得了更优的权衡，并减少了对人工特征工程的依赖。

Abstract: Small additive ensembles of symbolic rules offer interpretable prediction models. Traditionally, these ensembles use rule conditions based on conjunctions of simple threshold propositions $x \geq t$ on a single input variable $x$ and threshold $t$, resulting geometrically in axis-parallel polytopes as decision regions. While this form ensures a high degree of interpretability for individual rules and can be learned efficiently using the gradient boosting approach, it relies on having access to a curated set of expressive input features so that a small ensemble of axis-parallel regions can describe the target variable well. Absent such features, reaching sufficient accuracy requires increasing the number and complexity of individual rules, which diminishes the interpretability of the model. Here, we extend classical rule ensembles by introducing logical propositions with learnable sparse linear transformations of input variables, i.e., propositions of the form $\mathbf{x}^T\mathbf{w} \geq t$, where $\mathbf{w}$ is a learnable sparse weight vector, enabling decision regions as general polyhedrons with oblique faces. We propose a learning method using gradient boosting based on a weighted logistic regression. Empirical results across 14 regression and classification tasks demonstrate that the proposed method achieves lower model complexity than competitive baselines while maintaining similar or better predictive accuracy. Hence, the approach provides a favorable trade-off between interpretability and accuracy and reduces the reliance on manual feature engineering.

</details>


### [262] [Stability and Generalization of Straight-Through Estimators for Training Two-Layer Quantized Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06430)
*Yiming Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从统计学习理论角度研究二元激活网络的直通估计器（STE）训练，通过将饱和输出区域的STE递归等价为凸隐损失上的随机次梯度下降，建立了算法稳定性与泛化性之间的联系，推导出ℓ2平均模型稳定性、泛化界、过剩诱导风险保证以及期望过剩误分类误差的最优阶界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是算法稳定性能否解释由不连续的STE训练规则产生的估计器的统计泛化能力，从而为二元激活网络（如硬件友好的量化神经网络）的训练提供理论保障。

Method: 将零初始化样本级STE递归在饱和输出区域等价为凸隐损失(-yu^Tx)_+上的随机次梯度下降；推导两个耦合更新的精确距离恒等式，证明公共样本映射的近似非扩张性（仅在潜在间隔跨越零点时存在二次缺陷）；将稳定性等距地从潜在向量迁移到完整第一层矩阵以获得泛化界；结合优化界得到过剩诱导风险保证。

Result: 获得显式的ℓ2平均模型稳定性界和泛化界；结合标准优化界得到显式过剩诱导风险保证，在T=n^2时达到O(n^{-1/2})速率；在间隔可分条件下，通过互补论证得到随机单遍STE迭代的期望过剩误分类误差的最优阶O(R^2/(γ^2n))以及相应的多数投票界。

Conclusion: 算法稳定性确实能够解释不连续STE训练规则的统计泛化性，且在间隔可分条件下可获得最优阶的泛化保证，为二元激活网络的训练提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We study the identity straight-through estimator (STE) for training a two-layer binary-activation network with hinge loss from the perspective of Statistical Learning Theory (SLT). Our central question is whether algorithmic stability can explain the statistical generalization of the estimator produced by the discontinuous STE training rule. In the saturated-output regime, the zero-initialized samplewise STE recursion is exactly the stochastic subgradient descent on the convex latent loss $(-yu^\top x)_+$. This representation makes a stability analysis possible. We derive an exact distance identity for two coupled updates and prove approximate non-expansiveness of the common-example map, with a quadratic defect only when the two latent margins straddle zero. We then obtain explicit $\ell_2$ on-average model-stability and generalization bounds, transferring stability isometrically from the latent vector to the full first-layer matrix. Combining stability with a standard optimization bound yields an explicit excess induced-risk guarantee and the rate $O(n^{-1/2})$ when $T=n^2$. Under margin separability, a complementary argument gives the optimal-order $O(R^2/(γ^2n))$ expected excess misclassification error for a randomized one-pass STE iterate and a corresponding majority-vote bound.

</details>


### [263] [A Theoretical Framework for Masked Pretraining (MPT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06460)
*Qi Zhang,Runyu Zhou,Yifei Wang,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个理论框架来分析掩码预训练（MPT）的工作机制，建立了MPT与对比学习之间的理论联系，揭示了掩码隐式创建语义相似正样本对并通过重建损失拉近它们。文章指出了MPT的维度坍缩问题，提出均匀性增强的U-MPT损失来解决该问题，并基于理论分析提出新的掩码策略提升下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码预训练（MPT）作为一种基于重建任务的自监督学习范式在多个领域取得了显著性能，但其背后的工作机制缺乏理论理解，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 本文提出一个新的理论框架来分析与MPT和对比学习之间的内在联系，推导掩码在提取有意义表示中的关键作用；指出MPT的维度坍缩问题并设计均匀性增强的U-MPT损失函数；基于理论分析提出新的掩码策略，并在真实数据集上进行线性评估、跨数据集微调和分布外泛化等实验验证。

Result: 理论上证明了掩码技术隐式创建语义相似的正样本对，重建损失将其在特征空间中拉近；发现维度坍缩问题并通过U-MPT损失有效解决，在多种下游任务上带来显著性能提升；提出的新掩码策略增强了MPT的下游性能。

Conclusion: 该工作首次从理论上建立了MPT与对比学习之间的联系，揭示了掩码在表示学习中的作用，并提供了U-MPT损失和新掩码策略等实用改进方案，为MPT的设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Recently, Masked Pretraining (MPT) based on reconstruction pretraining tasks has risen to a promising self-supervised learning paradigm across various domains and achieves remarkable performance in multiple downstream tasks. However, the theoretical understanding of the working mechanism behind MPT is still limited. In this paper, we introduce a new theoretical framework to analyze MPT and understand the crucial role of masking in extracting meaningful representations. We establish theoretical connections between MPT and another popular self-supervised paradigm: contrastive learning. We prove that the masking technique implicitly creates positive pairs that are semantically similar and the reconstruction loss pulls them together in the feature space. Besides, as a result of the implicit alignment, we point out the dimensional collapse issue of MPT and propose a Uniformity-enhanced MPT (U-MPT) loss that can effectively address this issue and bring significant improvements in downstream tasks including linear evaluation, cross-dataset fine-tuning and out-of-distribution generalization on real-world data sets. Furthermore, we establish downstream guarantees of U-MPT and theoretically analyze the influence of masking strategies. Based on the theoretical analysis, we propose a new masking strategy which enhances the downstream performance of MPT and explains current improvements of masking strategies with our theoretical perspective.

</details>


### [264] [Local and Global Stability in Performative Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06467)
*Debmalya Mandal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究表演性强化学习中混合策略的稳定性。作者区分了局部混合稳定性和全局混合稳定性，证明了加权逐状态Hedge动态可以以O(1/√T)的速率驱动局部稳定性缺口趋零，且不需要任何环境映射的连续性假设；对于全局稳定性，引入比Lipschitz敏感性更弱的有界转移范围假设，并给出了匹配的下界。最后将两种概念扩展到n人表演性马尔可夫博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表演性强化学习的收敛保证依赖于环境映射π→(Pπ, rπ)的Lipschitz敏感性假设，这些假设难以验证并且在多智能体最佳响应动态等场景中不成立。作者希望探索是否可以通过混合策略来消除对这些敏感假设的需求，从而建立更普遍的稳定性保证。

Method: 提出两种概念：(1) 局部混合稳定性——占用权重一阶松弛，等价于平稳性；(2) 全局混合稳定性——能抵御任意偏离策略。使用加权逐状态Hedge算法，在精确反馈和轨迹反馈下进行分析；引入有界转移范围假设（严格弱于Lipschitz敏感性）来保证全局稳定性，并对n人表演性马尔可夫博弈进行扩展分析。

Result: 加权逐状态Hedge动态可在O(1/√T)速率下将局部稳定性缺口降为零，即使环境映射不连续也能成立；构造实例显示局部稳定与全局稳定确实不同。全局稳定性下，未加权Hedge收敛到O(γεP/(1-γ)³)的地板，并证明了Ω(γεP/(1-γ))的匹配下界。在n人表演性马尔可夫博弈中，局部稳定性无需任何假设，全局稳定性适用于表演性马尔可夫势博弈。

Conclusion: 作者表明混合策略为表演性强化学习中的稳定性提供了新的视角：局部混合稳定性可以在任意不连续环境映射下通过Hedge算法驱动到零，而全局混合稳定性需要比Lipschitz敏感性弱得多的假设。这从本质上不同于表演性预测，因为随机化并非总能消除稳定性分析中对假设的需求。

Abstract: In performative reinforcement learning the deployed policy shapes the environment that generates the learner's future data, and the natural solution concept is a performatively stable policy that is optimal in the environment it induces. Existing convergence guarantees rely on Lipschitz sensitivity assumptions on the environment map $π\mapsto (P_π, r_π)$, which are hard to verify and fail in settings such as multi-agent best-response dynamics. We instead study stability for mixtures of policies, and show that the resulting picture is fundamentally different from performative prediction, where randomization removes the need for any sensitivity assumption. We distinguish local mixed stability, an occupancy-weighted first-order relaxation that we show is equivalent to stationarity, from global mixed stability, which certifies against arbitrary deviating policies. Our first result is that a weighted per-state Hedge dynamic drives the local stability gap to zero at an $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ rate for an arbitrary, possibly discontinuous, environment map, both with exact and with trajectory feedback. The two notions genuinely differ: we exhibit an instance where local stability is achieved exactly but every mixture has global stability gap bounded away from zero. For global stability we introduce a bounded transition range assumption, strictly weaker than Lipschitz sensitivity, under which unweighted per-state Hedge converges up to a floor of $O(γε_P/(1-γ)^3)$, and we prove a matching-in-$ε_P$ lower bound of $Ω(γε_P/(1-γ))$ under trajectory feedback, so this floor is unavoidable. Finally, we extend both notions to $n$-player performative Markov games, obtaining local stability with no assumption on the joint environment map or game structure, and global stability for performative Markov potential games.

</details>


### [265] [Sector-Mean: Deterministic Initialization of K-Means Centroids via Angular Sector Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06468)
*Abhiyan Dhakal,Pranish Kafle,Rajani Chulyadyo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Sector-Mean的确定性K-Means初始化策略，其时间复杂度为O(N)。该方法围绕全局质心将二维数据空间划分为角度扇区，并使用扇区均值初始化质心。实验表明，在保持与K-Means++和Max-Min相当的聚类质量的同时，初始化时间分别减少了74.9%和59.8%，平均迭代次数分别减少了约5%和16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-Means是最广泛使用的聚类算法之一，但其对初始质心选择的敏感性是影响收敛速度和聚类精度的主要瓶颈。现有初始化方法（如随机、K-Means++、Max-Min）在计算效率或聚类质量方面存在不足，因此需要一种更高效且确定性的初始化策略。

Method: 提出Sector-Mean初始化算法：围绕数据集的全局质心，将二维数据空间划分为若干角度扇区，然后计算每个扇区内数据的均值作为初始质心。该方法具有O(N)的时间复杂度，且是完全确定性的。作者在SIPU和Birch等二维基准数据集以及多个真实世界数据集上进行评估，与随机、K-Means++和Max-Min初始化进行对比，所有方法均在相同的Lloyd迭代条件下运行。统计分析采用Friedman检验（p<0.05）和Nemenyi事后比较。

Result: 实验结果显示：Sector-Mean在聚类质量上与K-Means++和Max-Min相当（经Friedman检验和Nemenyi事后验证）；初始化时间较K-Means++减少74.9%，较Max-Min减少59.8%；平均迭代次数最低，较K-Means++减少约5%，较Max-Min减少约16%。

Conclusion: Sector-Mean初始化是一种确定性且计算高效的K-Means初始化策略，在保持聚类质量的前提下显著提升了初始化效率和收敛速度，为K-Means初始化问题提供了一种实用的替代方案。

Abstract: K-Means is one of the most widely used clustering algorithms, but its susceptibility to initial centroid selection remains a primary bottleneck for its convergence speed and clustering accuracy. This paper proposes Sector-Mean Initialization, a deterministic initialization strategy with O(N) time complexity that partitions the two-dimensional data space into angular sectors around the global centroid and initializes centroids using sector-wise means. We evaluate the method on established two-dimensional benchmarks (SIPU, Birch) and multiple real-world datasets, comparing against random, K-Means++, and Max-Min initialization under identical Lloyd iterations. The statistical analysis of Friedman's test (p<0.05) and Nemenyi post-hoc comparison indicates that, while delivering equivalent clustering quality as K-Means++ and Max-Min, Sector-Mean offers significant computational efficiency. Experimental results show that Sector-Mean reduces the initialization time by 74.9% and 59.8% in comparison to K-Means++ and max-min, respectively. And, it yields the lowest average number of iterations, achieving approximately 5% fewer iterations than K-Means++ and 16% fewer than max-min. These results highlight that Sector-Mean initialization offers a deterministic and computationally efficient initialization strategy while preserving cluster quality.

</details>


### [266] [One Step, One Lead: Mitigating Higher-Order Interference in Multi-Domain Reinforcement Learning via Cross-Step Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06469)
*Zihan Lin,Xiaohan Wang,Jie Cao,Jiajun Chai,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Ran He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OSOL方法，通过跨步输出空间的二阶交互检测，解决多领域RL训练中的顺序干扰问题，在Qwen3-30B-A3B上提升5.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多领域强化学习训练会削弱单个领域性能并导致优化不稳定。现有诊断方法仅从单步视角（如一阶梯度对齐或曲率代理）分析干扰，但会遗漏一种关键的顺序干扰：同点领域梯度在实现上近似正交时，连续的实际更新可能在输出空间相互反转。

Method: 提出OSOL框架：每个迭代指定一个焦点领域，利用前一个检查点的token级日志概率足迹进行二阶交互分析，无需显式重建同步曲率。基于漂移排序和自适应缩放的校正项，将其融入标准GRPO更新中。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B上，OSOL达到领域宏平均0.4822，相比最强基线提升5.7%。跨步回溯与后续任务损伤的关联强于同点梯度诊断；前序足迹比Hessian代理更准确预测反弹风险。

Conclusion: 跨步输出回溯是多领域RL中一种被忽视的干扰形式，OSOL通过利用相邻检查点的足迹信息可有效抑制该干扰，无需显式高阶微分，且效果优于现有单步诊断方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) across multiple domains can broaden the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs), yet joint training often degrades individual-domain performance and can destabilize optimization. Existing work typically diagnoses such interference from a single-step view using first-order gradient alignment or curvature-based proxies. We show that this view can miss a critical form of sequential interference: same-point domain gradients may remain nearly orthogonal even when consecutive realized updates partially reverse one another in output space. We further show that consecutive token log-probability footprints recover this interaction directly from adjacent checkpoints as a local second-order interaction in output space, without explicitly reconstructing same-step curvature. Building on this insight, we propose OSOL, which designates a focus domain at each iteration, uses the preceding checkpoint footprint to rank token-level rebound risk, and applies a drift-ranked, adaptively scaled correction within the standard GRPO update. Our analysis shows that this correction suppresses the targeted cross-step output backtracking component. Controlled studies further show that cross-step backtracking is more strongly associated with subsequent task damage than same-point gradient diagnostics, while the preceding footprint ranks future rebound risk more accurately than Hessian-based proxies. On Qwen3-30B-A3B, OSOL reaches a domain-macro average of 0.4822, improving by 5.7% over the strongest compared baseline, without explicit higher-order differentiation.

</details>


### [267] [Steering Under Compression: Dose-Response, Capability Cost, and Failure Asymmetry in Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06473)
*Saurav Bhandari,Benjamin Wade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了激活导向（activation steering）与权重量化（INT8/NF4）之间的交互作用。结果显示情感导向在量化后保持完好，但推理长度导向表现出不对称的剂量-反应关系（延长是渐进式的但会崩溃，缩短则是阶跃函数式的不连续失败）。文章还揭示了朴素iso-effect框架在底层受限目标上的方法论缺陷，并提出审查式构造来修复。尽管压缩可能主导导向干预（如Mistral-NF4基线能力大幅下降），导向向量与FP16版本仍高度共线（余弦相似度0.945-0.998），表明行为方向在量化中存续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理阶段的激活导向和训练后量化在实践中日益结合使用，但二者之间的交互作用尚未被系统刻画。理解这种交互对于在压缩模型上可靠部署行为控制技术至关重要。

Method: 在四种7-9B开源模型上，对两种量化方案（INT8和NF4）下的激活导向进行系统研究，覆盖两个行为目标（评判情感和推理长度）。采用iso-effect框架在匹配行为效果下比较能力成本，并引入审查式构造修复底层受限目标上的阶梯锚定问题，同时用统一的v2.3.1重新评分修正GSM8K解析伪影。

Result: 情感导向在量化下保持完整（INT8对比-0.010，等效；NF4为-0.017，不确定）。推理长度表现出不对称剂量-反应：延长为渐进式但终止于失控和崩溃，缩短为阶跃函数（仅缩短12-30%便不连续失败）。Mistral-NF4存在显著基线能力下降（0.545到0.365）。然而导向向量与FP16高度共线（余弦相似度0.945-0.998）。

Conclusion: 量化对激活导向的影响因行为目标而异：情感导向几乎无损存续，推理长度导向则受显著干扰。压缩带来的能力成本可能主导导向干预效果，但行为方向本身在量化中得以保留。研究揭示了方法论陷阱并提供了修复方案，所有代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Inference-time activation steering enables behavioral control of large language models without parameter modification, while post-training quantization reduces memory and compute costs for deployment. Despite their growing convergence in practice, the interaction between these two techniques remains uncharacterized. We systematically study activation steering under weight-only quantization (INT8 and NF4) across four open-weight 7-9B models and two behavioral targets: judged sentiment and judge-free reasoning length. Using an iso-effect framework that compares capability costs at matched behavioral effect, we find that sentiment steering survives quantization intact. After correcting a GSM8K parser artifact with a uniform v2.3.1 rescore, the pooled INT8 contrast is -0.010 (90% CI [-0.026, +0.007]), descriptively Equivalent under the preregistered three-label rule, while NF4 remains Inconclusive at -0.017 ([-0.067, +0.033]). In contrast, reasoning length exhibits a surprising asymmetric dose-response: lengthening is graded but terminates in cap-runaway and collapse, while shortening is a step function with only 12-30% shortening (model-dependent) before discontinuous failure. We expose a methodological pitfall: the naive iso-effect ladder anchors on the collapse floor for floor-bounded targets, and we introduce a censored construction that restores interpretable crossings. We also quantify a substantial baseline capability shift for Mistral-NF4 (0.545 to 0.365 GSM8K at alpha=0), demonstrating that compression can dominate the steering intervention.
